Conclusion: 先验引导的残差流匹配框架有效解决了纯噪声生成过程中的结构一致性问题和不可靠交互问题，在不牺牲多假设生成能力的前提下显著提升了3D多人运动预测精度。

Abstract: 3D multi-person motion prediction requires modeling both individual kinematics and inter-person interactions. While Flow Matching is effective for multi-hypothesis generation to improve prediction accuracy, directly predicting skeletal sequences from pure noise often compromises structural consistency and introduces unreliable cross-agent interactions during early noise-dominated integration steps. To address this, we propose a Prior-Guided Residual Flow Matching framework. First, a Deterministic Coarse Prior (DCP) establishes a kinematic anchor, formulating the generative process as a conditional flow over motion residuals to simplify the generative objective and preserve structural stability. Second, a Dynamic Cross-Interaction (DCI) mechanism temporally synchronizes inter-agent message-passing with the integration progress, ensuring the extraction of reliable social contexts and improving multi-person motion fidelity. Finally, a decoupled joint-motion architecture with bidirectional fusion effectively preserves fine-grained kinematic coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art prediction accuracy across multiple datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/Wei-Wei-a/Residual-Flow-Matching-with-Dynamic-Cross-Interaction-for-3D-Multi-Person-Motion-Prediction.

</details>


### [69] [FreqAdapt: Frequency-Adaptive Processing for RAW Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03385)
*Hanxi Li,Huiling Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqAdapt，一个轻量级的频域自适应RAW图像增强模块，将传统ISP操作映射到傅里叶频域，基于ISP操作的物理特性进行域分离，并通过自适应频域编码器联合分析幅度谱、相位谱和RAW图像特征来预测ISP参数，实现自适应特征增强，在多种光照和天气条件下的检测任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测方法大多使用sRGB输入，但sRGB是通过面向可视化设计的ISP从RAW传感器数据压缩而来，丢失了关键信息。相比之下，RAW图像具有更好的噪声特性和更丰富的信息表征，特别是在恶劣天气或低光环境等挑战性条件下，对目标检测至关重要。

Method: FreqAdapt采用频域处理方法：1) 将ISP操作创新性地映射到傅里叶频域，基于ISP操作的物理性质进行域分离，使每个操作在最适合的域中执行；2) 通过自适应频域编码器联合分析幅度谱、相位谱和RAW图像特征，为ISP参数预测提供全局上下文；3) 采用可学习融合机制实现自适应特征增强。

Result: 在多个包含不同光照和天气条件的数据集上进行了广泛实验，FreqAdapt达到了最先进的性能，同时保持了轻量级效率和良好的物理可解释性，并且可以无缝集成到现有目标检测框架中。

Conclusion: FreqAdapt为RAW域的视觉感知任务提供了一种新颖的解决方案，验证了频域处理在RAW图像增强和下游目标检测任务中的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Existing object detection methods predominantly utilize sRGB inputs, which are compressed from RAW sensor data using Image Signal Processors (ISP) originally designed for visualization purposes. Compared to RGB images, RAW images possess favorable noise characteristics and richer information representation, which are crucial for object detection, particularly under challenging conditions such as adverse weather or low-light environments. In this paper, we propose FreqAdapt, a lightweight module for adaptive RAW data enhancement in the frequency domain. Unlike traditional spatial domain processing methods, FreqAdapt innovatively maps ISP operations to the Fourier frequency domain and performs domain separation based on the physical properties of ISP operations, ensuring each operation is performed in its most suitable domain. Meanwhile, through an adaptive frequency domain encoder that jointly analyzes amplitude spectrum, phase spectrum, and RAW image features, we provide global context for ISP parameter prediction and employ a learnable fusion mechanism to achieve adaptive feature enhancement. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets with diverse lighting and weather conditions demonstrate that FreqAdapt achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining lightweight efficiency and good physical interpretability. Furthermore, our module can be seamlessly incorporated into existing object detection frameworks, providing a novel solution for visual perception tasks in the RAW domain.

</details>


### [70] [SRAP: SVD-Refined Adversarial Perturbations for Imperceptible Face-Swap Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03395)
*Sungwon Cho,Kwanghyun Ko,Myungjoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRAP方法，通过逐通道截断SVD精化和身份重要性掩码，在每次优化步骤中同时抑制PGD对抗扰动中的高频成分和身份不敏感区域，在保持身份破坏能力的同时大幅提升保护图像的视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造技术对面部隐私和身份安全构成日益严重的威胁，需要主动防御来在面部图像被滥用之前进行保护。虽然PGD生成的对抗扰动能够破坏换脸模型的身份表征，但其视觉质量因两个特性而退化：扰动广泛分布在图像各处（包括对身份不敏感的区域），且包含视觉上突出的高频分量。因此需要解决这些问题以改善保护图像的质量。

Method: 通过身份敏感性估计和SVD分析PGD扰动的空间与频谱低效性后，提出SRAP（SVD Refinement with identity-importance mAsk for Perturbation）。该方法在每个优化步骤中结合两种技术：(1) 逐通道截断SVD精化，用于抑制高秩、高频残差；(2) 身份重要性掩码，将扰动限制在对身份表征有强烈影响的位置。

Result: 在CelebA-HQ和VGGFace2-HQ数据集上的实验表明，SRAP在保持有竞争力的身份破坏性能的同时，在所有报告指标上显著提升了保护图像的保真度，实现了换脸防御与视觉不可感知性之间的良好权衡。

Conclusion: SRAP通过同时解决PGD扰动在空间分布和频谱成分两个方面的低效性，实现了更强的防御有效性与视觉质量的平衡，为面部隐私保护提供了一种更实用的主动防御方案。

Abstract: Deepfake technologies pose increasing threats to facial privacy and identity security, motivating proactive defenses that protect facial images before misuse. Although adversarial perturbations generated by projected gradient descent (PGD) can disrupt the identity representations used by face-swapping models, their visual quality is degraded by two characteristics: perturbations are distributed broadly over the image, including identity-insensitive regions, and they contain visually salient high-frequency components. We analyze these spatial and spectral inefficiencies through identity-sensitivity estimation and the singular-value decomposition (SVD) of PGD perturbations. Our analysis shows that later singular components contain a disproportionate amount of high-frequency energy, while the leading components preserve most of the perturbation energy and defense utility. Based on these observations, we propose SRAP, which combines per-channel truncated SVD refinement with an identity-importance mask at every optimization step. The SVD refinement suppresses high-rank, high-frequency residuals, while the mask restricts perturbations to locations that strongly influence identity representations. Experiments on CelebA-HQ and VGGFace2-HQ demonstrate that SRAP substantially improves protected-image fidelity across all reported metrics while maintaining competitive identity-disruption performance, yielding a favorable trade-off between face-swap defense and visual imperceptibility.

</details>


### [71] [Distilled Roads: Generalisable Road Network Extraction Across Sensors, Resolutions, and Region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03407)
*Sanayya,Rakshith Sathish,Ashwathi Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过将全球道路提取重新定义为持续自适应问题，结合跨分辨率知识蒸馏、分辨率递减课程学习、多传感器训练和拓扑感知监督，提出单一模型即可在0.3-1.0米分辨率的多卫星平台、多大陆图像上取得优异泛化性能，在公共基准上超越SOTA最高22个F1点和15个APLS点，且推理速度提高3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道路分割模型通常在狭窄的分辨率-区域组合下训练，面对农村环境、不同道路材料或新型卫星平台等未见领域时泛化能力差，产生断裂或不连通的预测结果。将模型适应到新领域通常需要昂贵的重训练或微调，并存在灾难性遗忘的风险。因此需要高效且稳健的跨域泛化方案。

Method: 将全球道路提取重构为持续自适应问题而非架构问题。具体方法包括：(1) 跨分辨率知识蒸馏，配合分辨率递减的课程学习（resolution-decreasing curriculum）；(2) 多传感器（多卫星平台）联合训练；(3) 拓扑感知监督损失。最终训练出单一模型，无需微调即可泛化到多种分辨率与平台。

Result: 在City-Scale和Global-Scale等公共基准上，该模型较现有最优方法在F1分数上最高提升22个点，在APLS指标上最高提升15个点；同时保持最高效率，推理速度提升3倍。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，通过针对性的训练策略（如数据课程学习、知识蒸馏和拓扑感知损失）而非日益复杂的网络架构，即可显著提升道路分割模型在不同亚米级卫星图像上的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为跨域遥感分割提供了新思路。

Abstract: Road network segmentation from satellite imagery remains challenging due to large geographic variation in road appearance, occlusions, and domain shifts introduced by differing resolutions and sensors. Existing models, typically trained under narrow resolution--region combinations, generalise poorly to unseen environments such as rural settings, regions with distinct road materials, or imagery from new satellite platforms, often producing broken or disconnected predictions. Adapting these models to new domains usually requires retraining or fine-tuning, which is costly and risks catastrophic forgetting.
  In this work, we reframe global road extraction as a continual adaptation problem rather than an architectural one. Our framework combines cross-resolution knowledge distillation across a resolution-decreasing curriculum, multi-sensor training, and topology-aware supervision, yielding a single model that generalises across $0.3-1.0$ m imagery from multiple satellite platforms across continents. On publicly available benchmarks, including City-Scale and Global-Scale, our model outperforms state-of-the-art results by up to $22$ F1 points and $15$ APLS points, while remaining the most efficient, with $3\times$ faster inference. Our results suggest that improved robustness across diverse sub-meter satellite imagery can be achieved through targeted training strategies, such as data curricula, distillation, and topology-aware losses, rather than increasingly complex architectures.

</details>


### [72] [Earth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03410)
*Adam J. Stewart,Heng Fang,Isaac A. Corley,Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了地球嵌入（Earth embeddings）技术，即将地球观测基础模型的中间特征输出打包为可复用的矢量产品，使用户无需处理原始卫星影像即可进行分析。文章比较了各类嵌入产品的覆盖范围、分辨率、维度、存储成本、许可与可复现性，并评估了其在土地覆盖、作物制图、生态与灾害建模、社会经济预测及语义搜索等任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测领域正从"用户自行运行基础模型"转向"嵌入产品"模式，即将模型特征输出直接作为可复用的数据产品提供。这一转变亟需系统梳理不同类型的嵌入产品、其适用场景与局限，以帮助用户避免重复训练或运行大规模模型处理原始卫星影像的高昂成本。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法：首先对地球嵌入进行分类（从隐式位置编码器到显式图像块与像素产品），并从覆盖范围、分辨率、维度、存储成本、许可及可复现性等维度进行对比；随后回顾了嵌入在多个下游任务中的应用证据，辅以两个实际案例研究展示相似性搜索与土地覆盖制图的工作流程。

Result: 研究发现地球嵌入在多数任务中可优于传统特征，但嵌入的池化、融合或空间迁移操作可能限制其性能。提供了选择、评估、存储、压缩和发布嵌入产品的实践指导，并指出了海洋与大气覆盖、不确定性量化及基准测试方面的开放问题。

Conclusion: 地球嵌入作为连接基础模型与终端应用的关键数据产品形式，具有显著的应用潜力，但需在覆盖范围、不确定性处理和标准化基准方面进一步完善，以推动该领域的成熟与广泛采用。

Abstract: Earth observation is moving from foundation models that users must run themselves toward embedding products that package model feature outputs as reusable data without needing to download and process the imagery used to generate them. Earth embeddings are vectors that summarize locations, image patches, or pixels, letting users analyze compact features instead of repeatedly training or running large models on raw satellite imagery. This chapter explains the main types of Earth embeddings, from implicit location encoders to explicit patch and pixel products, and compares their coverage, resolution, dimensionality, storage cost, licenses, and reproducibility. We review their use in land cover and crop mapping, ecological and hazard modeling, socioeconomic prediction, and semantic search, with evidence on when embeddings improve on conventional features and when pooling, fusion, or spatial transfer limit performance. Two case studies show practical workflows for similarity search and land cover mapping. We close with guidance for choosing, evaluating, storing, compressing, and publishing embeddings, and with open problems in oceanic and atmospheric coverage, uncertainty, and benchmarking.

</details>


### [73] [HyperbolicDiffusion: Sharp & Scalable Tiled Generation on the Hyperbolic Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03422)
*Hugo Caselles-Dupré*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperbolicDiffusion，一种无需训练的方法，直接在双曲平面H2上生成有限的视觉场。通过Hyperbolic Blooming Cover将窗口放置简化为紧凑动态规划，并使用永久表面ID作为共享潜画布，让标准扩散模型在局部窗口上降噪后融合回H2。针对曲率导致的窗口接缝处残差与模糊，采用几何推导的第二阶段对异常区域重新加噪并修复，最终生成清晰、可重投影且跨视角一致的图像，是Escher《圆极限》系列的生成式对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面拼接扩散方法对重叠窗口去噪适用于矩形画布，但双曲平面没有此类画布，且其面积随半径呈指数增长，无法直接应用现有扩散生成方法。

Method: 提出Training-free的HyperbolicDiffusion框架：(1) Hyperbolic Blooming Cover将窗口放置简化为可在数秒内运行的紧凑动态规划，并提供强理论保证；(2) 永久表面ID构成共享潜画布，标准扩散模型对局部窗口去噪后预测融合回H2；(3) 针对曲率导致的多窗口接缝处残差差异和模糊，利用几何推导的第二阶段重新加噪并修复该区域。

Result: 生成的视觉场清晰、可重投影、跨视角一致，成功实现了提示驱动的生成式Escher《圆极限》系列作品效果。

Conclusion: HyperbolicDiffusion证明了无需训练即可将标准扩散模型扩展到非欧几何（双曲平面）上，通过几何感知的覆盖策略和两阶段修复机制解决了曲率带来的挑战。

Abstract: Planar tiled diffusion denoises overlapping windows of one rectangular canvas. The hyperbolic plane has no such canvas, and its area grows exponentially with radius. We introduce HyperbolicDiffusion, a training-free method for generating finite visual fields directly on the hyperbolic plane H2. Our Hyperbolic Blooming Cover reduces window placement to a compact dynamic program that runs in seconds while providing strong theoretical guarantees. Permanent surface IDs form a shared latent canvas: a standard diffusion model denoises local windows, whose predictions are fused back onto H2. Because curvature causes residual disagreement and blur at multi-window junctions, a geometry-derived second stage re-noises and repairs precisely those regions. The resulting fields are sharp, reprojectable, and consistent across viewpoints, providing a prompt-driven generative counterpart to Escher's Circle Limit series.

</details>


### [74] [SGFormer: Structure-Guided Transformer for Robust Local Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03423)
*Runyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SGFormer（Structure-Guided Transformer），一种结构感知的局部特征匹配网络，通过Triple-Structure-Attention（TSA）模块引导Transformer关注重叠区域内的显著结构，从而缓解注意力发散问题并提升匹配精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检测器无关匹配方法（如LoFTR）利用无约束注意力机制的全局建模能力，但在大视角变化场景下，全局特征可能导致模型对显著结构的注意力被削弱，产生"注意力发散"现象，即部分高置信度匹配分布在有效匹配区域（重叠区域）之外，限制了匹配的可靠性。

Method: SGFormer采用半稠密粗到细（coarse-to-fine）流水线，在骨干网络中引入Triple-Structure-Attention（TSA）模块。该模块利用浅层局部特征增强显著结构周围的表征，引导后续Transformer阶段在全局范围内加强对显著结构区域的关注。

Result: 大量实验表明，SGFormer能够显著缓解注意力发散现象，并提升匹配精度。

Conclusion: 通过结构引导的注意力机制，SGFormer增强了对视觉一致区域的关注，同时减轻了非重叠区域的干扰，有效解决了在大视角变化环境下局部特征匹配的注意力发散问题。

Abstract: Local feature matching is a fundamental component of photogrammetry, enabling accurate image correspondence critical for tasks such as 3D reconstruction, stereo mapping, and visual localization. While recent detector-free matching methods, like LoFTR, have advanced the field, the global features obtained by leveraging the global-range modeling capacity of the unconstrained attention mechanism compromise the model's attention to the salient structures in certain scenarios. This limitation leads to a phenomenon we define as attention divergence, wherein a portion of high-confidence matches are distributed outside the valid matching region (overlapping region), especially in scenes with large viewpoint variations. This occurs because similar features in irrelevant regions may receive equal weighting and consideration within the standard Transformer, limiting matching reliability in challenging photogrammetric environments. To address this issue in feature matching, we propose SGFormer (Structure-Guided Transformer), a novel structure-aware matching network that adaptively updates attention on features near salient structure in overlapping regions. SGFormer employs a semi-dense coarse-to-fine pipeline and incorporates the proposed Triple-Structure-Attention (TSA) module into the backbone net for extracting distinctive features. The TSA module utilizes shallow local features from early network layers to enhance the representation around salient structure, guiding subsequent transformer stages to intensify the model's focus on regions with salient structure across the global scope. SGFormer, thereby reinforcing attention to visually consistent areas while mitigating the influence of non-overlapping regions. Extensive experiments show that SGFormer significantly mitigates attention divergence and improves matching accuracy.

</details>


### [75] [OliveGemma: A 3 Billion Visual Language Model for Recognising the Mediterranean & European Diet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03428)
*Dimitrios I. Zaridis,Traianos Tsiokris,Vasileios C. Pezoulas,Daphni Plati,Eugenia Mylona,Eleni Georga,Nikos Tsiknakis,Antonis Sakellarios,Dimitrios I. Fotiadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出OliveGemma，一个基于PaliGemma-2-3B架构、通过LoRA微调的地中海与欧洲美食视觉语言模型。该模型在17,340张图片和102,642个指令式问答对的统一语料上训练，实现了92.96%±0.91%的Top-1准确率，显著超过CNN基线和多个零样本前沿大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的饮食评估是自我报告食物日记的可扩展替代方案，但细粒度食物识别面临类内变异性高和视觉相似菜肴难以区分等挑战，现有方法在专业领域识别精度不足。

Method: 基于开源PaliGemma-2-3B架构，使用LoRA参数高效微调技术，在来自三个欧洲研究项目数据集（MedGR、ODIN、VIPPSTAR）统一整合的17,340张图像上训练，构建216个组合菜肴类别词汇表，配以102,642个指令风格问答对（涵盖菜肴识别、可能和可见成分、类别边界判别、视觉证据和整体视觉食物理解）。采用3折交叉验证评估。

Result: Top-1准确率达92.96%±0.91%，比最强CNN基线（DenseNet-121）高7.31%；比使用精确指令和受限类别的零样本前沿模型Gemini Flash 3/3.5、GPT-5.4 Mini、Claude Haiku 4.6分别高出8%、46%和64%。Top-3和Top-5准确率排名第二（仅次于DenseNet-121），可能成分的Exact-Set准确率达90.79%±1.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对小型视觉语言模型进行参数高效微调（PEFT）可以在专门的食物识别任务上超越显著更大的专有模型，验证了小模型专业化适配路径的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Image based dietary assessment offers a scalable alternative to self reported food diaries, yet fine-grained food recognition remains challenging due to high intra-class variability and visually similar dishes. This study presents OliveGemma, a vision language model for recognising and reasoning about Mediterranean and European cuisine. Built on the open-weight PaliGemma-2-3B architecture, OliveGemma is fine-tuned with LoRA on a unified corpus of 17,340 images from three European research project datasets (MedGR, ODIN, and VIPPSTAR), reconciled into a vocabulary of 216 composed dish categories and paired with 102,642 instruction style question-answer items covering dish recognition, likely and visible ingredients, class boundary discrimination, visual evidence and overall visual food understanding. Under a 3-fold cross-validation scheme, OliveGemma achieves a top-1 accuracy of 92.96% +/- 0.91%, exceeding the strongest CNN baseline (DenseNet-121) by 7.31% and outperforming zero-shot frontier models with exact instructions and bounded classes including Gemini Flash 3 and 3.5, GPT-5.4 Mini, and Claude Haiku 4.6 by 8%, 46%, and 64% respectively. Furthermore, OliveGemma demonstrates competitive performance on Top-3 and Top-5 accuracy, being second best across CNNs and frontier models, surpassed only by DenseNet-121. In addition, OliveGemma achieves 90.79% +/- 1.3% Exact-Set on the likely ingredients of the food categories. These results demonstrate that PEFT adaptation of a small VLM can surpass substantially larger proprietary models on specialised food recognition. The model is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/JamesZar/OliveGemma-3B and the experiments and results can be found at https://github.com/tsiokris/OliveGemma.

</details>


### [76] [SLAMFormer-$\infty$: Infinite SLAM Transformer for Unbounded Frontend and Backend Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03429)
*Zhijian Fang,Weicheng Zheng,Yijun Yuan,Weibang Wang,Zhuoguang Chen,Chang Sun,Junhao Huang,Kenan Li,Minghui Qin,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SLAMFormer-∞，首个无需显式距离约束即可支持长程前端与后端处理的几何Transformer。通过记忆条件定义灵活的坐标系与尺度，前端保持高效局部计算，后端实现全局一致的轨迹与场景几何联合优化，在大型数据集上取得优异性能，并可泛化到超过17km的超长轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的SLAM方法通常依赖第一帧锚定（first-frame-anchored）的坐标公式，这隐含引入了显式的距离上界，限制了模型处理长轨迹和大型场景的能力。作者旨在打破这一距离限制，使几何Transformer能够同时支持长程前端和后端处理，而不受显式距离约束。

Method: SLAMFormer-∞采用记忆条件（memory conditions）替代第一帧锚定公式，为输入帧定义灵活的坐标系与尺度，从而实现更具表达力的结构条件化。在前端，模型保持高效的局部计算；在后端，模型以全局一致的方式联合优化长程轨迹与场景几何。

Result: 实验表明SLAMFormer-∞在大型数据集的轨迹估计与场景重建任务上达到优越或极具竞争力的性能。尤为突出的是，模型能够成功泛化到超过17km的极端长轨迹序列，验证了其无显式距离边界设计在极长序列上的有效性。

Conclusion: SLAMFormer-∞通过记忆条件替代第一帧锚定，成功解除了几何Transformer的距离上界限制，使得同一模型能够同时处理高效局部前端计算和全局一致的长程后端优化，为大规模SLAM任务提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce the Infinite SLAM Transformer (SLAMFormer-$\infty$), the first geometric transformer capable of supporting both long-range frontend and backend processing without an explicit distance bound. Instead of relying on a first-frame-anchored formulation, SLAMFormer-$\infty$ employs memory conditions to define flexible coordinate systems and scales for input frames, enabling more expressive structural conditioning. Built upon this formulation, the frontend preserves efficient local computation, while the backend jointly optimizes long-range trajectories and scene geometry in a globally consistent manner. Experimental results demonstrate that SLAMFormer-$\infty$ achieves superior or highly competitive performance in both trajectory estimation and scene reconstruction across large-scale datasets. Notably, SLAMFormer-$\infty$ generalizes to extremely long trajectories, successfully operating on sequences exceeding $17\mathrm{km}$.

</details>


### [77] [Dual-domain U-Nets with embedded back projection operators for motion-resolved 4D CBCT reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03430)
*Ivo Herzig,Pascal Paysan,Daniel Barco,Marc André Stadelmann,Frank-Peter Schilling,Igor Peterlik,Michal Walczak,Lijin Aryananda,Woo Sang Ahn,Rudolf Marcel Füchslin,Lukas Lichtensteiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于CNN的深度学习4D CBCT重建方法，从常规自由呼吸扫描中直接预测静态体积及10个位移向量场（DVFs）构成的完整呼吸运动模型，无需呼吸信号或投影分箱。该方法在模拟数据和临床数据上均达到与SART-TV相当的图像质量，同时提供4D运动建模能力，且专家评估显示其在肿瘤和食管可见性上优于参考方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D CBCT对胸部癌症的图像引导放疗至关重要，但传统方法存在扫描时间长导致的高剂量和运动/稀疏采样伪影问题。现有方法需要呼吸信号或投影分箱，限制其临床应用。因此需要一种无需外部呼吸信号、能从常规自由呼吸扫描中直接重建运动分辨的4D CBCT的方法。

Method: 提出一种端到端CNN架构，扩展U-Net结构：编码器在滤波投影堆栈（域）上操作，解码器在体积域中操作，采用非可训练的反投影函数替代传统skip connections，实现多分辨率下的跨域特征传递。网络以自由呼吸3D CBCT投影为输入，输出最大吸气态静态体积加10个覆盖呼吸周期的DVFs。模型在模拟CBCT扫描上训练，并在11个模拟患者和13个临床自由呼吸扫描上评估。

Result: 模拟数据上图像质量与SART-TV相当（RMSE: -1.19 HU, PSNR: +0.09 dB, SSIM: -0.009），同时具备4D重建能力；临床数据上表现出更锐利的动态结构（如膈肌）和更少的运动条纹伪影。专家评估中，该方法在肿瘤可见性（59% vs 36%无偏好, 5%参考）和食管可见性（47% vs 42%, 11%）上均优于参考SART-TV方法。

Conclusion: 该非患者特异性CNN能从单次自由呼吸扫描中预测静态体积和完整4D呼吸运动模型，无需呼吸替代信号或投影分箱，在减少运动伪影的同时增加运动建模能力，具有临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Four-dimensional cone beam CT (4D CBCT) is important for image-guided radiation therapy of thoracic cancers, but its use is limited by long scan times, causing high patient dose and motion/sparse-sampling artifacts.
  We propose a deep learning method for motion-resolved 4D CBCT reconstruction from conventional free-breathing scans, without a respiratory signal or explicit projection binning. Our CNN takes free-breathing 3D CBCT projections as input and predicts a static volume at maximum inhalation plus ten displacement vector fields (DVFs) spanning a breathing cycle.
  The network extends U-Net: the encoder acts on filtered projection stacks, the decoder acts in the volume domain, and skip connections are replaced with non-trainable back-projection functions at multiple resolutions to transfer features between domains. The model is trained on simulated CBCT scans and evaluated on 11 unseen simulated patients and 13 clinical free-breathing scans. Two additional models (60 s and 6 s scans) were evaluated by clinical experts on three and two scans, comparing single phases of our 4D reconstruction to reference 3D SART-TV images for tumor and esophagus visibility.
  Experts preferred our method for tumor visibility (59% vs. 36% no preference, 5% reference) and esophagus visibility (47% vs. 42%, 11%). On simulated data, image quality matched SART-TV (mean RMSE: -1.19 HU, PSNR: +0.09 dB, SSIM: -0.009) while enabling 4D reconstruction. On clinical scans, our method showed sharper dynamic structures (e.g., diaphragm) and fewer motion streak artifacts than traditional reconstruction.
  This non-patient-specific CNN predicts static volumes and full 4D respiratory motion models from a single free-breathing scan, without a respiratory surrogate or projection binning, reducing motion artifacts while adding motion-modeling capability.

</details>


### [78] [Hi-Token: Hierarchical Coordinate Tokenization for Generative Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03471)
*Xiuyuan Zhu,Ke Lu,Kun Dong,Siwen Jiao,Hao Wu,Zijin Du,Shun Mao,Dongming Zhang,Jian Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hi-Token和Hi-GAR方法改进生成式视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉定位能力。Hi-Token将边界框坐标编码为轴特定、逐位（百/十/个位）的离散token，引入由粗到细的结构化表示；Hi-GAR则设计基于几何的奖励信号用于GRPO强化学习训练。在三个VLM骨干和RefCOCO系列基准上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式VLM通常将边界框坐标视为独立输出符号，忽视了数值顺序和坐标轴语义，这成为视觉定位误差的重要来源。

Method: （1）Hi-Token：为每个坐标引入轴特定且分百位、十位、个位的离散token，增添由粗到细的结构并提高token复用率，且不改变现有VLM架构；（2）Hi-GAR：设计基于几何的奖励函数，在多个尺度上结合框重叠度和坐标精度，用于GRPO训练，仅训练阶段使用。

Result: 在匹配训练条件下的受控对比中，Hi-Token在评估的整个IoU范围上改进了定位精度；Hi-GAR进一步减少低重叠预测。在三个VLM骨干和RefCOCO系列基准上获得一致提升，Hi-R1在大多数指标上超过强专家基线。

Conclusion: 对token频率、数字边界、目标尺度和IoU分布的分析解释了坐标表示与奖励训练的作用机制，表明结构化坐标生成是生成式视觉定位的有效途径。

Abstract: Generative Vision-Language Models (VLMs) commonly treat bounding-box coordinates as independent output symbols, leaving numerical order and axis semantics implicit. We identify this representation as an important source of error in visual grounding. Hi-Token encodes each coordinate with axis-specific tokens for the hundreds, tens, and ones digits, which adds coarse-to-fine structure and increases token reuse while retaining the existing VLM architecture. Hi-GAR complements this representation with a geometry-based reward for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), using box overlap and coordinate accuracy at multiple scales. Controlled comparisons under matched training conditions show that Hi-Token improves localization throughout the evaluated IoU range. Hi-GAR further reduces low-overlap predictions and is used only during training. Experiments on three VLM backbones and the RefCOCO family show consistent gains across models and benchmarks. Hi-R1 achieves higher values than strong specialist baselines on most reported metrics. Analyses of token frequency, digit boundaries, object scale, and IoU distributions explain the effects of coordinate representation and reward training. The results show that structured coordinate generation provides an effective approach to generative visual grounding.

</details>


### [79] [MT-Web2Code: Benchmarking Coding Agents on Multi-Turn Regional Reconstruction and Localized Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03474)
*Qiming Li,Shujie Hu,Haohan Liu,Xiaocheng Feng,Songxiang Liu,Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MT-Web2Code，这是首个面向多轮Web UI代码修复的多模态编码基准，涵盖102个任务和16个垂直领域，通过反向损坏轨迹引擎构建确定性修复轨迹，并设计了双轴评估协议，实验表明13个前沿编码代理在目标区域重建、局部修改和跨轮次错误累积方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM基准主要关注单轮全页面从头生成，忽略了真实前端工程中迭代式的工作流程——开发者需要反复重建缺失区域并在现有代码库中修改局部元素。这一差距导致现有基准无法真实评估模型在实际前端开发场景中的能力。

Method: (1) 构建MT-Web2Code基准，包含102个任务、16个垂直领域，涵盖宏级区域重建和微级局部修改两类任务；(2) 提出可扩展的反向损坏轨迹引擎，通过迭代注入结构和风格缺陷到黄金页面，无需昂贵的逐轮人工标注即可构造确定性修复轨迹；(3) 设计双轴评估协议：区域重建采用5维VLM评分标准，局部修改采用基于像素对齐的确定性评估，同时衡量目标区域保真度和未受影响内容的保持度。

Result: 对13个前沿编码代理的实验发现：(1) 现有代理难以在忠实重建目标区域的同时保持未受影响内容；(2) 缺乏细粒度的视觉-代码对齐能力以完成局部编辑；(3) 在多轮迭代中遭受错误雪球效应，错误会随轮次累积恶化。

Conclusion: MT-Web2Code填补了多轮迭代Web UI代码修复基准的空白，其确定性评估指标能提供细粒度的反馈信号，为未来训练迭代式UI编码代理提供了基础。评估代码和数据将即将发布。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in web UI generation. However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on single-turn full-page generation from scratch, overlooking the iterative workflow of real-world frontend engineering, where developers repeatedly reconstruct missing regions and modify localized elements within existing codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce MT-Web2Code, the first multimodal coding benchmark for multi-turn Macro-Level Regional Reconstruction and Micro-Level Localized Modification, which contains 102 tasks spanning 16 vertical domains. To construct deterministic repair trajectories without costly turn-level human annotation, we develop a scalable Reverse-Corruption Trajectory Engine that iteratively injects structural and stylistic defects into golden pages. We further propose a dual-axis evaluation protocol that measures target-region fidelity and the preservation of unaffected content, where regional reconstruction is assessed by a 5-dimensional VLM-based rubric and localized modification by deterministic pixel-grounded alignment. Experiments on 13 frontier coding agents reveal that current agents struggle to faithfully reconstruct target regions while preserving unaffected content, lack fine-grained visual-code alignment for localized edits, and suffer from error snowballing over multiple turns. Beyond benchmarking, our deterministic evaluation metrics provide fine-grained feedback signals that may facilitate future research on training iterative UI coding agents. Our evaluation code and data will soon be released.

</details>


### [80] [From Multi-Resolution Cells to Gigapixel Whole Slide Images Foundation Model for Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03508)
*Basit Alawode,Moshira Ali Abdalla,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Muhammad Muzammal Naseer,Sajid Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MRPT（多分辨率金字塔Transformer），用于计算病理学中全切片图像（WSI）的多分辨率理解。MRPT通过生物意义上合理的连续跨分辨率注意力（CCRA）机制聚合从细胞到组织和WSI级别的层次化多分辨率信息，并通过跨分辨率嵌入对齐实现语义一致性。该模型在624M patches、2.4M区域和36K WSIs上进行多分辨率自监督预训练，在34个数据集上超越了现有基础模型和MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision Transformer及层级变体在计算病理学中表现优异，但大多在单一分辨率的WSI上预训练，限制了跨任意分辨率的泛化能力。而十亿像素级的WSI天然包含多尺度诊断模式（细胞形态、组织结构、全局上下文），类似病理学家的多尺度阅片方式，因此需要能够层次化聚合多分辨率信息的模型。

Method: MRPT采用层次化金字塔结构，从细胞级别到组织级别再到WSI级别逐层聚合多分辨率信息。核心是连续跨分辨率注意力（CCRA）机制，用于捕获尺度独立的交互；同时通过跨分辨率嵌入对齐来强制多分辨率语义一致性，从而获得鲁棒且可泛化的WSI表示。模型以多分辨率自监督方式在大规模数据上预训练。

Result: 在34个多样化数据集上的大量实验表明，MRPT在癌症亚型分类、组织表型分析和WSI视觉问答（VQA）等任务上超越了近期的基础模型和多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）。

Conclusion: 通过多分辨率金字塔结构和跨分辨率注意力机制，MRPT有效解决了WSI多尺度表示学习问题，证明了多尺度自监督预训练对计算病理学基础模型的泛化能力有显著提升。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) and their hierarchical variants have achieved strong performance in Computational Pathology (CPath). However, most are pre-trained on single-resolution Whole Slide Images (WSIs), limiting their generalization across arbitrary resolutions. Gigapixel WSIs inherently contain diagnostic patterns at multiple scales, including cellular morphologies, tissue architectures, and global context, mirroring how expert pathologists examine WSIs. We introduce Multi-Resolution Pyramid Transformer (MRPT), a model that hierarchically aggregates multi-resolution information from cellular to tissue and WSI levels. MRPT employs a biologically meaningful Consecutive Cross-Resolution Attention (CCRA) mechanism to capture scale-independent interactions and enforces multi-resolution semantic consistency by aligning embeddings across resolutions, yielding robust and generalizable WSI representations. Pre-trained in a multi-resolution self-supervised manner on 624M patches, 2.4M regions, and 36K WSIs, MRPT learns rich coarse-to-fine histopathology features. Extensive experiments on 34 diverse datasets show that MRPT surpasses recent foundation models and Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in cancer subtype classification, tissue phenotyping, and Visual Question Answering (VQA) for WSI understanding.

</details>


### [81] [How Many Labels Are Enough? ALDA: Active Learning Deployment Advisor for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03511)
*Julia Machnio,Mads Nielsen,Mostafa Mehdipour Ghazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ALDA（Active-Learning Deployment Advisor），一个面向部署的主动学习策略选择框架。通过短期的试点阶段拟合学习曲线模型，预测各候选策略能否达到临床性能目标及所需标注数量，并引入"部署窗口"量化成本估计对临床阈值不确定性的敏感度，最终采用风险感知规则在成本近优的策略中推荐最稳健的选择。在四个医学影像分类任务上验证，仅需15-30%预算即可做出部署最优的方法选择，相比劣质策略最高可降低82%标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习（AL）虽有潜力降低医学影像标注成本，但实际部署中必须在完整标注预算用完之前就选定采样策略，而选错策略反而可能增加成本。目前缺乏一个能提前预测各策略效果、帮助用户在部署前做出可靠选择的决策框架。

Method: ALDA框架包含三个步骤：(1) 在短期试点阶段为每个候选策略拟合参数化学习曲线模型；(2) 估计该策略能否达到临床性能目标并预测所需标注数量；(3) 引入"部署窗口"来量化成本估计对临床性能阈值（受医生、监管等不确定性影响）变化的敏感度。最终采用风险感知决策规则，在预测成本近优的策略中优先选择部署窗口最窄（即对阈值修订最稳健）的策略。

Result: 在四个医学影像分类领域上的实验表明，ALDA仅需15-30%的预期预算作为试点即可准确预测部署最优的AL方法，相比选择不佳的策略可降低最高82%的标注成本。

Conclusion: ALDA不是提出新的采样启发式方法，而是提供了一个实用的决策层，回答了主动学习部署中的关键问题——"多少标注才够？"从而帮助临床团队在标注预算投入之前做出更可靠的策略选择。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) promises to reduce the cost of medical imaging projects by lowering the number of clinical labels required. However, practical deployment requires committing to a sampling strategy before the full annotation budget is spent, and choosing the wrong strategy can increase rather than decrease costs. We propose Active-Learning Deployment Advisor (ALDA), a deployment-oriented framework for AL method selection under clinical performance constraints. Given a short pilot phase, ALDA fits a parametric learning-curve model to each candidate strategy, estimates whether that strategy is expected to reach a required clinical performance target, and predicts the number of expert annotations needed to do so. In addition to absolute annotation cost, ALDA introduces a deployment window that quantifies the sensitivity of this cost estimate to uncertainty in the clinical threshold. The final recommendation follows a risk-aware rule: among strategies with near-optimal predicted cost, ALDA prefers the strategy with the narrowest deployment window, the most robust to threshold revisions. Experiments on four medical imaging classification domains show that ALDA predicts the deployment-optimal method from a pilot of 15-30% of the intended budget and reduces annotation costs by up to 82% compared with a poor strategy choice. Rather than introducing a new sampling heuristic, ALDA provides a practical decision layer that answers a deployment-critical question: how many labels are enough?

</details>


### [82] [Detecting Pose Estimation Failures via Keypoint Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03516)
*Robin Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一组手工设计的几何特征，通过检查2D关键点之间的空间关系来检测基于关键点的姿态估计失败，仅用逻辑回归分类器即可超越基于置信度的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键点级姿态估计方法通常独立预测各个关键点，忽略了旋转保持物体形状这一性质。不精确的关键点预测会导致姿态估计精度下降，进而影响下游任务的可靠性，因此需要一种简单有效的方法来识别不可靠的姿态估计。

Method: 提出一组手工设计的几何特征，包括：成对距离（pairwise distances）、重投影一致性（reprojection consistency）、以及渲染与掩码一致性（render and mask consistency），用于捕获关键点预测的自我一致性。使用逻辑回归分类器对这些特征进行训练，以区分姿态估计的成败。

Result: 尽管方法简单，基于这些几何特征训练的逻辑回归分类器能够可靠地检测姿态估计失败，优于依赖关键点不确定性的置信度方法（如共形关键点预测）。

Conclusion: 通过利用旋转保持形状的几何先验，仅通过检查2D关键点的空间位置就能有效识别失败姿态估计，无需复杂模型，为姿态估计的可靠性验证提供了一种轻量且高效的方案。

Abstract: One common approach to pose estimation involves predicting object keypoints in an image, followed by using Perspective-n-Point algorithms to compute the object's rotation and translation relative to the camera. While rotations preserve object shapes, this property is often neglected in keypoint-based pose estimation methods, where keypoints are typically predicted independently from each other. As imprecise keypoint predictions negatively affects pose estimation accuracy, it also limits its reliability in downstream tasks. In this work, we explore whether such inaccurate pose estimates can be identified by simply examining spatial locations between 2D keypoints. We propose a set of hand-crafted geometric features that capture the self-consistency of keypoint predictions, including pairwise distances, reprojection consistency, as well as render and mask consistency. Despite its simplicity, a logistic regression classifier trained on these features reliably detects pose estimation failures, outperforming confidence-based approaches like conformal keypoint predictions that rely solely on keypoint uncertainty.

</details>


### [83] [GVCCTurbo: Rate-Compute Quality Scheduling for Codebook Driven Generative Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03517)
*Ziyue Zeng,Dingjie Peng,Xun Su,Hiroshi Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GVCCTurbo提出了一种BPP驱动的调度器，将码本增益压缩中的"先验刷新"与"码本校正"解耦，通过一次性校准原子计数工作点和跳间隙比例，将目标码本载荷码率映射为轨迹长度和刷新周期，使BPP成为调度输入而非采样器长度的固定结果。在720p Wan-GVCC受控实验中，先验评估从20次降至9次，解码时间减少约44%，同时保持可接受的LPIPS成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有码本驱动生成式压缩编解码器将每个有限码率校正与一次全新的先验评估绑定，缩短采样器会同时移除携带目标依赖信息的校正槽位，导致比特率与计算量无法解耦。

Method: 提出BPP驱动的调度器，将昂贵的先验刷新与码本校正分离：每个协议一次性校准原子计数工作点和跳间隙比例，然后将目标码本载荷码率映射为轨迹长度和刷新周期。统一端点预测与有限码率引导接口，覆盖GVCC样式的整流流视频压缩和DDCM样式的扩散图像压缩。

Result: 在受控720p Wan-GVCC研究中，先验评估次数从20降至9，解码时间降低约44%；在调度族内，均匀刷新稀疏化（纯跳跃）是边界点，BPP感知内点以少2.9%的码本载荷比特获取一致更高的PSNR和相当的LPIPS；原生1080p曲线定位于超低位码率区间。

Conclusion: BPP到计算的调度可作为采样器长度调优的可控扩展，无需分配的调度点在每个边界点上占优；该调度器保持零训练部署特性，并兼容未来蒸馏先验。

Abstract: Codebook-driven generative compression uses a pretrained image or video generator as a zero-shot visual prior and transmits compact codebook indices to guide reconstruction at ultra-low bitrate. Current codecs tie each finite-rate correction to a fresh prior evaluation, so shortening the sampler also removes correction slots that carry target-dependent information. We propose GVCCTurbo, a BPP-driven scheduler that separates expensive prior refreshes from codebook corrections: after calibrating an atom-count operating point and skip-gap ratio once per protocol, it maps a target codebook-payload bitrate to a trajectory length and refresh period, making BPP a schedule input instead of a fixed consequence of sampler length. The same endpoint-prediction and finite-rate steering interface covers GVCC-style rectified-flow video and DDCM-style diffusion image compression, preserving zero-training deployment and compatibility with future distilled priors. Native 1080p curves position the complete zero-shot codec in the ultra-low-bitrate regime. In a controlled 720p Wan-GVCC study, the scheduler cuts prior evaluations from 20 to 9 for a $\sim\!44\%$ measured decoding-time reduction shared across the whole schedule family, at a small shared LPIPS cost on high-motion content; within that family, uniform refresh thinning (pure-skip) is a boundary point, and the BPP-aware interior point trades $2.9\%$ fewer codebook-payload bits for consistently higher PSNR at comparable LPIPS. These results support BPP-to-compute scheduling as a controllable extension of sampler-length tuning, without requiring the allocated point to dominate every boundary point.

</details>


### [84] [MinerU.Chem: A High-Precision System for Optical Chemical Structure and Reaction Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03525)
*Haote Yang,Jiang Wu,Jingchao Wang,Xingjian Wei,Lixin Ma,Linye Li,Chen Zhu,Xiaolong Wu,Yuheng Lu,Ziran Zhu,Junyuan Gao,Lingli Ge,Yuan Xu,Huijie Ao,QianQian Wu,Dechen Lin,Huaiyu Gu,Lu Chen,Shengxin Lu,ShaSha Wang,Yuanyuan Cao,Zhejia Yu,Ruijie Zhang,Zimai Tian,Jiaxing Sun,Yinfan Wang,Jiahe Song,Chuang Wang,Yubin Wang,Rui Nie,Hao Zheng,Bowen Jiang,Hongbin Lai,Yifan He,Chengjin Liu,Tingting Zhang,Liqun Wei,Lijun Wu,Bin Wang,Yuqiang Li,Guangyu Wang,Wei Li,Bowen Zhou,Dahua Lin,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MinerU.Chem是一个面向有机化学文献的文档解析系统，集成在MinerU在线平台上，通过五个化学专属模块将文献中的化学图像区域转换为分子摘要列表和反应摘要列表，在MolRecBench-Wild基准上SMILES精确匹配准确率达到93.02%，显著优于GPT-5.6-Sol（74.87%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机化学文献中的分子结构、反应示意图和实验条件多以图像、表格等非结构化形式呈现，通用文档解析系统难以将其转换为机器可读数据，这严重限制了有机化学知识库的构建以及反应预测、逆合成、条件推荐、分子性质预测和药物分子设计等AI for Chemistry任务的数据生产。

Method: MinerU.Chem构建在MinerU通用文档解析管道之上，新增五个化学专属模块：(1)化学相关性过滤、(2)分子结构检测、(3)分子标识符提取、(4)分子结构识别、(5)反应方案解析。这些模块将文档中的有机化学相关图像区域转换为分子摘要列表和反应摘要列表。分子结构识别采用CARBON（复杂原子表示与键合对象符号）作为核心表示，既能保留原始图像的视觉布局和复杂化学语义，又能导出MolFile和SMILES等标准下游格式。

Result: 在MolRecBench-Wild的SMILES可评估子集（N=2,392）上，MinerU.Chem的分子结构识别模块取得93.02%的SMILES精确匹配准确率，以18.15个百分点优势超越最佳对比系统GPT-5.6-Sol（74.87%）。系统已集成至MinerU在线平台。

Conclusion: MinerU.Chem通过化学专属的文档解析能力有效解决了有机化学文献中非结构化化学信息向机器可读数据转换的问题，其分子结构识别的性能大幅领先现有系统，为有机化学知识库构建和AI for Chemistry任务提供了可靠的数据生产工具，并已实现线上平台部署。

Abstract: In organic chemistry papers and patents, molecular structures, reaction schemes, and experimental conditions are often presented as molecular structure depictions, reaction diagrams, and complex tables or figures. Such information is difficult for general-purpose document parsing systems to directly convert into machine-readable data. This limits data production for organic chemistry knowledge base construction and for AI for Chemistry tasks such as reaction prediction, retrosynthesis, condition recommendation, molecular property prediction, and drug molecule design. This report introduces MinerU.Chem, a document parsing system for organic chemistry literature integrated into the MinerU online platform. Built on top of MinerU's general document parsing pipeline, MinerU.Chem adds five chemistry-specific modules: chemistry relevance filtering, molecular structure detection, molecule identifier extraction, molecular structure recognition, and reaction scheme parsing. Together, these modules convert organic-chemistry-related image regions in documents into a Molecule Summary List and a Reaction Summary List. For molecular structure recognition, MinerU.Chem uses CARBON (Complex Atomic Representation and Bonding Object Notation) as its core representation. CARBON enables recognition results to preserve both the visual layout of the original image and complex chemical semantics, while supporting the export of standard downstream formats such as MolFile and SMILES. On the SMILES-evaluable subset of MolRecBench-Wild (N=2,392), MinerU.Chem's molecular structure recognition module achieves a SMILES exact-match accuracy of 93.02%, outperforming the best evaluated comparison system, GPT-5.6-Sol (74.87%), by 18.15 percentage points. The system has been integrated into the MinerU online platform and is available at https://mineru.net/OpenSourceTools/Extractor .

</details>


### [85] [IRIS: Visual-Semantic Binding for Forgery-Resistant Watermarking of Diffusion Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03539)
*Xiaoyan Feng,Zheng Gao,Tong Guan,Rui Bao,Bokang Zeng,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IRIS是一种无需训练的扩散模型水印方案，从生成图像本身读取内容代码并派生一次性环形水印，在采样轨迹最后低噪声步骤中嵌入，实现语义绑定水印，既能抵抗移植伪造，又能抵抗再生成攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型水印大多嵌入与图像内容无关的固定图案，攻击者可将水印移植到模型未生成的图像上造成伪造。虽然有水印绑定视觉语义的方法，但现有绑定锚定的是代理图像而非被标记图像本身。此外，水印面临一对相反的敏感性要求：需要在语义变化下失效，同时在常见处理后保持稳定。

Method: IRIS从非水印生成图像中读取内容代码，通过内容代码与密钥派生一次性环形（ring），随后返回到同一采样轨迹的最后低噪声步骤，在语义已定型后将环形融合进图像中。内容代码通过共享的规范化（canonicalization）流程在嵌入和检测两侧读取，使其在常见失真和轻度再生成下保持稳定、在语义变化下翻转。检测时仅凭查询图像和密钥重算环形，因此水印在异源或拼接图像上自动失效，接受度随语义位移而变化。

Result: 在三个提示数据集上，IRIS检测可靠且保持与其同种子非水印对照版本接近的保真度，达到现有生成内水印无法企及的保真水平。与传统固定图案水印和事后（post-hoc）水印相比，IRIS是唯一同时抵御水印移植伪造和再生成攻击的方案。

Conclusion: IRIS通过将水印与图像自身语义绑定并在生成轨迹后期嵌入，解决了现有水印易被移植伪造和事后水印易被再生成剥离的问题，实现了语义级鲁棒且高保真的生成内水印方案。

Abstract: Most in-generation diffusion watermarks embed patterns independent of the image that carries them, and attackers transplant the marks onto images the generator did not produce, resulting in forgery. Binding the mark to visual semantics prevents such transplantation, yet existing bindings anchor to a proxy image rather than the image they mark. Realizing visual-semantic binding inside generation faces two challenges. The mark derives from the image itself yet enters the sampling trajectory before that image exists, and may itself shift the semantics it binds. The binding also meets opposite sensitivity demands, breaking under semantic change while holding through common processing. We present IRIS, a training-free watermarking scheme that embeds an Intrinsic Ring Identifier from Semantics. IRIS reads a content code from the non-watermarked generated image, derives a one-time ring from the code and a secret key, returns to the final low-noise steps of the same trajectory and blends the ring in, after the semantics it binds are settled. To meet the opposite sensitivity demands, the code is read through a canonicalization shared between embedding and detection, holding through common distortions and mild regeneration while flipping under semantic change. Detection recomputes the ring from the query image and the key alone, and the mark therefore fails on a foreign or spliced image, with acceptance tracking semantic displacement. On three prompt datasets IRIS detects reliably and stays close to its same-seed non-watermarked counterpart, a fidelity prior in-generation marks do not reach. While forgeries transfer fixed-pattern marks and regeneration strips post-hoc marks, IRIS alone among the compared marks withstands both.

</details>


### [86] [S$^3$-Diff: Structural Semantic Synergy Diffusion Model for High Fidelity Super Resolution of Pathological Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03540)
*Jiaming Liang,QiHui Han,Guangye Ou,Jiawen Liu,Haolin Chen,Xi Zhong,Jiazhou Chen,Xiaoqi Sheng,Hongmin Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S3-Diff，一种面向病理图像超分辨率的扩散模型框架，通过标本感知结构锚定（SSA）和结构引导语义保真调优（SSFT）两个核心模块，在提升重建质量的同时保持病理形态结构和语义一致性，并改善了下游生存分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理诊断依赖高分辨率全切片图像，但实际临床中常受限于成像设备、存储和传输等条件而使用低分辨率图像。现有超分辨率方法倾向于平滑诊断关键形态特征，导致纹理过度平滑和语义漂移，损害下游临床判读。因此需要一种既能提升分辨率又能保持病理形态结构真实性的超分辨率方法。

Method: S3-Diff包含两个核心模块：(1) Specimen-aware Structural Anchoring (SSA)——结合固定SAM提取的预后感知组织支持信息和低分辨率-高分辨率梯度差异，生成标本特定的结构锚点以保持病理形态；(2) Structure-guided Semantic Fidelity Tuning (SSFT)——利用SSA导出的结构监督来适配DINOv3表示，将适配后的语义能量与低分辨率图像提取的边缘和灰度线索相结合，形成控制信号引导去噪过程，抑制随机伪影并维持结构一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明S3-Diff在重建质量和下游生存分析性能两方面均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: S3-Diff通过结构语义协同的扩散框架有效解决了病理图像超分辨率中的形态保持和语义一致性问题，为低分辨率病理图像的临床可用性提供了可行方案，源代码将公开。

Abstract: Digital pathology relies on high-resolution whole slide images for accurate diagnosis, yet limitations in imaging devices, storage, and transmission often make lower-resolution pathology images more common in clinical workflows. Current super-resolution techniques often tend to smooth diagnostically relevant morphology, leading to over-smoothed textures and semantic drift that compromise downstream clinical interpretation. To this end, we develop the Structural Semantic Synergy Diffusion Model (S3-Diff), a diffusion framework for high-fidelity super-resolution of pathological images. The core of S3-Diff is Specimen-aware Structural Anchoring (SSA), which combines prognosis-aware tissue support extracted by a fixed SAM with LR-HR gradient discrepancies to generate a specimen-specific structural anchor to preserve pathological morphology. Concurrently, we introduce Structure-guided Semantic Fidelity Tuning (SSFT) to adapt DINOv3 representations using SSA-derived structural supervision. SSFT combines the adapted semantic energy with LR-derived edge and grayscale cues. The resulting control guides denoising to suppress stochastic artifacts and maintain structural consistency. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that S3-Diff consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both reconstruction quality and downstream survival analysis performance. The source code will be made public.

</details>


### [87] [Test-Time Augmentation for Tabular-to-Image Classifiers under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03557)
*Malena Loza,Felipe Grijalva,Eva Milara,Luis Bote-Curiel,Francisco J. Lara-Abelenda,David Chushig-Muzo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了25种测试时增强（TTA）技术对表格转图像方法的分布外（OOD）性能影响，涵盖6种编码方法（TINTO、IGTD、DeepInsight等）和6类TTA策略，实验表明复合与光度类TTA能有效提升OOD鲁棒性，而频域变换会损害编码器的特征映射导致性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格转图像方法利用深度学习模型处理结构化数据虽表现优异，但其在分布偏移下的鲁棒性鲜有研究。测试时增强（TTA）在图像分类中已被证明能提升泛化能力，但其在该新兴范式中的效果尚不明确，亟需系统性评估。

Method: 使用两个TableShift基准数据集（HELOC和Voting），对6种表格转图像编码方法（TINTO、IGTD、DeepInsight、BIE、DistanceMatrix、Fotomics）生成的图像表示，应用25种TTA技术（分为几何、光度、结构、频率/编码、混合、复合六类）进行测试时增强，评估OOD条件下的预测性能、鲁棒性与方差。

Result: TTA整体提升OOD性能；复合和光度类策略在鲁棒性与方差之间取得最佳平衡；而改变编码器特征到强度映射的频域变换持续导致性能下降。

Conclusion: TTA是提升基于表格转图像表示的分类器在分布偏移下鲁棒性和泛化能力的有效手段，尤其推荐复合与光度类增强策略，同时应避免使用频域变换类增强。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image methods that convert tabular data into visual representations have emerged as a novel paradigm for leveraging the high performance of deep learning models. Despite their advantages, the robustness of these methods under distribution shifts remains under explored. Test-Time Augmentation (TTA) is an effective approach in image classification to improve model generalization and robustness, where predictions over multiple transformed views of each input are aggregated. This work evaluates the impact of TTA techniques on predictive performance under Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) for representations generated by tabular-to-image methods. Six tabular-to-image encoding methods were considered: TINTO, IGTD, DeepInsight, BIE, DistanceMatrix, Fotomics. Twenty-five TTA techniques were used, organized into six types: Geometric, Photometric, Structural, Frequency/Encoding, Mixup, and Composite. We employed two datasets from the TableShift benchmark (HELOC and Voting) that provide in-distribution and OOD test subsets designed to evaluate the effect of distribution shifts on tabular data. The results indicate that TTA improves OOD performance, with composite and photometric strategies providing the best trade-off between robustness and variance. In contrast, frequency-domain transformations that alter the encoder's feature-to-intensity mapping consistently degrade performance. These findings highlight TTA as a promising approach for improving the robustness and generalization of classifiers trained on image representations derived from tabular data, particularly under distribution shifts.

</details>


### [88] [Compass: Degradation-Simulated Reciprocal Learning with Lightweight Needle RWKV for Multimodal Crack Segmentation under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03559)
*Hui Liu,Chen Jia,Fan Shi,Xu Cheng,Mianzhao Wang,Shengyong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Compass是一个轻量级网络，用于任意模态缺失下鲁棒的裂纹分割，通过退化模拟蒸馏（DSD）、Needle Block和证据拓扑保持融合（ETPF）三大模块，在仅2.58M参数下于CrackDepth数据集中实现90%深度模态缺失时的F1=0.8216、mIoU=0.8434，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业设施中的多模态裂纹分割面临关键挑战：在模态缺失时防止像素级性能退化，同时保持低计算成本。现有方法无法有效解决因模态缺失导致的语义退化问题，亟需一个既能保持语义完整性又计算高效的鲁棒分割方案。

Method: Compass由三个核心组件构成：(1) DSD（退化模拟蒸馏）：构建模拟更严重缺失状态的退化流，与原流进行互蒸馏，解耦完整感知与退化适应；其中FAPT（特征感知原型发射器）执行模态无关的原型引导特征补全以维持语义完整性。(2) Needle Block：轻量级骨干网络，将裂纹方向线索注入WKV调制，结合连通性感知门控与各向异性上下文探测进行结构感知建模。(3) ETPF（证据拓扑保持融合）：通过Dempster-Shafer证据组合与不确定性门控解码融合多模态特征，在抑制不可靠特征的同时保持裂纹拓扑。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，Compass在多样的模态缺失场景下均达到SOTA性能。具体而言，在CrackDepth上即使90%深度模态缺失，仍能达到F1=0.8216、mIoU=0.8434，且仅需2.58M参数，体现了优越的鲁棒性和计算效率。

Conclusion: Compass通过解耦完整感知与退化适应的蒸馏策略、模态无关的原型补全以及基于证据理论的拓扑保持融合，有效解决了多模态裂纹分割中模态缺失导致的语义退化问题，在保持极低参数量的同时实现了最先进的鲁棒分割性能。

Abstract: In multimodal crack segmentation for industrial facilities, the key challenge is preventing missing modalities from degrading pixel-level performance while maintaining low computational cost. Existing methods struggle to address semantic degradation caused by missing modalities. We propose Compass, a lightweight network for robust crack segmentation under arbitrary missing modalities. Compass comprises Degradation Simulation Distillation (DSD), Needle Block, and Evidential Topology-Preserving Fusion (ETPF). DSD constructs a degradation simulation stream that mimics more severe missing conditions and performs reciprocal distillation with the original stream, decoupling complete perception from degradation adaptation. Within DSD, Feature-Aware Prototype Transmitter (FAPT) performs modality agnostic prototype-guided feature completion to maintain semantic integrity under incomplete modality conditions. As a lightweight backbone, Needle injects crack-direction cues into WKV modulation and combines connectivity-aware gating with anisotropic context probing for structure-aware modeling. ETPF fuses multimodal features via Dempster-Shafer evidential combination with uncertainty-gated decoding, preserving crack topology while suppressing unreliable features. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance under diverse missing modality scenarios. Even with 90\% depth modality missing on CrackDepth, Compass achieves F1 of 0.8216 and mIoU of 0.8434 with only 2.58M parameters. The code is available at https://github.com/Karl1109/Compass.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Simply Environment Scaling: Designing Effective Environment Distributions for Multimodal Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03571)
*Kejian Zhu,Zhuoran Jin,Dongqi Huang,Hongbang Yuan,Yupu Hao,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现简单地增加多模态环境数量并不总能提升智能体训练效果，并提出从多样性和难度结构两个维度构建更有效的训练环境分布：通过Ability-aware Environment Selection (AES)获取多样化环境集，以及通过Hierarchical Difficulty Curriculum (HDC)组织两级难度课程学习（武器弱化与状态规模递进）。实验证明两种方法能有效改进多模态智能体训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过构建大规模多模态环境池来训练智能体，但作者发现简单地增加多模态环境数量并不总是有利的。通过一系列实验分析了当前多模态环境分布中存在的局限性，为后续改进提供依据。

Method: 从两个维度改进训练环境分布：(1) 多样性维度，提出Ability-aware Environment Selection (AES)方法选择多样化的环境集；(2) 难度结构维度，提出Hierarchical Difficulty Curriculum (HDC)方法，通过武器弱化和状态规模递进两个难度层级组织课程学习。

Result: 实验表明，AES和HDC两种方法均能有效提升多模态智能体的训练效果。

Conclusion: 仅仅增加多模态环境数量并非提升智能体训练效果的有效策略，关键在于构建合理的环境分布，兼顾多样性（通过能力感知的环境选择）和难度结构（通过层级化难度课程）。

Abstract: Recent works train agents by constructing large-scale multimodal environment pools. However, we find that simply increasing the number of multimodal environments does not always benefit. We further analyze the limitations in current multimodal environment distributions through a series of experiments. Based on these findings, we study how to build more effective training environment distributions from two dimensions: **diversity** and **difficulty structure**. For diversity, we propose **Ability-aware Environment Selection (AES)** to obtain diverse environment sets. For difficulty structure, we propose **Hierarchical Difficulty Curriculum (HDC)**, which organizes curriculum learning through two difficulty levels: harness weakening and state-scale progression. Experiments show that AES and HDC effectively improve multimodal agent training.

</details>


### [90] [SlimVLM: Sensitivity-aware Dynamic Structured Pruning with Adaptive Visual Token Selection for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03580)
*Yaozhi Wen,Jialong Guo,Zhenliang Ni,Han Shu,Xinghao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SlimVLM是一种面向视觉语言模型(VLM)的结构化剪枝框架，通过自适应视觉token选择策略和敏感性感知的动态剪枝机制，在压缩模型参数的同时保持任务性能，在多项多模态基准上达到最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(VLM)虽在多模态理解上表现优异，但其庞大的参数量带来了巨大的计算开销，难以部署在资源受限设备上。虽然剪枝在大型语言模型(LLM)上已证明有效，但直接应用于VLM会导致显著的性能下降，主要原因是冗余的视觉token干扰了重要性估计。

Method: SlimVLM包含两个核心机制：(1) 自适应视觉token选择策略：利用平均文本到视觉注意力分数评估视觉token的重要性，在剪枝过程中根据设定阈值去除冗余token，从而优化重要性计算；(2) 敏感性感知动态剪枝机制：通过计算剪枝模块与未剪枝模块输出之间的线性重建误差，为每个模块确定合适的剪枝比例，确保整体性能稳定。

Result: 实验结果表明，SlimVLM在多个多模态基准测试上优于现有的方法，达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SlimVLM通过结合视觉token选择性剪枝和模块级敏感性感知的动态剪枝策略，有效解决了VLM直接应用剪枝导致性能下降的问题，实现了VLM在资源受限设备上的高效部署。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in processing and understanding both text and images, their large parameter sizes lead to significant computational overhead, limiting their deployment on resource-constrained devices. While pruning has been effective for compressing Large Language Models (LLMs), directly applying it to VLMs leads to significant performance drops, largely due to redundant visual tokens interfering with importance estimation. To this end, we propose SlimVLM, a structured pruning framework designed to compress VLMs while preserving their task performance. We introduce an adaptive visual token selection strategy for VLMs that leverages average text-to-visual attention scores to assess the importance of visual tokens, removing redundant ones during pruning based on a set threshold, thereby optimizing the importance calculation. Recognizing the varying tolerance to sparsity across different modules, we also propose a Sensitivity-aware dynamic pruning mechanism that determines the appropriate pruning ratio for each module by calculating the linear reconstruction error between the outputs of the pruned and unpruned modules, ensuring overall performance stability. Experimental results show that SlimVLM outperforms existing methods across multiple multimodal benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [91] [Geospatial-Prior Guidance for 3D Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03618)
*Meng Wang,Shougao Zhang,Wenzhe He,Ruihui Li,Nan Hu,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoScene是一个地理空间引导的3D语义场景补全框架，联合使用卫星图像和结构化OpenStreetMap线索作为软先验，通过学习逐体素的可靠性权重来平衡车载观测与地理空间引导，在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360基准上一致提升了几何与语义补全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载图像因遮挡和受限视野导致大范围场景区域欠约束，卫星图像虽能提供广域上下文，但仅靠外观线索结构指导有限且可能因时空差异而不可靠。

Method: GeoScene框架联合卫星图像与结构化OpenStreetMap线索作为软先验，学习车载观测和地理空间引导的互补逐体素可靠性权重，并利用这些权重控制可见与不可见区域的体素特征细化，在保留局部视觉证据的同时利用车载视野之外的大尺度道路和建筑结构。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360上的实验表明，GeoScene在地理空间先验辅助设置下一致提升了几何与语义补全性能，对大规模静态和地理空间结构化的类别提升最为显著。

Conclusion: GeoScene验证了地理空间先验（卫星图像+OSM结构化线索）结合可靠性加权机制可以有效缓解车载感知的视野与遮挡局限，为3D语义场景补全提供了可行的软先验融合方案。

Abstract: Inferring complete 3D geometry and semantics from onboard images remains challenging because occlusions and restricted fields of view leave large scene regions underconstrained. Although satellite imagery provides wide-area context, appearance cues alone offer limited structural guidance and may be unreliable because of spatial or temporal discrepancies. We present GeoScene, a geospatially guided framework that jointly uses satellite imagery and structured OpenStreetMap cues as soft priors for 3D semantic scene completion. GeoScene learns complementary voxel-wise reliability weights for onboard observations and geospatial guidance, and uses them to control feature refinement in observed and unobserved regions. This design preserves local visual evidence while exploiting large-scale road and building structure beyond onboard visibility. Experiments on SemanticKITTI and SSCBench-KITTI-360 demonstrate that GeoScene consistently improves both geometric and semantic completion under the geospatial-prior-assisted setting, with the most pronounced benefits for large-scale static and geospatially structured classes.

</details>


### [92] [SEER: A Self-Grounded Evidence Interface for Controlled Spatial Relation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03631)
*Feixiang Liu,Likun Wang,Qiang Qiu,Hui Xu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SEER（Self-grounded Evidence for Entity-Relation Reasoning），一种针对冻结VLM的训练无关推理时证据接口，通过隐藏候选关系、构建查询特定视图（明确主体/客体角色）并保留完整图像与稀疏框几何作为互补证据，提升空间关系问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间关系问题要求模型在比较布局前先识别查询的主体和客体，但VLM即使能识别两个实体，仍可能从错误的实例或模糊的全局视图作答。作者探究是否通过使查询特定的证据显式化来缓解这一失败模式。

Method: SEER包含三个关键组件：(1) 在实体对定位阶段隐藏候选关系；(2) 构造具有明确主体/客体角色的查询特定视图；(3) 保留完整图像和稀疏框几何作为互补证据。对于具有精确逆关系支持的relation-choice协议，可选优化模块在恰好一个视觉状态服从对应逆关系时交换实体角色并改变前向决策（互逆一致性优化）。

Result: 在图像不相交的GQA-Train900测试中（冻结在模型评分前），SEER相较Full提升+3.94 [2.17,5.72]；在标签无关的grounding-order counterbalancing下以及535行实体名称唯一的子集上增益仍为正。在2,434条过滤后的EmbSpatial pair-relation问题上，未改变协议下三种模型均获得+4.35到+11.79的提升。匹配对照实验区分了局部重聚焦与角色显式条件化的贡献。

Conclusion: 查询特定的证据构建是主要的干预手段，互逆一致性是较小但协议特定的优化。

Abstract: Spatial relation questions require a model to identify the queried subject and object before comparing their layout. Yet a VLM can recognize both entities and still answer from the wrong instance or an ambiguous global view. We ask whether making query-specific evidence explicit can mitigate this failure and propose SEER (Self-grounded Evidence for Entity-Relation Reasoning), a training-free inference-time evidence interface for frozen VLMs. SEER hides candidate relations during pair localization, constructs a query-specific view with explicit subject/object roles, and retains the full image and sparse box geometry as complementary evidence. For relation-choice protocols with exact inverse support, an optional refinement swaps the entity roles and changes the forward decision only when exactly one visual state obeys the corresponding inverse relation. On an image-disjoint GQA-Train900 test frozen before model scoring, SEER pools to +3.94 [2.17,5.72] over Full; the gain remains positive under label-independent grounding-order counterbalancing and on the 535 rows whose entity names are unique. The unchanged protocol yields +4.35 to +11.79 on all 2,434 filtered EmbSpatial pair-relation questions across three models. Matched controls separate local refocus from role-explicit conditioning. These results establish query-specific evidence construction as the principal intervention, with reciprocal consistency as a smaller protocol-specific refinement.

</details>


### [93] [Learning Biomechanically Plausible Human Motion from Sparse Radar Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03637)
*Jonas Leo Mueller,Markus Gambietz,Alexander Weiss,Daniel Krauss,Bjoern M. Eskofier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于雷达的可微分端到端人体姿态估计框架，通过全骨骼运动学模型（前向运动学）监督姿态网络，并预测个体化身体节段比例以缩放生物力学骨架，同时引入接触分类损失鼓励脚-地交互的物理合理性。在11名健康受试者进行康复训练的留一受试者交叉验证中，实现了6.456±1.759 cm的MPJPE和8.083±0.884°的MPJAE，证明单低代价雷达传感器在受控实验室条件下恢复可解释生物力学描述符的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于雷达的人体姿态估计方法主要关注改进学习算法，但将人体表示为不受约束的关键点坐标，忽视了解剖学保真度这一未充分探索的维度。作者旨在通过集成全身骨骼运动学模型来解决这一问题，为未来临床运动分析奠定基础。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从雷达点云特征预测受试者特异的身体节段比例，用于缩放生物力学骨架；(2) 运动预测网络将时间序列雷达序列映射为广义坐标（关节角度）；(3) 可微分前向运动学将预测的关节角度转换为3D位置。此外，引入接触分类损失以鼓励脚-地交互的物理合理性。训练时，姿态网络通过前向运动学进行监督，受试者特异的几何参数事先拟合。

Result: 在11名健康受试者执行康复训练的留一受试者交叉验证下，该方法取得6.456±1.759 cm的平均每关节位置误差（MPJPE）、8.083±0.884°的平均每关节角度误差（MPJAE）、0.935±0.009的接触分类F1分数，以及3.4±1.3%的缩放误差。

Conclusion: 该概念验证研究表明，从单个低代价雷达传感器在受控实验室设置下恢复可解释的生物力学描述符是可行的，这是未来临床运动分析的必要前提条件。

Abstract: Radar-based human pose estimation has focused on improving learning algorithms while representing the body as unconstrained keypoint coordinates. We address the underexplored dimension of anatomical fidelity by integrating a full-body skeletal model into a differentiable, end-to-end trainable radar-based pose estimation framework, in which the pose network is supervised through forward kinematics while subject-specific geometry is fitted beforehand. Subject-specific body segment proportions are predicted from radar point cloud features to scale a biomechanical skeleton. A motion prediction network maps temporal radar sequences to generalized coordinates, and differentiable forward kinematics converts predicted joint angles into 3D positions. A contact classification loss encourages physically plausible foot-ground interaction. Under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation on 11 healthy participants performing rehabilitation exercises, the framework achieves 6.456 +/- 1.759 cm mean per-joint position error (MPJPE), 8.083 +/- 0.884 degrees mean per-joint angle error (MPJAE), 0.935 +/- 0.009 contact classification F1, and 3.4 +/- 1.3 % scaling error. This proof-of-concept study demonstrates the feasibility of recovering interpretable biomechanical descriptors from a single low-cost radar sensor in a controlled laboratory setting, a prerequisite for future clinical motion analysis.

</details>


### [94] [When Do Fewer Visual Tokens Accelerate Multimodal Inference? A Break-Even Study Across Decision Locations and Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03649)
*Hao Dou,Ruiwen Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型中减少视觉token对端到端延迟的影响，发现更少的视觉token并不一定意味着更低的端到端延迟，并通过可复现的实验协议评估了不同策略（post-vision预测器、pre-vision图像尺寸规则、pre-vision路由）的盈亏平衡点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设减少视觉token可以降低延迟，但实际端到端延迟还受决策开销、共享工作及算子可优化空间等因素影响。作者旨在建立一个可复现的评估协议，厘清不同视觉token减少策略的真实延迟收益。

Method: 提出一种可复现的评估协议，将延迟分解为阶段级（stage-level）组成部分，统一核算决策开销、共享工作以及各策略可避免的算子。在30个示例的试点中，对比了两种自回归probe策略、一种post-vision轻量预测器、一种pre-vision图像尺寸规则以及pre-vision路由策略，在RTX 3090和A100两种GPU上运行，并进行保守的all-pairs Holm多重比较校正。

Result: 两种自回归probe虽使用状态复用，仍比Full策略更慢。post-vision轻量预测器在两个GPU上均产生置信区间小于零的配对结果，且在Holm校正后仍然显著。pre-vision图像尺寸规则的区间在两个GPU上均低于零，但校正后不再显著。pre-vision路由具备结构性优势（可避免预处理和视觉编码），在A100上此优势超过了post-vision策略近8倍的token缩减量。

Conclusion: 减少视觉token并不等比例降低端到端延迟；pre-vision路由由于能跳过预处理和视觉编码阶段，在结构性延迟收益上优于post-vision剪枝；且报告的质量指标仅是Full模型答对案例上的条件结果，不等同于benchmark精度。

Abstract: Fewer visual tokens do not guarantee lower end-to-end latency. We evaluate break-even with a reproducible protocol that accounts for decision overhead, shared work, and the operators each policy can avoid. A stage-level decomposition reconciles these components with measured end-to-end latency. In a 30-example pilot, the two tested autoregressive probes remain slower than Full despite state reuse. A lightweight post-vision predictor yields paired confidence intervals below zero on RTX 3090 and A100 and remains significant after a conservative all-pairs Holm correction. A pre-vision image-size rule also yields intervals below zero on both GPUs, although neither comparison remains significant after the same correction. Pre-vision routing has a structural opportunity unavailable to post-vision pruning: it can avoid preprocessing and vision encoding. On A100, this opportunity outweighs a nearly eightfold larger downstream token reduction by the post-vision policy. Reported quality is conditional on examples answered correctly by Full and is not benchmark accuracy.

</details>


### [95] [Morphology-Aware Implicit Super-Resolution Network for Pathological Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03664)
*Jiaming Liang,QiHui Han,Haolin Chen,Chengxin Ye,Jiawen Liu,Jiazhou Chen,Xiaoqi Sheng,Hongmin Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Morph-ISR，一种用于数字病理学（DP）的形态感知隐式超分辨率框架，通过将SR重新构建为连续坐标重建问题，并结合隐式位置感知核生成器（IPKG）和形态保真先验（MFP）来恢复亚像素级精度的诊断相关细节。在TCGA和SurGen数据集上，Morph-ISR在LPIPS和ST-LPIPS指标上分别超越次优方法达38.37%和39.55%，同时保持优异的PSNR和SSIM，实现高效边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理诊断依赖高分辨率全切片图像，但临床部署常受硬件成本限制。超分辨率（SR）可通过计算增强低分辨率采集图像作为替代方案，然而现有SR方法在复杂组织变异性下难以保留精细细胞形态，导致纹理过度平滑和结构边界模糊。因此需要一种能够保持诊断相关形态细节的超分辨率方法。

Method: Morph-ISR包含两个核心组件：（1）隐式位置感知核生成器（IPKG），将SR重构为连续坐标重建问题，自适应建模空间变化的组织形态；（2）形态保真先验（MFP），利用预训练细胞分割网络的语义指导，强制执行边界保持和区域感知重建，增强关键细胞边界和核纹理的表征。

Result: 在TCGA和SurGen数据集上，Morph-ISR在LPIPS和ST-LPIPS指标上达到最佳性能，相比次优方法分别降低38.37%和39.55%，同时保持较强的PSNR和SSIM。此外，紧凑的参数化和高吞吐量支持高效的边缘部署。

Conclusion: Morph-ISR在保留诊断相关的细胞边界和核纹理方面优于现有方法，同时具有参数紧凑和高效推理的优势，适合临床边缘部署。代码和训练模型将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Accurate diagnosis in Digital Pathology (DP) relies on high-resolution whole-slide images, yet clinical deployment is often limited by hardware costs. Super-Resolution (SR) offers a promising alternative by computationally enhancing low-resolution acquisitions. However, existing SR methods frequently struggle to preserve fine-grained cellular morphology, leading to texture oversmoothing and blurred structural boundaries under complex tissue variability. To address this issue, we propose Morph-ISR, a morphology-aware implicit super-resolution framework for DP that restores diagnostically relevant details with sub-pixel precision. Morph-ISR reformulates SR as a continuous coordinate-based reconstruction problem and integrates an Implicit Position-aware Kernel Generator (IPKG) to adaptively model spatially varying tissue morphology. To further enhance structural fidelity, a Morphological Fidelity Prior (MFP) is introduced, leveraging semantic guidance from a pre-trained cell segmentation network to enforce boundary-preserving and region-aware reconstruction, thereby improving the representation of critical cellular boundaries and nuclear textures. Experiments on TCGA and SurGen datasets show that Morph-ISR achieves the best LPIPS and ST-LPIPS among the evaluated methods, reducing them by up to 38.37% and 39.55%, respectively, over the second-best methods while maintaining strong PSNR and SSIM. These results demonstrate superior preservation of diagnostically relevant cellular boundaries and nuclear textures, while compact parameterization and high throughput support efficient edge deployment. Code and trained models will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [96] [XiDepth: a Lightweight and Efficient Network for Self-supervised Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03666)
*Elena Izzo,Riccardo Toniolo,Lamberto Ballan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XiDepth，一种基于XiNet算子块的轻量级自监督单目深度估计架构，在KITTI数据集上以仅0.8M参数达到最先进性能，并在Raspberry Pi 4上实现FLOPs降低40%、能耗降低35%，适用于嵌入式环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督单目深度估计因其无需深度传感器标注且数据采集成本低而受到广泛关注，但现有最先进模型常采用深度可分离卷积和注意力机制，这些组件在嵌入式环境中能耗高且兼容性差。因此，在保持性能的同时实现资源高效的神经网络是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出XiDepth轻量级架构，核心创新是基于XiNet算子块来增强特征提取能力，同时保持低计算复杂度和低能耗需求，从而替代传统的高能耗深度卷积和注意力机制。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上取得最先进性能，仅需0.8M参数；在Raspberry Pi 4上验证了实际嵌入式应用可行性，相比领先方法FLOPs降低40%，能耗降低35%。

Conclusion: XiDepth证明了在嵌入式设备上实现高性能自监督单目深度估计的可行性，通过新颖的算子块设计在计算效率、能耗与模型精度之间取得了良好的平衡，适合部署在资源受限的计算环境。

Abstract: Self-supervised monocular depth estimation has emerged as an appealing solution to design lightweight and effective models for deployment on computationally constrained devices due to its reduced reliance on expensive depth sensors. By eliminating the need for ground-truth annotations and leveraging the simplicity of monocular camera setups, this approach facilitates cost-effective data collection and broad applicability across fields such as computer vision and robotics. A critical challenge is achieving resource-efficient neural networks without compromising the overall performance. State-of-the-art models generally adopt depth-wise convolutions and attention mechanisms; however, these functions often incur high energy costs and face compatibility issues in embedded environments. To address this, we propose XiDepth, a lightweight architecture based on the XiNet operator block, designed to enhance feature extraction while maintaining low computational complexity and energy demand. On the KITTI dataset, XiDepth achieves state-of-the-art performance with only 0.8M parameters. Tests on a Raspberry Pi 4 further confirm its suitability for real-world embedded applications, reducing FLOPs by 40% and energy consumption by 35% compared to leading methods.

</details>


### [97] [Keep the Needle, Prune the Haystack: Defect-Preserving Token Pruning for Efficient Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03681)
*Yanning Hou,Jingyuan Zhang,Xiaoyun Wang,Qixiang Ma,Sihang Zhou,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KeepAD是一个保缺陷的token剪枝框架，通过高召回率、异常感知的路由机制将视觉零样本异常检测中的token保留率降至20%以下，同时将图像级和像素级AUROC的平均下降控制在2.7个百分点以内，在最激进配置下相比最强的CLIP基线实现了7.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本视觉异常检测方法对所有图像和空间token进行密集计算，但实际场景中正常样本占绝大多数且异常通常只占据小区域。直接使用token剪枝存在不对称风险：移除正常token仅带来冗余计算，而移除异常token可能消除检测与定位的唯一证据，尤其在早期层中剪枝收益最大但异常语义最不可靠。

Method: KeepAD将token选择建模为高召回率、异常感知的路由问题。在浅层，通过局部2x2补丁邻域的覆盖保留选择与确定性异常救援相结合，降低丢弃细微缺陷的风险；在深层，利用冻结的正常与异常原型在图像自适应token预算下引导剪枝，激进移除低风险正常token同时保留局部异常证据；此外采用稠密到稀疏的自蒸馏来监督早期token路由，且不引入额外推理开销。

Result: 在六个工业基准和七个医学零样本异常检测基准上，KeepAD将token保留率降至20%以下，图像级与像素级AUROC的平均下降被限制在2.7个百分点以内，在最激进配置下相比最强CLIP基线实现了7.9倍加速。

Conclusion: KeepAD证明了在零样本视觉异常检测中通过缺陷保留式的token剪枝，可以在大幅降低计算开销的同时保持异常检测性能，解决了token剪枝在异常检测中的不对称风险问题。

Abstract: Zero-shot visual anomaly detection has achieved remarkable progress, with recent vision-only approaches further improving performance while simplifying the inference pipeline. However, existing methods typically perform dense computation over all images and spatial tokens, despite the fact that normal samples dominate real-world scenarios and anomalies usually occupy only small regions. Token pruning offers a promising solution, but introduces an asymmetric pruning risk in anomaly detection: retaining normal tokens mainly incurs redundant computation, whereas removing anomalous tokens may eliminate the only evidence for detection and localization. This risk is particularly severe in early layers, where pruning provides the greatest computational benefit but anomaly semantics remain unreliable. We propose KeepAD, a defect-preserving token pruning framework that formulates token selection as high-recall, anomaly-aware routing. In shallow layers, KeepAD combines coverage-preserving selection over local $2\times2$ patch neighborhoods with deterministic anomaly rescue to reduce the risk of discarding subtle defects. In deeper layers, frozen normal and abnormal prototypes guide pruning under an image-adaptive token budget, aggressively removing low-risk normal tokens while preserving local anomaly evidence. Dense-to-sparse self-distillation further supervises early token routing without introducing additional inference overhead. Experiments on six industrial and seven medical zero-shot anomaly detection benchmarks show that KeepAD reduces the token retention ratio to below $20\%$, while limiting the average degradation in image-level and pixel-level AUROC to within $2.7$ percentage points. At the most aggressive operating point, KeepAD achieves a $7.9\times$ speedup over the strongest CLIP-based baseline.

</details>


### [98] [Attention is Case-Sensitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03711)
*Maximilian Dillitzer,Tin Stribor Sohn,Jason J. Corso,Michael Auerbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大小写字母格式对大型语言模型（LLMs）内部注意力分配的调制作用，发现"大小写效应"（casing effect）可以作为一种零样本（zero-shot）的注意力操纵机制。该效应在13个模型（9个LLM和4个VLM）中普遍存在，能够将注意力集中于目标文本片段，但对下游任务准确率的影响并非单调——即注意力集中并不必然提升准确率，在交替大小写的高熵情境下甚至会降低性能。推理模型的"思考"阶段起到语义缓冲作用，可缓解排版敏感性。该效应在VLM中部分迁移，主要通过将注意力从图像宏观转移到文本提示、并在目标区域集中残余视觉注意力来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉感知中，大写字母在纯小写文本中是自然的显著性线索。作者希望探究大语言模型是否也具有类似的属性，即字母大小写是否能调节模型的内部注意力分配。这一问题的探索意义在于：如果存在该属性，则可将其作为一种无需模型访问或微调的零样本注意力引导机制，加深对预训练模型如何内化排版强调（typographic emphasis）的理解。

Method: 对13个模型（9个LLM和4个VLM）的注意力内部激活进行系统性实证分析。实验中，将目标信息以交替大小写或全大写格式嵌入小写上下文中，通过分析模型内部注意力分布来量化"大小写效应"对注意力集中的影响，并同时评估其对下游任务准确率的影响。研究还比较了推理模型与非推理模型的差异，以及VLM中跨模态注意力的重组模式。

Result: 大小写效应在文本任务中对所有非推理模型普遍成立，能有效将注意力集中在目标文本跨度上。然而注意力集中与任务准确率之间呈现分离现象：注意力增加并不必然提升准确率，且在交替大小写等高熵情境下会显著降低准确率。推理模型的思考阶段作为语义缓冲，可缓解排版敏感性。VLM中该效应部分迁移，表现为两个耦合轴上的跨模态注意力重组：主要从图像向文本提示的宏观脱离，以及残余视觉注意力在目标区域的次要集中。

Conclusion: 大小写效应是预训练Transformer的一种此前未被充分探索的潜在属性，而非一种规定性方法。该效应可作为一种零样本地注意力引导机制，无需模型访问或微调即可实现，为理解预训练如何内化排版强调提供了新的基础性认识。

Abstract: In human visual perception, uppercase lettering serves as a natural salience cue that captures attention within lowercase text. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical characterization study revealing that Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit an analogous property: letter casing modulates internal attention allocation. Through analysis across 13 models, nine LLMs and four Vision-Language Models (VLMs), with diverse tokenization schemes, we show that formatting target information in alternating or uppercase against a lowercase context concentrates attention on those textual spans. In text this effect is universal, holding across every evaluated non-reasoning model. We frame it as a previously under-explored latent property of pretrained transformers rather than a prescriptive method. Our investigation reveals a central attention-performance divergence: while this "casing effect" robustly shifts attention, its impact on downstream accuracy is non-trivial, increased concentration does not inherently improve task accuracy and, in high-entropy contexts like alternating case, can degrade it. We further identify a boundary condition: the deliberative "thinking" phase in reasoning models acts as a semantic buffer that mitigates typographic sensitivity in text. Extending the study to VLMs, we find the effect transfers partially: the same prompt-side casing reorganizes cross-modal attention along two coupled axes, predominantly a macroscopic disengagement from the image toward the text prompt, and secondarily a concentration of the residual visual attention on the target region. By isolating casing as a zero-shot mechanism for attention steering that requires no model access or fine-tuning, we provide a new foundational understanding of how pretraining internalizes typographic emphasis.

</details>


### [99] [MultiCompose: Multi-Concept Personalized Composition with Per-Subject Attribute Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03708)
*Ruirui Zhang,Zhengkai Zhao,Pan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiCompose框架和MSP-Bench基准，用于解决文本到图像扩散模型中多主体个性化生成时出现的主体身份退化和属性对齐失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型虽然支持从少量参考图像中个性化特定视觉概念，但当生成包含多个个性化主体且每个主体绑定用户指定属性（如衣物、配饰、手持物体）的图像时，缺乏有效的解决方案。同时激活多个概念检查点会产生重叠的交叉注意力响应，导致主体身份退化和属性错位。此外，缺少能联合评估这两种失败模式的基准。

Method: MultiCompose包含两个关键组件：(1) 语义保持正则化（semantic preservation regularization），在微调过程中维持属性绑定能力；(2) 两阶段推理流程，自动建立主体布局并通过空间排他掩码组合各概念的预测。同时提出MSP-Bench基准，通过双路径协议联合评估身份保真度（ID）、属性绑定准确率（BIND）和属性错位（MIS）。

Result: 实验表明MultiCompose在传统指标和MSP-Bench上都优于现有方法，并验证了该基准能够揭示传统指标所忽视的失败模式。

Conclusion: MultiCompose通过解耦概念个性化和多主体推理有效解决了多主体个性化生成中的身份退化和属性错位问题，MSP-Bench为多主体个性化设置提供了更全面的联合评估标准。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models enable personalization of specific visual concepts from a small number of reference images. However, generating a single image that contains multiple personalized subjects, each bound to user-specified attributes such as clothing, accessories, and held objects, remains largely unaddressed. Without explicit spatial constraints, concurrently activated concept checkpoints produce overlapping cross-attention responses, causing per-subject identity degradation and attribute misalignment. Moreover, no established benchmark jointly evaluates these two failure modes in the personalized multi-subject setting. We present MultiCompose, a composition framework that decouples per-concept personalization from multi-subject inference. A semantic preservation regularization maintains attribute binding capacity during fine-tuning, while a two-phase inference procedure automatically establishes subject layout and composes per-concept predictions through spatially exclusive masks. We further introduce MSP-Bench, a benchmark that jointly evaluates identity fidelity (ID), attribute binding accuracy (BIND), and attribute misalignment (MIS) through a dual-pathway protocol. Experiments show that MultiCompose outperforms existing methods on both conventional metrics and MSP-Bench, confirming the benchmark's ability to reveal failure modes that conventional metrics overlook. Code is available at https://github.com/I2-Multimedia-Lab/MultiCompose

</details>


### [100] [Towards Reliable and Reproducible Fetal Brain Biometry: A Deep Learning Approach Using MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03724)
*Francesca Maccarone,Marina Di Stefano,Giorgio Longari,Giulia Frigerio,Gloria Rizzato,Rocco Prudentino,Nivedita Agarwal,Tommaso Ciceri,Denis Peruzzo,Simone Melzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的全自动胎儿脑部生物测量框架，通过3D超分辨率重建的胎儿脑MRI，结合3D CNN回归解剖标志点并用几何优化进行精炼，在150个跨扫描仪和协议的胎脑MRI样本上（20-37周）实现了可靠、可重复且解剖可解释的五项关键生物测量，比现有自动化流水线具有更好或相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿脑部生物测量对评估脑发育、估算胎龄和检测异常至关重要，但临床实践中的人工测量耗时且易产生变异性。已有的自动化方法存在可重复性差、缺乏解剖可解释性标志点定位等问题，亟需一种可靠且可解释的自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出四步流水线：(1) 从3D超分辨率重建的胎儿脑MRI获取脑分割标签图；(2) 使用3D卷积神经网络回归地标坐标；(3) 进行测量特异性的几何优化以精炼地标位置；(4) 基于精炼的地标计算线性测量值。该框架联合估计线性测量及其对应的解剖标志点。

Result: 在两个公开胎儿MRI数据集（共150个体积，胎龄20-37周，跨不同扫描仪和协议）上评估了五项关键生物测量。与唯一可用的自动化流水线相比，该方法在大多数测量上达到相当或更高的精度，同时提供了定量和视觉上的标志点定位评估。

Conclusion: 该流水线提供了直接、高效、可解释且可扩展的胎儿脑部生物测量方案，具备了整合到临床应用工作流程中的可行性。

Abstract: Fetal brain biometry is essential for quantitative assessment of brain development, supporting gestational age estimation, developmental monitoring, and detection of abnormalities. In clinical practice, measurements are manually performed, making them time-consuming and prone to variability. While automated approaches have been proposed, reproducible methods remain limited, particularly those providing anatomically interpretable landmark localization. We present a fully automated deep learning-based framework for reliable and reproducible brain biometry from 3D super-resolution-reconstructed fetal brain MRI. The proposed four-step pipeline derives biometric parameters by jointly estimating linear measurements and their corresponding anatomical landmarks. A 3D convolutional neural network is trained to regress landmark coordinates from brain segmentation label maps, followed by measurement-specific geometric optimization to refine landmark positions and compute measurements. The pipeline is evaluated on two publicly available fetal MRI datasets comprising 150 volumes (gestational age range: 20-37 weeks) acquired across different scanners and protocols, assessing five key biometric measurements across varying acquisition settings and providing a comprehensive evaluation of both measurement accuracy and landmark localization using quantitative metrics and visual assessment. Compared with the only available automated pipeline, the proposed method achieves comparable or improved accuracy for most measurements. In conclusion, we introduce a straightforward pipeline for reliable biometry estimations, with efficiency, interpretability and scalability that support integration into clinical workflows.

</details>


### [101] [TDVR: Joint Text Disambiguation and Viewpoint Reasoning for Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03763)
*Qingxi Du,Junbo Wang,Yuke Li,Yining Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDVR，一个无需训练的推理框架，通过语义3D场景图构建、LLM驱动的文本消歧、链式思维推理生成结构化查询、视点推理与混淆物体判别，实现零样本3D视觉定位，在ScanRefer数据集上以Acc@0.25和Acc@0.5分别超越SOTA方法15.25%和14.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
