This pressure can help when utility rewards behavioral alignment, as in sustainable resource governance and subjective consensus. It does not, however, reliably improve accuracy. In objective QA debates, higher positivity can increase agreement even when correctness-conditioned agreement does not improve and may decline in some settings. Effects vary by model backbone, relation type, and topology; explicit neutrality is not equivalent to omitting relational framing. We argue that relational priors should not be a default add-on for LLM-MAS. Their safer use is diagnostic and task-specific: compare against a no-prior baseline, monitor correctness-conditioned metrics when truth matters, and omit the relational layer when validation does not justify it.

</details>


### [167] [MoEGen: Mixture-of-Experts for Instance-Adaptive LoRA Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03275)
*Yiming Zeng,Lei Lu,Zexin Li,Zhuochun Li,Shuoqiu Li,Shuyi Liao,Xidong Wu,Zeyu Zhang,Minmei Wang,Yu Zhao,Tingting Yu,Shangqian Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MoEGen提出了一种将MoE-based PEFT从"专家选择"转变为"专家条件参数生成"的框架，每个专家仅用一个小的可学习向量（专家编码）表示，并通过轻量级超网络为每个输入生成特定的低秩更新，从而解耦专家容量与适配器存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MoE的PEFT方法通常为每个专家存储完整的LoRA模块，导致适配器存储随专家数量线性增长，且只能适应固定的专家池。作者希望在不显式存储每个专家LoRA模块的情况下，实现实例级别的自适应。

Method: MoEGen将每个专家表示为一个小的可学习向量（专家编码），对输入进行路由分配到这些向量上，使用加权组合条件化一个轻量级超网络，该超网络生成输入特定的低秩更新。该方法将专家容量与适配器存储解耦。

Result: 在八个常识推理基准上，MoEGen在三个骨干网络上均持续优于强静态和MoE-based PEFT基线；在联合医疗和法律领域自适应中也表现强劲。

Conclusion: MoEGen通过专家编码和超网络生成输入特定参数的方式，有效解决了MoE-based PEFT中存储开销随专家数线性增长的问题，同时实现实例条件化自适应，在多个基准上取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) enables efficient adaptation of large language models, but existing MoE-based PEFT methods typically improve capacity by storing multiple full LoRA experts, causing adapter storage to grow linearly with the number of experts and restricting adaptation to a fixed expert pool. We ask whether MoE-based PEFT can produce instance-specific adaptations without explicitly storing a separate LoRA module for each expert. To address this gap, we propose MoEGen, an adaptation framework that shifts MoE-based PEFT from expert selection to expert-conditioned parameter generation. Instead of storing each expert as a full LoRA adapter, MoEGen represents each expert as a small learnable vector, termed an expert code. It routes each input over these vectors and uses their weighted combination to condition a lightweight hypernetwork that generates input-specific low-rank updates. This design decouples expert capacity from adapter storage while enabling instance-conditioned adaptation. Experiments on eight commonsense reasoning benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong static and MoE-based PEFT baselines across three backbones. MoEGen also performs strongly in joint medical and legal-domain adaptation.

</details>


### [168] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Testing the Predictive Validity of Commonsense Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03340)
*Ine Gevers,Walter Daelemans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了常识基准（commonsense benchmarks）在预测LLM下游任务表现方面的有效性。研究覆盖6个模型家族共23个模型，使用4个常见常识基准及其变体、3个非常识对照组和8个需要隐含社会、语用、时间或物理推理的下游任务，通过排序比较、受控相关性和留一家族交叉验证来检验常识基准的标准效度。结果表明修订版基准基本保留原始模型排序，且未提升下游预测力；常识基准仅对少数下游任务子集具有跨家族预测效度，整体呈现任务依赖性而非广泛的常识能力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测LLM在真实任务中的能力至关重要，但常识基准性能在多大程度上能预测下游任务表现仍不明确，作者希望建立广泛使用的常识基准的实际可用性。

Method: 对6个模型家族共23个模型，在4个常识基准、4个修订变体、3个非常识对照组和8个下游任务上进行评估；通过模型排名比较、受控相关性分析和留一家族交叉验证来评估常识基准的标准效度。

Result: 修订版基准基本保持了原始模型排名且不提升下游预测能力；常识基准仅在狭窄的下游任务子集上表现出跨家族预测效度，其余方面的改进有限且具有指标特异性。

Conclusion: 标准化常识基准提供的是任务依赖性的证据，而非对下游常识能力的广泛预测证据。

Abstract: Predicting LLM's capabilities on real-world tasks is essential, yet the extent to which performance on commonsense benchmarks predicts downstream performance remains underspecified. To establish the practical usability of widely adopted commonsense benchmarks, we evaluate 23 models from six families on four established commonsense benchmarks, four reworked variants, three non-commonsense controls, and eight downstream tasks requiring implicit social, pragmatic, temporal, or physical reasoning. We compare model rankings, compute controlled correlations, and use leave-one-family-out cross-validation to assess the criterion validity of commonsense benchmarks. Our results show that revised benchmarks largely preserve original model rankings and do not improve downstream predictive power. Commonsense benchmarks show consistent cross-family predictive validity for only a narrow subset of downstream tasks, with smaller or metric-specific gains elsewhere. Overall, standardized commonsense benchmarks provide task-dependent rather than broad evidence of downstream commonsense competence.

</details>


### [169] [ArtECulture: Benchmarking Culture-Conditioned Visual Emotion Understanding in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03358)
*Xiaolin Chen,Xuemeng Song,Wenhao Shi,Xianjing Han,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出文化条件化视觉情绪理解任务及ArtECulture基准（含6,792件艺术品，覆盖英、中、阿三种文化），评估16个多模态大模型发现最佳准确率低于50%，并提出了基于检索增强的文化情绪知识库框架来注入显式文化知识，无需额外训练即可提升情绪预测与解释生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉情绪理解方法忽略文化差异对情绪感知的影响；已有基准存在个体标注不一致导致难以获得多数支持的群体级情绪标签，以及文化覆盖不均衡的问题。

Method: 1) 构建ArtECulture基准（6,792幅艺术品，含英/中/阿三种文化特定情绪标签与解释，兼顾西方与非西方内容）；2) 提出检索增强的文化条件化情绪理解框架，利用基于概念的文化情绪知识库向MLLM注入显式文化知识，无需额外训练。

Result: 在零样本设置下评估16个开源与闭源多模态大语言模型，任务仍具挑战性，最佳模型准确率低于50%；所提检索增强框架在文化对齐的情绪预测与基于依据的解释生成上均有提升。

Conclusion: 文化条件化视觉情绪理解是尚未被充分解决的任务，检索增强的知识注入方法有效改善MLLM在该任务上的表现，基准与代码将公开发布以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Existing visual emotion understanding methods typically ignore cultural variations in emotional perception. We introduce culture-conditioned visual emotion understanding, a task that predicts the culture-specific emotional perception of a given image and explains the underlying rationale. Although related benchmarks exist, they are limited by inconsistent individual annotations, which hinder the derivation of majority-supported culture-level emotion labels, and imbalanced cultural coverage. Thus, we present ArtECulture, a benchmark containing 6,792 artworks with culture-specific emotion labels and explanations across English, Chinese, and Arabic cultures, with balanced Western and non-Western content. Evaluations of 16 open- and closed-source Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) under a zero-shot setting reveal that the task remains challenging, with the best model achieving below 50\% accuracy. To address this limitation, we introduce a retrieval-augmented culture-conditioned emotion understanding framework, which leverages a concept-based cultural emotion knowledge base to inject explicit cultural knowledge into MLLMs without additional training. The framework improves both culturally aligned emotion prediction and grounded explanation generation. Our benchmark and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [170] [FACTWASH: Catching AI Rewrites That Wash Hearsay into Fact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03372)
*Alex Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了"事实洗白"(factwashing)概念，指AI系统在重写信息时保留主张却抹去了可核查性线索（来源、确定度、时效）。作者发布了factwash，这是一个开源的写入时门控工具，用确定性规则和命名标志来捕获该失败，并系统分析了"何时用廉价检查、何时需要大模型"的边界：封闭的表面线索清单用词表即可达到0.91 F1，而开放式的转述与归因线索需借助单问题LLM证人恢复召回。在105,596句标注语料上，对话类传闻是最主要的失败场景（55%），而商业邮件仅占7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在重写信息（如对话变记忆、文档变答案）时，常常保留主张内容却抹去了使其可核查的属性（谁说的、多确定、何时有效），这种被忽视的失败模式缺乏系统检测工具。作者旨在提出该失败概念并回答一个实用问题：廉价的确定性检查何时足够、何时必须依赖语义模型。

Method: 发布factwash，一个开源写入时门控器，用确定性规则、命名标志和证据（而非LLM评审）来捕获事实洗白。作者区分两类线索：封闭的"表面线索清单"（如显式否定词）可用词表枚举并跨域迁移；开放实现（如转述和归因）则词表召回饱和，改用"单问题LLM证人"恢复识别。实验在105,596句独立标注语料上测量线索检测，并结合盲标注的记忆写入语料定位失败分布。

Result: 显式否定线索用词表达到0.91 F1（未调参文本上可迁移）；转述与归因线索的词表召回仅约一半（plateau near half recall），而单问题LLM证人分别提升+17和+15点线索检测召回（精度持平）。部署时LLM证人只降低判定，从而购买精度而非覆盖。在105,596句上，55%的坏写入出现在对话传闻场景，商业邮件仅7%（p<0.001）。对未修改的mem0 2.0.7，门控在8条转述传闻写入中成功标记5条。

Conclusion: 检测器的选择不是首要问题——部署前应先判断所处理内容是否会发生事实洗白；对封闭线索用简单词表即可，对开放式线索则需LLM证人补充召回；最关键的失败场景是对话中的传闻式重写。

Abstract: AI systems rewrite information constantly: conversations become stored memories, documents become answers. The rewrite can keep a claim while washing away what made it checkable, who said it, how sure they were, when it held. We call that failure factwashing, and release factwash, an open-source write-time gate that catches it deterministically, with named flags and evidence rather than an LLM judge. Building it answers a practical question: when does a cheap check suffice, and when do you need a model? What decides is whether the property has a bounded surface-cue inventory. Explicit negation cues are close to enumerable, so a word list finishes and transfers, reaching 0.91 F1 on untuned text. Hedging and attribution have open-ended realizations, so vocabulary plateaus near half recall, and a one-question LLM witness recovers +17 and +15 points of cue-detection recall at equal precision. Deployed, that witness may only lower a verdict, so it buys precision rather than coverage. We measure cue detection on 105,596 independently annotated sentences. A blind-labelled corpus of memory writes then locates the failure: 55% of bad writes in conversational hearsay, 7% in business email (p < 0.001), so the first deployment question is not which detector to use but whether the failure occurs at all. On unmodified mem0 2.0.7, the gate flags 5 of 8 hedged-hearsay writes.

</details>


### [171] [Don't Let Me Ask for It: LLMs Show Deficiencies in Active Multi-Turn Information Acquisition for Abductive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03388)
*Shahrukh Mohiuddin,Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sherzod Hakimov,David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入"外星人绑架游戏"(Alien Abduction game)作为交互式探针，研究大语言模型在溯因推理中的行为特征，包括证据获取、假设更新和停止决策。研究发现：一次性提供证据比多轮分布证据成功率更高；多轮设置下模型要么过早承诺、要么耗尽预算不收敛；由预言机提供示例比模型自主选择查询成功率更高，但模型最终假设与自选证据更一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要评估LLM能否正确解决溯因推理任务，但对其如何获取证据、如何更新假设以及何时决定停止等过程性行为知之甚少。作者旨在通过交互式探针揭示这些未被充分研究的认知行为。

Method: 设计"外星人绑架游戏"交互式探针，在多种交互模式下测试LLM的溯因推理行为。交互模式在以下维度上变化：证据是预先提供还是跨回合分布；查询由模型自主选择还是由预言机提供示例。通过比较不同模式下的成功率、证据使用情况和假设一致性来分析模型行为。

Result: 1) 预先提供证据比跨回合分布证据的成功率更高；2) 多轮设置中，部分模型在未充分利用可用证据时就过早提交假设，而另一些模型耗尽回合预算仍不收敛；3) 预言机提供示例时成功率高于模型自主选择查询，但模型在自主选择查询时最终假设与所选证据更一致。

Conclusion: LLM可能倾向于形成与自选证据拟合的假设，而没有充分区分备选假设；模型在验证、修正假设以及判断何时停止方面存在困难，这为改进LLM的溯因推理能力提供了方向。

Abstract: Abductive reasoning requires forming hypotheses that explain observed evidence and revising them as new evidence becomes available. While large language models (LLMs) are often evaluated on whether they solve abductive reasoning tasks correctly, less is known about how they acquire evidence, update their hypotheses, and decide when to stop. We introduce Alien Abduction game, an interactive probe for studying these behaviours under different interaction modes. The modes vary in whether evidence is provided upfront or across turns, and whether queries are selected by the model or examples are provided by the oracle. Across models, providing evidence upfront leads to higher success rates than distributing it across turns. In multi-turn settings, some models commit before using the available evidence, while others exhaust the turn budget without converging. Models also achieve higher success rates when examples are provided by the oracle than when they select their own queries, although their final hypotheses are more consistent with the evidence they selected. These findings suggest that models may form hypotheses that fit self-selected evidence without sufficiently distinguishing them from alternatives, and may struggle to validate and refine their hypotheses or determine when to stop.

</details>


### [172] [DUD: Decoupled Update Dynamics for Reliable Uncertainty Quantification in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03411)
*Yixin Bu,Runze Xia,Guanyun Zou,Yupeng Ji,Haodong Liu,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DUD框架，通过噪声注入因果干预解耦FFN（参数记忆）和Attention（上下文处理）的更新动态，构建双流动态画像以更精确地量化LLM的不确定性，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于概率的不确定性度量无法捕捉LLM的真实认知状态，而现有机制性方法通常聚合残差流更新，混淆了参数记忆（FFN）与上下文处理（Attention）的不同角色，掩盖了记忆-上下文错位等细粒度机制冲突——这些恰恰是不确定性的关键指标。

Method: 提出Decoupled Update Dynamics (DUD)框架：(1) 通过噪声诱导的因果干预显式解耦FFN和Attention的贡献；(2) 分别量化两个模块的独立恢复能力；(3) 构建双流动态画像以捕捉模型内部脆弱性，作为不确定性的代理指标。

Result: 大量实验表明，DUD在不确定性估计和校准上显著优于最先进的基线方法，并展现出卓越的跨数据集泛化能力，验证了解耦动态作为模型忠实性稳健代理的有效性。

Conclusion: 解耦的模块级更新动态比聚合的残差流分析能更准确地反映LLM的不确定性，DUD为可靠的不确定性量化提供了新的机制性视角。

Abstract: Accurate Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) is critical for reliable deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet traditional probability-based metrics often fail to capture the model's true epistemic state. While recent mechanistic approaches leverage hidden state dynamics, they typically aggregate residual stream updates, conflating the distinct roles of parametric memory (Feed-Forward Networks) and contextual processing (Attention). We argue that this aggregation obscures fine-grained mechanistic conflicts, such as memory-context misalignment, that are fundamental indicators of uncertainty. To address this, we introduce \textbf{D}ecoupled \textbf{U}pdate \textbf{D}ynamics \textbf{(DUD)}, a framework that explicitly decouples FFN and Attention contributions via noise-induced causal interventions. By quantifying the independent restoration capabilities of each module, we construct a dual-stream dynamic profile that captures the model's internal fragility. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DUD significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both uncertainty estimation and calibration, while exhibiting superior cross-dataset generalization, validating decoupled dynamics as a robust proxy for model faithfulness.

</details>


### [173] [Dynamically Allocating Evaluation Effort for Model Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03437)
*Vilém Zouhar,Julia Kreutzer,Alon Lavie,Tom Kocmi,Matt Post,Ondřej Bojar,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将多模型人工评估形式化为多臂老虎机中的最优臂识别问题（带相关臂），通过基于中间模型排名的自适应采样，将标注预算集中在最具竞争力的模型上，从而加速评估、降低成本并提升对顶尖模型的区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工评估虽被视为NLP任务的黄金标准，但成本高昂且可扩展性差。传统的评估协议会在整个基准上穷举评估所有模型，虽然安全但效率低下。本文旨在解决如何以更高效的方式识别出表现最佳的模型。

Method: 将多模型人工评估形式化为带相关臂的多臂老虎机最优臂识别问题。拉动手臂对应人工评估一个模型；根据已获得样本上的中间模型排名进行自适应采样，将有限的标注预算集中在最有竞争力的模型子集上；并证明所提算法的最优性。

Result: 所提算法被证明具有最优性，且能改善对顶尖模型之间的区分能力，使评估更快、更便宜，并与大规模竞赛评估目标更加契合。

Conclusion: 通过自适应采样策略，本文方法能够在保持评估质量的同时大幅提升人工评估的效率，为大规模多模型评估场景提供了一种理论上有保证且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: While human evaluation is the gold standard in many NLP tasks, it suffers from prohibitive costs and poor scalability. When identifying top-performing models, typical evaluation protocols waste effort by exhaustively evaluating all models on the entire benchmark, a safe but inefficient approach. In this work, we formalize multi-model human evaluation as a best-arm identification problem in a multi-armed bandit setup with correlated arms, where pulling an arm corresponds to human-evaluating a model. By sampling adaptively based on the intermediate model rankings obtained on the samples so far, we can focus the annotation budget on the most competitive models. We prove the optimality of the proposed algorithms and show that it improves discrimination between top-performing models. This makes evaluations faster, cheaper and more aligned with large-scale competition evaluation goals.

</details>


### [174] [Predicting Multilingual Classification and Translation Performance of LLMs with Cross-Lingual Alignment $\unicode{x2013}$ Is English Enough?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03446)
*Adnan Al Ali,Kathy Hämmerl,Jindřich Libovický,Alexander Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对27种跨语言对齐（CLA）评分变体进行了比较分析，首次将其应用于生成任务（机器翻译）而不仅是分类任务，并提出了一种基于PMI的翻译评估指标来支持跨目标语言的相关系数计算。研究发现英语作为中枢语言的CLA分数对翻译质量的预测能力与源-目标语言CLA相当甚至更好，为LLM使用英语作为内部枢轴语言提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于多语言LLM跨语言对齐的研究几乎完全聚焦于分类任务，但LLM被广泛用于生成任务（如机器翻译）。因此需要探究CLA分数是否也能预测翻译性能，同时需要解决现有CLA变体和嵌入提取方法缺乏系统比较的问题。

Method: 对27种CLA分数变体进行比较分析，评估其在不同任务（三个分类任务及机器翻译）中预测下游性能的能力。针对跨目标语言相关性的计算需求，提出了一种基于PMI（点互信息）的翻译评估指标，该指标对目标语言依赖性较弱且与chrF高度相关。

Result: 研究发现CLA与英语的分数对翻译质量的预测能力与源-目标语言CLA相当甚至更好。新提出的PMI翻译指标与chrF强相关，且降低了对目标语言的依赖，使得跨语言相关性的计算成为可能。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM使用英语作为内部枢轴语言提供了新证据，表明英语对齐的CLA分数在生成任务中同样具有预测力，扩展了CLA分数在评估多语言模型时的适用场景。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models (LLMs) have been shown to perform better on non-English classification tasks when the representations of the given language are more aligned to English within the model. Several cross-lingual alignment (CLA) scores have been proposed for use with LLMs, along with multiple approaches for extracting embeddings from the models. We provide a comparative analysis of 27 CLA score variants, examining how they differ and how well each predicts downstream performance across three tasks. Crucially, while LLMs are widely used for generative tasks such as machine translation, prior work has focused almost exclusively on classification. We therefore investigate whether CLA scores are similarly predictive of translation performance. To enable computing correlations across target languages, we propose a PMI-based translation metric, which is less dependent on the target language and correlates strongly with chrF. We find that CLA with English predicts translation quality comparably to or better than source-target CLA, providing new evidence that LLMs use English as an internal pivot language.

</details>


### [175] [Probing Character-level Transformers for the Spanish L-shaped Morphome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03452)
*Akhilesh Kakolu Ramarao,Kevin Tang,Wiebke Petersen,Dinah Baer-Henney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer模型如何编码西班牙语L形形态（不规则词干交替模式）。通过五种架构、各12个训练模型，在词素不相交交叉验证下进行探测，发现模型编码的是L形类别本身而非仅其表面交替形式——该编码可在所有表面基线上解码，在全部同词干实例中仍存在，且跨实例泛化。编码定位于中间解码器词干末尾辅音位置。关键发现：模型将形态存储为逐项词汇抽象，个体学习差异远大于架构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明字符级Transformer能复现西班牙语L形形态模式，但该证据只说明模型能产生该模式，未揭示模型内部真正表征了什么。作者旨在探究模型学习这种不可预测的不规则形态模式时，究竟是编码了抽象的类别本身，还是仅仅记忆了可见的表面交替。

Method: 采用探测（probing）方法，对五种架构、每种12个训练模型，在词素不相交的交叉验证设置下进行系统分析。设计了三类对比：控制组（controls）、表面基线（surface baselines）、以及探针训练与测试设置（包括交替实例训练、非交替实例分类）。通过特征归因定位编码的神经解剖位置，并结合架构间与个体间差异分析。

Result: 模型确实编码了L形类别本身而非仅表面交替：该编码高于所有表面基线可解码；即使在每个形式均显示相同词干的实例中依然存在；用交替实例训练的探针能对非交替实例分类。编码定位于中间解码器层词干末尾辅音位置，在读到交替体之前。更重要的是，逐项特异性显著：不同模型学会了哪些动词对表征的影响远大于不同架构的影响。

Conclusion: 模型将L形形态存储为逐项（item-specific）的词汇抽象，足以复现该模式，但无法像人类那样将其概括化推广。这揭示了神经网络学习不规则形态的机制本质——以词汇条目级抽象实现，而非规则化的类别泛化。

Abstract: When a transformer learns an irregular morphological pattern, what has it learned? Our test case is the Spanish \emph{L-shaped morphome}, a complex irregular pattern in which the verb's stem alternates in exactly the first-person singular indicative and all subjunctive forms, and whose membership no phonological, semantic, or syntactic feature predicts. Prior studies have shown that character-level transformers can reproduce this pattern, but that evidence describes what models produce, not what they represent. Probing five architectures, twelve trained models each, under lemma-disjoint cross-validation with controls and surface baselines, we show that the models encode the L-shaped class itself, not just its visible alternations. It is decodable above every surface baseline, survives instances in which every form shows the same stem, and probes trained on alternating instances still classify non-alternating ones. The encoding is localized where the stem choice is made, at the stem-final consonant position of the middle decoder, before the alternant is read. And it is item-specific: which verbs a model learned matters far more than which architecture it is. The models store the morphome as an item-specific lexical abstraction, sufficient to reproduce the pattern but not to generalize it as humans do.

</details>


### [176] [Efficient Multilingual Neural Machine Translation via Corpus-Driven Vocabulary Pruning: An English-Arabic Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03480)
*Ahmed Amine Aliane,Nasredine Semmar,Hassina Aliane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合词汇表剪枝与针对性微调方案的通用优化框架，用于解决多语言神经机器翻译（MNMT）模型因超大词表和嵌入层导致的内存与计算开销问题。在M2M100、NLLB-200和mBART-50三个模型上、英阿语言对上，该方法将词表从12.8万以上缩减至约1万词元，实现60%内存节省且性能无损，剪枝微调后的M2M100在BLEU上与双语基线相当（42.04 vs 44.59），在COMET指标上显著更优（0.8730 vs 0.7911）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏）虽能减少参数冗余，但均保留了原始词汇表的结构，未能从根本上解决MNMT模型因超大词表和嵌入层带来的内存与计算效率低下问题。

Method: 提出一个通用优化框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 词汇表剪枝方法，用于压缩超大词表；(2) 针对性的微调协议（fine-tuning protocol），在剪枝后对模型进行适配训练以恢复或提升性能。

Result: 在M2M100、NLLB-200、mBART-50三个模型上，词表从128,000以上缩减至约10,000词元，实现60%内存节省且性能无损失；优化后的多语言模型可匹配或超越专用双语基线的性能，M2M100在BLEU（42.04 vs 44.59）上接近OPUS-MTen-ar双语模型，在COMET（0.8730 vs 0.7911）上显著领先。

Conclusion: 词汇表剪枝结合微调的优化框架能够有效解决MNMT模型词表冗余问题，在大幅节省内存的同时保持甚至提升翻译质量，优化后的多语言模型在语义充分性和流畅度上可超越专用双语模型。

Abstract: The adoption of large pre-trained multilingual models for neural machine translation (MNMT) faces a major challenge: excessive memory and computational consumption due to overly large vocabularies and embedding layers. Although existing compression methods like pruning, quantization and knowledge distillation reduce parameter redundancy, they mainly preserve the structure of the original vocabulary, thereby leaving a major source of inefficiency unresolved. We propose in this paper a general optimization framework that combines a vocabulary pruning method with a targeted fine-tuning protocol for MNMT models. We evaluate the proposed framework using three models (M2M100, NLLB-200, mBART-50) on the English-Arabic language pair. Our approach reduces the vocabulary size from over 128,000 to approximately 10,000 tokens, enabling a 60% memory saving without any loss in performance. Results show that optimized multilingual models can match or exceed the performance of dedicated bilingual baselines. In particular, the pruned and fine-tuned M2M100 model achieves a competitive BLEU score of 42.04 (against 44.59 for the OPUS-MTen- ar bilingual model) while it significantly outperforms it on the COMET metric (0.8730 vs 0.7911) revealing superior semantic adequacy and fluency.

</details>


### [177] [Beyond Initialization Loss: A Systematic Study of Token Embedding Initialization Strategies for LLM Vocabulary Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03494)
*Raviraj Joshi,Utkarsh Vaidya,Sanjay Singh Chauhan,Niranjan Wartikar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了20多种词汇扩展初始化策略，用于将预训练LLM适配到新语言（印地语）。研究发现子词组合方法优于词汇平均和外部/学习式初始化方法，其中非对称变体效果最佳。最优配置（均匀子词平均+印地语特定范数校准用于输入，字符长度加权子词平均用于输出头）相比基线可将CPT步骤减少6倍以上。此外，初始损失和Init BPB不能可靠预测下游收敛，而轻量级CPT（50步）是选择最优初始化策略的经济有效信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇扩展是使预训练LLM适配新语言的高效途径，但新加入token嵌入的初始化方式会显著影响继续预训练（CPT）的效率。已有研究中缺乏对多种初始化策略的系统性比较，难以确定哪种策略最优。

Method: 在Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B模型上对印地语词汇扩展进行了超过20种初始化策略的系统对比，涵盖：词汇平均基线、外部/学习式初始化（FOCUS、top-k语义检索、残差MLP映射）、子词组合、范数校准以及输入输出非对称变体，并通过轻量级CPT和多阶段评估验证策略效果。

Result: 子词组合方法优于词汇平均和外部/学习式初始化方法。非对称变体达到最低的早期验证损失。最优配置（输入用均匀子词平均+印地语特定范数校准，输出用字符长度加权子词平均）相对Mean-all基线实现6倍以上的CPT步骤缩减，500步即超过基线3500步的MILU-Hindi准确率。初始损失和Init BPB不能可靠预测下游收敛，而50步轻量CPT即可提供可靠的选择信号。

Conclusion: 词汇扩展初始化中，子词组合特别是非对称输入/输出策略是最优选择；评估初始化策略时应使用轻量级CPT信号而非仅依赖初始指标，这为多语言LLM适配提供了实用的方法学指导。

Abstract: Vocabulary extension is an efficient way to adapt pretrained large language models (LLMs) to new languages, but the initialization of newly added token embeddings can strongly affect continued pre-training (CPT) efficiency. We present a systematic study of more than 20 initialization strategies for Hindi vocabulary extension in Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B. Our comparison spans vocabulary-averaging baselines; external and learned initialization methods, including FOCUS, top-k semantic retrieval, and residual MLP mappings; subword composition; norm calibration; and input-output asymmetry. We find that subword composition methods outperform both vocabulary averaging and external/learned initialization approaches. Within subword composition, asymmetric variants achieve the lowest observed early validation loss and reveal distinct preferences for input and output embedding initialization. The best observed configuration initializes the input embedding matrix with uniform subword averaging and Hindi-specific norm calibration, and the output language modeling head with character-length-weighted subword averaging. Relative to the standard Mean-all baseline, this full initialization pipeline reaches comparable validation loss with over a 6x reduction in CPT steps and exceeds the baseline's 3,500-step MILU-Hindi accuracy after only 500 steps. Finally, we show that initialization loss and initialization bits-per-byte (Init BPB) are unreliable predictors of downstream convergence, whereas lightweight CPT, as few as 50 steps, provides a cost-effective and reliable signal for selecting the best initialization strategy.

</details>


### [178] [ConlangBench: Exploring Language Knowledge and Learning in LLMs through Diverse Constructed Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03505)
*Jinhong Jeong,Seungyeop Yi,Sangah Lee,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ConlangBench，首个大规模人工语言（conlang）基准数据集，包含21种现存人工语言、超过2100万对conlang-英语平行句对（其中43万对来自20种非世界语言）及32.1万个词汇条目。双向翻译实验表明模型在先验式人工语言上表现更好，且模型能够学习有足够平行语料的8种人工语言，学习曲线因语言构造方式而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工语言（conlang）具有丰富的语言创造性，是研究大型语言模型（LLM）语言学习的潜在测试平台，但现有的人工语言在LLM研究中尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个系统化基准来评估和训练LLM在人工语言上的表现。

Method: 作者构建了ConlangBench基准，收集了21种现存人工语言的超过2100万条conlang-英语平行句对（包括20种非世界语的43万条数据）和32.1万个词汇条目。随后进行了双向翻译实验，并进一步在ConlangBench上对模型进行训练，观察其学习表现。

Result: 实验发现：1）模型在先验式（a posteriori）人工语言上表现更好，因为这类语言的词汇源自自然语言；2）模型能够学习全部8种具有足够平行语料的人工语言；3）模型的学习曲线因人工语言的构造方式不同而各异。

Conclusion: 人工语言为研究LLM如何习得低资源语言提供了独特的测试平台，ConlangBench可作为该领域研究的基准工具。

Abstract: Constructed languages (conlangs) are intentionally created human languages with a rich tradition of linguistic creativity. Despite their potential for studying language learning in large language models (LLMs), existing conlangs remain largely underexplored in LLM research. We present ConlangBench, the first large-scale benchmark for evaluating and training LLMs on 21 existing conlangs. We collect over 21M conlang-English parallel sentence pairs (including 430K pairs across the 20 non-Esperanto conlangs) and 321K vocabulary entries. In bidirectional translation experiments, we find that models perform better on a posteriori conlangs, whose vocabularies are derived from natural languages, reflecting the design characteristics of conlangs. Training on ConlangBench also shows that models can learn all eight conlangs for which sufficient parallel corpora are available, while their learning curves vary depending on how the conlangs were created. Our findings suggest that conlangs provide a unique testbed for investigating how LLMs acquire low-resource languages.

</details>


### [179] [ChronoLens: Measuring Language Change Across Time, Languages, and Linguistic Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03507)
*Gagan Bhatia,Julian Schlenker,Simone Paolo Ponzetto,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoLens框架，利用冻结多语言语言模型、特征对齐的crosscoders和事后语言干预，将44.98百万文档、约172亿词元的五个议会传统（1803-2026年）统一映射到单一分析空间，研究词形、句法、语义和语用在历时语言演变中的变化幅度与方向，发现各语言层面变化幅度相当，而语言间在变化时机、距离和方向上差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史语言变化涉及词形、句法、语义和语用等多个层面，但计算研究通常使用不相容的表示方法，无法判断这些层面是否在不同语言中协同演变，缺乏统一的跨语言跨层面分析框架。

Method: 提出ChronoLens框架，结合三部分：冻结的多语言语言模型（提供稳定的语义表示）、特征对齐的crosscoders（将表示分解为稀疏、可解释的特征）、事后语言干预（将不同语言层面映射到同一语义空间）。应用于5个议会传统、44.98M文档、~17.2B token的语料（1803-2026年），在统一空间中测量各语言层面变化的幅度和方向。

Result: 稀疏表示与语言统计的一致性远高于稠密嵌入和池化稀疏自编码器（ρ=0.72 vs 0.29和0.28）；同一语言内词形、句法、语义、语用的变化幅度总体相当，而不同语言在变化时机、距离和方向上差异显著。

Conclusion: 历史语言变化是一个结构化的多维过程：相似的变化幅度可能隐藏不同的变化轨迹，有意义的跨语言比较必须同时度量变化的距离和方向。

Abstract: Historical language change affects morphology, syntax, semantics, and pragmatics, yet computational studies typically examine these levels with incompatible representations and therefore cannot determine whether they evolve together across languages. We address this problem by asking how the magnitude and direction of change vary across linguistic levels, languages, and historical periods within a single analytical space. We introduce ChronoLens, a framework that combines frozen multilingual language models, feature-aligned crosscoders, and post-hoc linguistic interventions, and apply it to 44.98 million documents and approximately 17.2 billion tokens from five parliamentary traditions spanning 1803--2026. The resulting sparse representations agree substantially more strongly with linguistic statistics than dense embeddings or a pooled sparse autoencoder ($ρ=0.72$ versus $0.29$ and $0.28$), and reveal that morphology, syntax, semantics, and pragmatics generally change by comparable amounts within a language, while languages differ markedly in when, how far, and in which direction they change. These findings show that historical language change is a structured, multidimensional process: similar magnitudes can conceal different trajectories, and meaningful cross-linguistic comparison requires measuring both distance and direction.

</details>


### [180] [Consensus Measures for Unstructured Biomedical Text Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03529)
*Pascal Wullschleger,Christian Kreis,Martin A. Walter,Marc Pouly,Jennifer Foster*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究生物医学文献开放标签注释中的软注释者间可靠性（soft inter-rater reliability），比较了多种语义等价性度量方法，提出自然语言推理（NLI）为基础的度量作为合理折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学文献被越来越多地用于挖掘原始写作目的之外的知识。由于目标概念无法预先确定，注释者倾向于使用开放式标签，而这种开放标签之间的共识度很难量化，因此需要研究软注释者间可靠性的度量方法。

Method: 通过合成实验比较多种语义等价性度量方法对软可靠性的量化效果，包括嵌入方法（可扩展但难以区分相似概念）、大型语言模型（有前景但存在偶然一致性估计的可扩展性限制），以及自然语言推理（NLI）为基础的度量方法。

Result: 软可靠性可以通过多种语义等价性度量进行量化；度量方法的选择影响估计的失败模式。嵌入方法可扩展但难以区分相似但不同的概念；大型语言模型有前景但受限于可扩展性；NLI-based度量被建议作为合理折中。

Conclusion: 建议采用基于自然语言推理的度量方法作为平衡准确性与可扩展性的折中方案，用于量化生物医学开放标签注释的软注释者间可靠性。

Abstract: Biomedical literature is increasingly mined for knowledge beyond the questions it was written to answer. Because the target concepts are not known in advance, annotators prefer open-ended labels, whose agreement is hard to quantify. We study soft inter-rater reliability for annotators providing unstructured texts for biomedical annotation tasks. Synthetic experiments show that soft reliability can be quantified using a variety of semantic equivalence measures, and that the choice of measure affects failure modes of the estimation. Embeddings are scalable, but limited when differentiating similar but distinct concepts. Large language models are promising, but limited by scalability for estimating agreement by chance. Finally, we suggest measures based on natural language inference as a sensible compromise.

</details>


### [181] [Cross-Lingual Bias in Large Language Models: A Comparative Analysis of English and Swahili](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03532)
*Ruolei Zhang,Teddy Njuguna,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过4,900对对称的英语-斯瓦希里语提示词对，对两个大语言模型在九个人口统计学偏见维度上进行跨语言偏见评估，发现偏见在跨语言中"转化而非迁移"：刻板印象率最多偏移12个百分点，Gemini在斯瓦希里语中中性情绪率翻倍，GPT-5.2在英语中拒绝169个提示而斯瓦希里语中零拒绝，超过55%的提示对产生语义不同的结果，表明仅英语偏见审计不足以覆盖多语言部署场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多语言环境中越来越广泛地部署，但安全对齐和偏见评估仍然以英语为中心。作者希望探究社会偏见是否能在不同语言之间普遍化，以验证仅基于英语的偏见审计是否足以保障多语言部署的安全性。

Method: 向GPT-5.2和Gemini 2.5 Flash提交4,900对对称的英语-斯瓦希里语提示词对，覆盖九个人口统计学偏见轴（共19,600个生成结果），从刻板印象流行率、情感倾向、拒绝行为和跨语言语义相似性四个维度进行评估。

Result: 发现偏见在跨语言中"转化而非迁移"：特定轴上的刻板印象率偏移最多达12个百分点；Gemini在斯瓦希里语中中性情感率翻倍；GPT-5.2在英语中拒绝169个提示而在斯瓦希里语中零拒绝，表明拒绝行为在行为层面锚定于英语表面形式；超过55%的提示对在两个模型上产生语义不相似的生成结果。

Conclusion: 英语单一的偏见审计无法为多语言部署提供充分覆盖，模型在不同语言下会表现出不同的偏见模式，需要针对多语言场景设计相应的评估与对齐策略。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multilingual contexts, yet safety alignment and bias evaluation remain overwhelmingly English-centric. We investigate whether social biases generalise across languages by submitting 4,900 symmetric English--Swahili prompt pairs to GPT-5.2 and Gemini 2.5 Flash across nine demographic bias axes, yielding 19,600 completions evaluated for stereotype prevalence, sentiment, refusal behaviour, and cross-lingual semantic similarity. Our findings show that bias transforms rather than transfers: stereotype rates shifted by up to 12 percentage points on specific axes, Gemini's neutral-sentiment rate doubled in Swahili, and GPT-5.2 refused 169 prompts in English and zero in Swahili, consistent with refusal behaviour anchored to English-language surface forms at the behavioural level. Over 55% of prompt pairs produced semantically dissimilar completions across both models. These reinforce the idea that English-only bias audits do not produce adequate coverage for multilingual deployment.

</details>


### [182] [Hi-TTRL: Regulating Consensus with Hints for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03545)
*Kunbin Xu,Xingzuo Li,Xuefeng Bai,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hi-TTRL，一种测试时强化学习框架，通过在采样过程中使用提示（hints）来调节rollout群体的共识强度（consensus strength），以解决标准TTRL中奖励信号对共识强度过度敏感的问题。Hi-TTRL先估计部分rollout群体的共识强度，当超出目标区间时调用基于马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）的提示采样器，通过功率变换的prefix分布生成提示，从而将共识强度引导至目标区间。实验表明Hi-TTRL在多个数据集和骨干模型上一致优于标准TTRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTRL通过多数投票构造伪标签来更新策略，无需标注数据。但多数投票产生的奖励信号高度依赖于共识强度（rollout群体中最常见答案的频率）。低共识会因不成比例的大优势放大不可靠伪标签的更新，而高共识会降低奖励对比度导致梯度消失。这两种极端情况都会损害训练效果，因此需要一种机制来调节共识强度。

Method: Hi-TTRL框架包含两个关键组件：(1) 共识强度估计器，从部分rollout群体中快速估计当前共识强度；(2) MCMC提示采样器，当共识强度偏离目标区间时被激活。该采样器以功率变换后的prefix分布为目标，通过有限步近似采样生成rollout前缀作为提示。通过调整功率指数，可生成锐化或平坦化功率目标对应的提示，从而引导rollout共识强度朝向目标区间收敛。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种骨干模型上的实验表明，Hi-TTRL相比标准TTRL有一致性的性能提升。消融实验和共识引导分析验证了自适应提示引导的共识调节机制的有效性。

Conclusion: Hi-TTRL通过自适应提示引导的共识强度调节，有效缓解了TTRL中因共识强度过高或过低带来的训练问题，为测试时强化学习提供了一种更稳健的奖励信号调控方案。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models without labeled data by updating the policy with pseudo-labels constructed through majority voting. While effective, the reward signal assigned from majority voting is highly sensitive to consensus strength, defined as the frequency of the most common answer within a rollout group. In TTRL, consensus strength plays a dual role: it reflects both the reliability of the pseudo-label and the distribution of advantages. Low consensus can amplify updates from unreliable pseudo-labels through disproportionately large advantages, whereas high consensus reduces reward contrast and ultimately yields vanishing gradients. In this paper, we introduce Hi-TTRL, a test-time reinforcement learning framework that utilizes hints during sampling to regulate rollout consensus strength. Hi-TTRL first estimates consensus strength from a partial rollout group. When the consensus strength falls outside a target interval, it invokes a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) hint sampler. The sampler targets the power-transformed prefix distribution and uses finite-step approximate sampling to generate rollout prefixes as hints. By tuning the power exponent, Hi-TTRL generates hints with a sharpened or flattened power target, steering rollout consensus strength toward the target interval. Experiments on multiple datasets and backbones show that Hi-TTRL consistently improves over standard TTRL, with ablations and consensus-steering analyses validating the effectiveness of adaptive hint-guided consensus regulation.

</details>


### [183] [SFT Conflicts, RL Coexists: A Theoretical and Empirical Analysis of Multi-Task Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03573)
*Kejian Zhu,Zhuoran Jin,Shangqing Tu,Hongbang Yuan,Yushi Bai,Kang Liu,Juanzi Li,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现SFT和RL在多任务推理增强中存在本质差异：SFT在多阶段训练中遭受严重的任务冲突，而RL能够实现多任务的稳定共存。其根本原因在于两者梯度干扰的界限不同——SFT的干扰受梯度绝对幅度限制，而RL的干扰受梯度方差限制（由优势归一化和在策略优化引起），方差界限小使得RL在不同任务间产生近似正交的更新方向。基于此洞察，作者提出Parallel-RL范式，解耦多任务训练，显著提升效率和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究SFT与RL在增强大语言模型多任务推理能力时表现出的根本性差异，尤其是SFT在多阶段训练中面临严重任务冲突的问题，并寻求一种更高效、更灵活的多任务训练范式。

Method: 首先通过实验观察SFT与RL在多任务训练中的行为差异；其次在参数层面追踪到RL产生稀疏且近似正交的任务间更新；然后从多任务梯度干扰分析角度给出理论解释，区分SFT的范数受限干扰与RL的方差受限干扰；最后基于该理论提出Parallel-RL训练范式，解耦多任务训练过程。

Result: 实验表明RL的梯度干扰是方差受限的，由于优势归一化和在策略优化导致的小方差界限，产生了跨任务的近似正交优化方向；而SFT的干扰受梯度绝对幅度限制，导致多阶段训练中的任务冲突。Parallel-RL在此洞察基础上实现多任务解耦训练，显著提升效率与灵活性。

Conclusion: SFT与RL在多任务推理增强中的差异根源于梯度干扰的本质区别（范数受限vs方差受限），这为设计更高效的多任务训练范式（如Parallel-RL）提供了理论基础，展示了解耦多任务训练的显著优势。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) exhibit fundamentally different behaviors in enhancing multi-task reasoning for large language models (LLMs). Our preliminary experiments revealed a phenomenon: SFT suffers from severe task conflicts under multi-stage training, whereas RL enables stable coexistence across diverse tasks. Empirically, we trace this to the parameter level, observing that RL induces sparse and approximately orthogonal updates across tasks. We provide a theoretical explanation for this mechanism by analyzing multi-task gradient interference. Our results reveal a distinction: interference in SFT is norm-limited, scaling with the absolute gradient magnitude, whereas interference in RL is variance-limited, bounded by the gradient variance induced by advantage normalization and on-policy optimization. This small variance bound yields near-orthogonal optimization directions across tasks. Leveraging this insight, we propose Parallel-RL, a paradigm that decouples multi-task training, significantly improving efficiency and flexibility.

</details>


### [184] [Looking under the Wrong Lamppost: On the Limitations of Automated Translation Quality Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03577)
*Serge Gladkoff,Angelika Vaasa,Sue Ellen Wright,Ingemar Strandvik,Lifeng Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实证两个角度系统审视了翻译质量评估（QE）技术的基本局限性，指出当前QE系统在结构上不适合作为真实翻译工作流中的独立可靠工具，主张片段级QE分数不应单独用于生产环境中的路由、发布或审核跳过，未来应转向基于MQM的自动化人工评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动化翻译质量评估工具的大量涌现，其研究的稳健性、透明性和可重复性并未同步提升，这一差距值得深入批判性审视。作者希望揭示QE系统在真实翻译工作流中的结构性问题。

Method: 论文采用理论分析和实证证据综述相结合的方法，从多个维度（片段级评估的局限性、泛化失败、系统性偏差、过拟合与分布坍缩、性能差距、错误标注挑战、数据稀缺）系统梳理QE技术的根本性局限。

Result: 研究发现QE存在一系列相互关联且基本未解决的局限：最根本的是片段级评估会遗漏衔接、连贯性、文体和修辞等文本特征；此外还存在泛化失败、系统性偏差、过拟合、性能差距、错误标注困难及数据稀缺等问题。更重要的是，这些是源于人类语言复杂性和翻译认知/交际本质的结构性局限，更多数据和更好的架构迄今未能克服。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：片段级QE分数不应作为生产环境中路由、发布或审核跳过的独立依据，未来研究应聚焦于以MQM框架为核心实现人工评估的自动化。

Abstract: Automation of Translation Quality Estimation (QE) has emerged as a widely discussed approach to managing translation quality at scale, and a growing number of tools and technologies have been released in pursuit of this goal. However, the proliferation of new QE systems has not always been accompanied by robust, transparent, and reproducible research and testing. This gap deserves critical scrutiny. This paper examines some fundamental limitations of the QE technology from both theoretical and empirical perspectives, arguing that current QE systems are structurally ill-equipped to serve as reliable standalone tools in real-world translation workflows. The reviewed evidence suggests that QE suffers from a range of interrelated and largely unresolved limitations. Most fundamentally, the evaluation of the quality of translation at the level of isolated segments is problematic because it tends to miss out on cohesion, coherence, and stylistic and rhetorical text features. In addition, empirical research documents several other limitations and flaws, including failure to generalize, systematic biases, overfitting and distribution collapse, performance gaps, error annotation challenges, and data scarcity. These are structural limitations arising from the complexity of human language and translation as a cognitive and communicative act - limitations that more data and better architectures have so far not overcome. Consequently, segment-level QE scores should not be used as a standalone basis for routing, release, or review bypass in production; we argue future work should focus on automating human evaluation grounded in MQM.

</details>


### [185] [Disentangling Language Modeling and Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03599)
*Mykola Haltiuk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证字节级语言模型具有结构性优势：任意两个字节级模型共享相同的输出空间（字节），因此彼此间的知识迁移是精确的，不受各自分词器差异的影响。作者提出假设：字节级模型产生的两种分布（下一个字节的分布、patch边界位置的分布）可以几乎独立地被解耦和修改，并提出了两个实验来验证该假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，字节级语言模型被辩护的出发点通常是鲁棒性、多语言公平性和字符级技能。作者指出一个被忽视的结构性优势：由于所有字节级模型都读写字节，任意两个模型共享输出空间，知识迁移不需要解决分词器不匹配的问题。当前模型间迁移受限于per-model tokenizer，这阻碍了能力的低成本共享和新模型的能力调整。

Method: 作者先提出核心假设：字节级模型中"下一个字节"的分布（表征能力）与"patch边界落在何处"的分布（表征边界策略）可以被解耦并几乎独立地修改。基于此设计了两个关键实验：（1）让一个模型在不改变自身边界策略的前提下吸收教师的语法或知识能力；（2）在保持能力不变的情况下单独调整模型放置patch边界的方式。此外提供了支撑该假设的初步度量结果。

Result: 论文本身尚未完成两个决定性实验，仅提供了支撑假设所需的初步测量数据。其核心主张是：如果假设成立，一旦字节级模型成为标准，能力迁移和边界重塑将变得廉价且常规，彻底摆脱当前每模型tokenizer的阻碍。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁社区将字节级接口作为共享标准推进。如果解耦假设得到验证，字节级模型将成为能力迁移和架构调整的通用桥梁，消除tokenizer异构性带来的迁移壁垒，从而大幅降低模型定制和进化的成本。

Abstract: Byte-level language models are usually argued for on the grounds of robustness, multilingual fairness, and character-level skills. We point to a different, structural advantage: because they read and write bytes, any two of them share an output space, so knowledge transfer between them is exact and independent of how either was originally tokenized. We hypothesize that the two distributions a byte-level model produces, one over the next byte, one over where its patch boundaries fall, can be disentangled and changed almost independently. A model could absorb a teacher's capability while keeping its own boundaries, or change how it places those boundaries while keeping its capabilities. We lay out the two experiments that would settle the hypothesis, alongside preliminary measurements of the properties they rest on. We argue that the community should move toward a byte-level interface as a shared standard: if the hypothesis holds, then once byte-level models are the norm, transferring capabilities and reshaping boundaries between them become cheap and routine, free of the per-model tokenizer that blocks them today.

</details>


### [186] [Language-Specialized Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Multilingual LLM-Based ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03610)
*Yuan Xie,Jiaqi Song,Xianliang Wang,Ming Lei,Jie Gao,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LS-MOPD方法，通过语言专用多教师在线策略蒸馏解决多语种ASR中异构语言联合建模的优化冲突问题，将语言特定知识获取与多语能力整合解耦，实验证明其超越所有RL教师的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的ASR系统虽已具备多语种能力，但联合建模声学、音系、词汇特征异构的语言会不可避免地引发优化冲突，损害单语言专门化性能。需要一个机制来解耦语言特定知识获取与多语能力整合，减少跨语言优化冲突。

Method: LS-MOPD将语言特定知识获取与多语能力整合解耦：首先通过强化学习（RL）独立优化语言专用教师模型，随后通过语言路由和token级多教师蒸馏将这些教师的知识整合到通用多语学生模型中，从而减少直接的跨语言优化冲突。此外探索了静态和动态两种声学前缀配置，研究教师-学生前缀一致性对在线策略蒸馏效果的影響。

Result: 在涵盖普通话、普通话次方言、粤语和英语的基准测试上，LS-MOPD大幅优于RL基线，并持续超越由最优RL教师定义的实证性能包络，展现出超越所有教师泛化的潜力。

Conclusion: LS-MOPD通过语言专用多教师蒸馏机制有效缓解了多语种ASR中的跨语言优化冲突，其性能不仅超越RL基线，甚至能超越单个最优RL教师的表现，表明该方法在多语种ASR领域具有通用泛化潜力。

Abstract: Modern LLM-based ASR systems have established multilingual capability as a standard feature, leveraging large-scale multilingual corpora and LLMs' cross-lingual knowledge to achieve competitive performance across multilingual benchmarks. However, joint modeling of languages with heterogeneous acoustic, phonological, and lexical characteristics inevitably introduces optimization conflicts, undermining language-wise specialization. To address this challenge, we propose Language-Specialized Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (LS-MOPD), which decouples language-specific knowledge acquisition from multilingual capability integration: language-specialized teachers are independently optimized via reinforcement learning (RL), after which their expertise is integrated into a generalist multilingual student through language routing and token-level multi-teacher distillation, thereby reducing direct cross-lingual optimization conflicts. We further explore two acoustic-prefix configurations, static and dynamic, to examine how teacher--student prefix consistency influences the efficacy of on-policy distillation. Experiments on benchmarks covering Mandarin, Mandarin subdialects, Cantonese, and English demonstrate that LS-MOPD substantially outperforms RL baselines and consistently surpasses the empirical performance envelope defined by best-performing RL teachers, revealing its potential to generalize beyond all teachers in multilingual ASR.

</details>


### [187] [A machine-readable catalogue of the Tsiolkovsky papers (fond 555, Archive of the Russian Academy of Sciences), and a way to measure how well its handwriting can be read](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03617)
*Vladimir Beskorovainyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了将齐奥尔科夫斯基（Konstantin Tsiolkovsky）个人档案数字化为机器可读目录的过程，包括2,019个文件、51,008张扫描件、页面级手写/打字稿分类，以及一个不断增长的机器转录语料库。文章还提出了一种在无地面真值档案中衡量手写文本识别准确率的方法，并报告了该方法的验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齐奥尔科夫斯基档案虽然已被扫描公开，但缺乏可查询目录、全文搜索和数据集，研究者只能逐页浏览，严重阻碍了学术研究。作者旨在将这一重要档案转化为机器可用的结构化数据，并解决档案领域长期存在的手写识别精度难以量化评估的问题。

Method: 作者构建了全部2,019个文件与51,008张扫描件的机器可读目录，从档案自身描述中提取1,969个文件的日期信息，并对每个扫描件进行手写/打字稿的页面级分类。在无地面真值的情况下，作者利用打字机时代档案常以手稿和打字稿两种形式保存同一文本的特点：同时转录两种版本并比较，可在源与流程相同的情况下隔离读取错误。通过294对手稿-打字稿配对验证了该方法。

Result: 294对配对的比较显示，同一手写页面的两次读取在单词层面的中位数一致率为37%。在有已出版版本可对照的两个文件上，该估计方法无偏且在1个百分点以内，页面排序相关度达0.92（当出版版本忠实于原稿时）。该方法也能界定工具的使用边界：同一作品的两个变体仅共享19%的词汇，低于单页两次读取的一致率，因此在该质量水平下无法逐词比对不同版本。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅提供了齐奥尔科夫斯基档案的机器可读语料库，还提出了一种可推广的无地面真值手写识别精度评估方法。作者坦率报告了负面结果（当前转录质量不足以进行逐词比勘），并将该限制内置于工具设计中，体现了严谨的学术态度和实用导向。

Abstract: The personal archive of Konstantin Tsiolkovsky (1857-1935) is held as fond 555 of the Archive of the Russian Academy of Sciences. The archive scanned the fond and published the images, but with no queryable catalogue, no full-text search and no dataset: the holdings can only be browsed one page at a time. This paper describes a machine-readable catalogue of all 2,019 files and 51,008 scans, a dating for 1,969 files taken from the archive's own descriptions, a page-level classification of every scan into handwriting and typescript, and a growing corpus of machine transcriptions (currently 322 files, 5,454 scans).
  It also reports a way to measure handwritten-text-recognition accuracy in an archive with no ground truth. Archives of the typewriter era often preserve one text twice, as manuscript and as a typed copy; transcribing both and comparing isolates the reading error, since source and pipeline are identical and only page difficulty differs. Across 294 such pairs from 27 files, two readings of a handwritten page agree on a median 37% of words. On two files that also have a published edition the estimate can be checked against ground truth: it is unbiased to within a percentage point and ranks pages as the truth does (rank correlation 0.92 where the edition is a faithful witness). This bounds use: two variants of one work here share 19% of words, below the rate at which two readings of a single page agree, so the redactions cannot be collated word by word at this quality. That negative result is reported as such, and the constraint is built into the tool.

</details>


### [188] [LoopMTP: A looped transformer guided by latent multi-token prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03624)
*Behzad Shomali,Markus Frey,David Berghaus,Joachim Koehler,Mehdi Ali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoopMTP通过多token预测（MTP）为循环Transformer提供密集的前瞻性监督，将循环t次的隐状态与未来t步的token嵌入软对齐，解决潜变量过度思考与非差异化计算问题，在固定参数下将平均准确率相对提升最多8.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过复用单层堆叠实现参数高效的深度扩展，但中间表示在循环间缺乏引导，导致潜伏的过度思考（latent overthinking）和未区分的计算（undifferentiated computation），影响推理质量。

Method: 提出LoopMTP框架，利用多token预测提供循环所缺失的密集前瞻监督。核心思路是建立结构对应：循环T次的模型可预测未来T个token。具体实现为：将循环t步的隐状态与未来t步token的嵌入进行软对齐（softly aligning），并用轻量级门控（lightweight gate）在迭代间保留有用信息。

Result: 相比非循环基线，平均准确率相对提升最多8.1%，且训练在多达15个循环时保持稳定。

Conclusion: LoopMTP通过MTP监督成功改善了循环Transformer的推理质量，验证了多token预测与循环深度之间的结构对应关系有效性，同时保持了训练的稳定性。

Abstract: Looped transformers have emerged as a parameter-efficient alternative to scaling depth for strong reasoning. By reusing one stack of layers across $T$ iterations, they attain the effective depth and reasoning capabilities of larger models at a fixed parameter count. Yet existing approaches suffer from latent overthinking and undifferentiated computation, largely because intermediate representations receive no guidance across loops. Multi-token prediction (MTP) supplies exactly the dense, forward-looking supervision the loop is missing. We propose \textsc{LoopMTP}, which links the two through a structural correspondence in latent space: a model that loops $T$ times can anticipate $T$ future tokens. \textsc{LoopMTP} realizes this by softly aligning the hidden state of loop $t$ with the embedding of the token $t$ steps ahead, while a lightweight gate preserves useful information across iterations. \textsc{LoopMTP} improves average accuracy by up to 8.1\% (relative) over the non-looped baseline, with training remaining stable for up to 15 loops.

</details>


### [189] [Decoupling Generation and Selection for Budget-Constrained Faithful Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03655)
*Zeyu Wang,Guanghua Wang,Meng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化的"生成-选择"框架用于句子预算受限的摘要任务。预训练生成器产生多个候选摘要，随后组合选择器在显式预算下平衡相关性、事实性和冗余度来构建最终摘要，无需重新训练生成器即可支持MMR、ILP和DPP风格的目标函数。实验表明在事实性和source-grounding指标上有一致提升，尤其在多文档摘要中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的抽象式摘要模型存在事实不一致、冗余和长度控制薄弱等问题，需要一种不重新训练模型即可改善事实接地性的机制。

Method: 模块化生成-选择框架：1) 预训练生成器生成多个候选摘要；2) 将候选摘要分解为句子级候选；3) 组合选择器通过平衡相关性、事实性和冗余度，在显式句子预算约束下构建最终摘要。框架支持最大边际相关(MMR)、整数线性规划(ILP)和基于DPP的对数行列式目标函数。

Result: 在CNN/DailyMail、Multi-News、FaithBench和TofuEval数据集上，事实性和source-grounding指标一致提升（尤其多文档摘要），但参考重叠分数有所下降。人工评估显示感知一致性、相关性、清晰度和简洁性更高，但连贯性略有降低。

Conclusion: 将生成与选择解耦提供了一种模型无关的、改进事实接地性的机制，代码已公开。

Abstract: Abstractive summarization models remain vulnerable to factual inconsistency, redundancy, and weak length control. We propose a modular generation-and-selection framework for sentence-budget-constrained summarization. A pretrained generator produces multiple candidate summaries, which are decomposed into sentence-level candidates. A combinatorial selector then constructs the final summary by balancing relevance, factuality, and redundancy under an explicit budget. The framework supports MMR, ILP, and a DPP-inspired log-determinant objective without retraining the generator. Experiments on CNN/DailyMail, Multi-News, FaithBench, and TofuEval show consistent improvements in factuality and source-grounding metrics, especially for multi-document summarization, at the cost of lower reference-overlap scores. Human evaluation further indicates higher perceived consistency, relevance, clarity, and conciseness, with a small reduction in coherence. These results show that decoupling generation from selection provides a model-agnostic mechanism for improving factual grounding. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/bcfs-D05E/.

</details>


### [190] [How Closely Do LLM Reviews Align with Human Peer Review?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03659)
*Abraham Camelo-Guerrero,Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在受控条件下系统比较了OpenAI GPT-5.4、Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview和Anthropic Claude Opus 4.6三个大语言模型对300篇ICLR 2026投稿的评审结果，与人类评审及最终录用决策进行对齐分析。三个模型均能区分录用与拒绝的论文，但均无法复现人类评审中oral与poster论文之间的区分；评分模式因模型而异，且LLM与人类在识别论文弱点的侧重点上存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM越来越多地被用于生成科学评审，但现有评估很少在相同的受控设置下检验不同模型提供商是否既对齐会议录用决策又对齐人类评审偏好。该研究旨在填补这一空白，提供跨模型提供商的系统性比较。

Method: 选取300篇主题匹配的ICLR 2026投稿，均匀分为oral、poster和rejected三类。三个LLM（GPT-5.4、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview、Claude Opus 4.6）在移除决策信息后，使用完全相同的指令和评分量表对每篇论文进行评审。从三个互补维度分析：与宽泛及细粒度决策类别的对齐程度、推荐量表使用差异、识别弱点的主题一致性。

Result: 所有三个LLM都能区分录用与拒绝的论文，但均未复现人类评分中oral与poster之间的区分。评分模式因提供商而异：Gemini系统性给出更高评分，OpenAI和Claude在rejected和poster论文上更接近人类但对待oral论文更加严格。LLM更频繁识别缺失基线对比的问题，而人类更常提出计算效率方面的担忧。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，宽泛的决策对齐并不意味着一模型与更细致的人类判断或评审优先事项一致。评估LLM评审能力时需要超越粗粒度的录用判断，考虑细粒度决策类别、评分分布和评审主题侧重点等多维度对齐。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate scientific reviews, yet existing evaluations rarely examine whether different providers align with both conference decisions and human reviewing priorities within the same controlled setting. We compare reviews from OpenAI GPT-5.4, Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview, and Anthropic Claude Opus 4.6 with human reviews and final decisions for 300 topic-matched ICLR 2026 submissions, equally divided among oral, poster, and rejected papers. Each model reviewed every paper using identical instructions and rating scales after decision information was removed. Our study contributes a cross-provider analysis of three complementary dimensions: alignment with broad and fine-grained decision categories, differences in recommendation-scale usage, and thematic agreement in identified weaknesses. All three LLMs distinguished accepted from rejected papers, but none reproduced the oral versus poster distinction present in human ratings. Scoring patterns were provider-specific: Gemini assigned systematically higher ratings, while OpenAI and Claude were closer to humans for rejected and poster papers but more critical of oral papers. Human and LLM reviews also differed in emphasis, with LLMs more frequently identifying missing baseline comparisons and humans more often raising computational-efficiency concerns. These results show that broad decision alignment does not imply agreement with finer human judgments or reviewing priorities.

</details>


### [191] [VetScore: Risk-Weighted Fact Verification for Veterinary Long-Form QA with Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03675)
*Ivan Kartáč,Jan Tovarys,Mateusz Lango,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了VetScore，一种用于兽医领域长篇问答的多步骤评估方法，通过将输出分解为独立声明、按危害潜力加权并评估其对引用摘录的忠实度，来计算风险调整后的总体分数。实验表明即使使用小型评判模型也能与兽医专家高度相关，且支持多维度的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管引用摘录可以提高生成输出的可靠性和对引用来源的忠实度，但这并不能保证生成的声明确实与提供的摘录内容相一致。在兽医和人类医学等高风险领域，这一忠实度问题尤为关键，因此需要专门的评估方法来衡量生成声明是否真正由支持的摘录所支撑，并考虑声明的潜在危害。

Method: VetScore是一种多步骤评估方法：(1) 将输出分段并分解为独立的声明；(2) 根据每个声明的危害潜力进行加权评分；(3) 评估每个声明对源摘录的忠实度；(4) 计算整体的风险调整后分数。研究收集了专家标注的元评估数据集，并使用多种评判模型进行评估。

Result: VetScore在与兽医专家的评分相关性上取得了高分数，即使使用小型评判模型也能保持高相关性，并且能提供跨多个维度的可解释性。

Conclusion: VetScore为兽医长形式问答提供了一种既忠实又考虑风险危害的评估框架，通过危害加权的声明级评分方法，在模型规模较小的情况下依然能达到专家级评估水平，同时保留多维度可解释性，适用于高风险领域中对生成内容可靠性的评估需求。

Abstract: Citation excerpts can be used to increase the reliability of generated outputs and their faithfulness to cited sources, which is especially important in high-stakes domains such as human and veterinary medicine. However, this does not guarantee that generated claims are faithful to the provided excerpts. We present VetScore, a multi-step evaluation method for veterinary long-form question answering, designed to assess how well are generated claims supported by the provided excerpts, weighing this information by each claim's harm potential. VetScore first segments the output and decomposes it into individual claims, then scores each claim with respect to its harm potential and evaluates its faithfulness to source excerpts, and finally calculates the overall risk-adjusted score. We collect an expert-annotated meta-evaluation dataset, evaluate our approach with a range of judge models, and show that it achieves high correlations with veterinary experts even with small judge models, while offering explainability across multiple dimensions.

</details>


### [192] [Predicting Deep Neural Network Training Outcomes from Early Training Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03709)
*Ranjita Naik,Anh D. Nguyen,Pankaj Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了能否仅利用单个训练运行自身的早期遥测数据（前5个epoch的损失、训练准确率、梯度信噪比、权重范数增长和激活饱和快照）及其采样的超参数，来预测该运行的最终结果。在23,788个训练运行、六种架构/数据集组合上，梯度提升树模型实现了R²=0.92-0.99的最终准确率回归和ROC-AUC=0.983-0.998的相对性能分类，且单个epoch即可产生有用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模超参数搜索中大量计算资源浪费在早期就已注定失败的超参数配置上。现有方法通常需要参考其他运行或外部数据，作者希望探索是否仅依赖单个运行自身的早期遥测数据就能预测其最终结果，从而为计算资源分配提供决策支持。

Method: 使用梯度提升树（gradient-boosted trees）作为预测模型，输入特征包括前5个epoch的每-epoch损失、训练准确率、梯度信噪比、权重范数增长和激活饱和快照，以及采样的超参数。评估三个预测任务：最终测试准确率回归、域内相对性能分类、训练动力学失败（含数值发散）检测。通过配对消融实验分析各类遥测特征的贡献，并在永久保留的超参数配置集上进行评估。

Result: 在23,788个训练运行上，前5个epoch的遥测数据达到R²=0.92-0.99（最终准确率回归）和ROC-AUC=0.983-0.998（相对分类）。单个epoch即可产生有用预测。配对消融显示梯度级和权重级遥测相比仅用损失和准确率曲线提供了统计上一致的改进，但实际收益因域而异。相似架构间迁移强，跨数据集迁移主要受准确率尺度差异限制而非潜在关系丢失。

Conclusion: 早期训练遥测数据可以作为实用的决策支持信号用于计算资源分配，但任何自动化干预仍需人类监督。

Abstract: Large hyperparameter sweeps for deep neural networks spend substantial compute on configurations that are effectively doomed from the first few epochs. We study whether a single training run's own early telemetry - per-epoch loss, training accuracy, gradient signal-to-noise ratio, weight-norm growth, and an activation-saturation snapshot - together with its sampled hyperparameters, can predict that run's eventual outcome without reference to other runs. We evaluate three prediction tasks: final test accuracy, relative performance within a domain, and training-dynamics failure, including numerical divergence. Across 23,788 training runs spanning six architecture/dataset combinations, gradient-boosted trees using only the first five epochs of telemetry achieve R^2 = 0.92-0.99 for final-accuracy regression and ROC-AUC = 0.983-0.998 for relative classification on a permanently held-out set of hyperparameter configurations. Useful prediction is already available after a single epoch. A paired ablation shows that gradient- and weight-level telemetry provides a statistically consistent improvement over loss and accuracy curves alone, although the practical gain varies by domain. Transfer is strong between similar architectures, while cross-dataset transfer is limited mainly by differences in accuracy scale rather than loss of the underlying relationship. These results suggest that early-training telemetry can provide a practical decision-support signal for compute allocation while motivating human oversight for any automated intervention.

</details>


### [193] [Detecting Hallucinations and Recovering Verified Answers in Arabic Islamic Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03720)
*Khaled Ziani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HalluScoring 2026任务2.1的系统：基于微调的google/gemma-4-12B-it模型，用于伊斯兰教义问答的幻觉检测（区分AI生成回答是否幻觉）和正确答案选择（从六个相近候选中选出真实答案），在600个问答实例上取得0.912的综合分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在回答伊斯兰教相关问题时可能生成难以识别的事实性错误（幻觉），需要构建一个统一的两阶段预测系统来检测幻觉并找出真实答案，以保障AI在宗教敏感领域的回答可靠性。

Method: 基于共享任务提供的600个问答实例（341个幻觉、259个非幻觉）的伊斯兰知识数据集，微调google/gemma-4-12B-it模型，推理时采用确定性解码，并对生成输出进行归一化处理以提取幻觉标签和所选选项。

Result: 幻觉检测的Macro-F1为0.928，标签准确率为0.935，选项选择准确率为0.895，综合得分0.912。

Conclusion: 系统在幻觉检测和答案选择两个阶段均表现良好，但选项选择准确率相对较低，说明从看似合理的候选答案中辨别真实答案比检测幻觉回答更具挑战性。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent responses to Islamic questions while introducing factual errors that are difficult to identify. This paper presents our system for \textsc{HalluScoring 2026} Task 2.1, \textit{Islamic Hallucination Detection and Find the Truth}. The task requires a unified two-step prediction: determining whether an Arabic answer generated by an LLM is hallucinated and selecting the verified answer from six closely related candidate options. We use the Islamic knowledge dataset provided by the shared task, which contains 600 question--answer instances, including 341 hallucinated and 259 non-hallucinated answers. Our system is based on the fine-tuned \texttt{google/gemma-4-12B-it} model and uses deterministic decoding during inference. The generated outputs are normalized to extract the hallucination label and the selected option. The system achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.928 and a label accuracy of 0.935 for hallucination detection, together with an option accuracy of 0.895 for answer selection. These results yield a combined score of 0.912, demonstrating strong performance across both stages of the task. The lower option-selection accuracy indicates that distinguishing the verified answer from plausible alternatives remains more challenging than detecting hallucinated responses.

</details>


### [194] [GPTKB 2.0: Direct Construction of Disambiguated Knowledge Bases from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03729)
*Yujia Hu,Tuan-Phong Nguyen,Simon Razniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GPTKB 2.0 提出一种直接从大语言模型构建消歧知识库的方法论，通过实时的实体、关系和类别消歧，成功构建了包含超过100万个消歧实体和3840万条三元组的大规模知识库，这是首个百万规模且具有显式内部规范化的LLM原生知识库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化知识库构建（AKBC）工作倾向于直接从LLM生成知识库，将模型本身视为知识来源。然而LLM天然不具备实体表示能力，导致知识库中出现重复条目和概念混淆的问题。因此需要一种方法来实现直接从LLM构建消歧后的知识库。

Method: GPTKB 2.0 提出一种系统性的方法论，在生成过程中嵌入实时的（on-the-fly）实体、关系和类别消歧机制。该方法精心设计以同时满足可扩展性和消歧准确性两个目标，并对核心设计决策进行分析，刻画了准确性、规模与成本之间的权衡关系。

Result: GPTKB 2.0 在规模上执行，获得了包含超过100万个消歧实体和3840万条三元组的实体化知识库。这是首个百万规模、具有实体、关系和类别显式内部规范化的LLM原生知识库，与以往基于维基媒体中心化的研究工作有显著区别。

Conclusion: GPTKB 2.0 证明了直接从LLM构建大规模、消歧良好的知识库是可行的，标志着从维基媒体中心化的KB构建范式向LLM原生知识库构建范式的重要转变，并已在公开网站发布供社区使用。

Abstract: Automated Knowledge Base Construction (AKBC) is a core NLP task, and recent work proposes generating knowledge bases directly from large language models (LLMs), treating the model itself as the knowledge source. However, LLMs natively possess no representation of entities, leading to duplicate entries as well as conflations. We propose GPTKB 2.0, a methodology for constructing disambiguated KBs directly from LLMs. GPTKB 2.0 incorporates on-the-fly disambiguation of entities, relations and classes, and is meticulously designed to satisfy both scalability and disambiguation accuracy. We analyze the central design decisions and characterize the trade-offs between accuracy, scale, and cost. We execute GPTKB 2.0 at scale, obtaining a materialized KB containing over 1M disambiguated entities and 38.4M triples. This represents the first million-scale LLM-native KB with explicit internal canonicalization of entities, relations, and classes, a significant departure from prior Wikimedia-centric works. GPTKB 2.0 is available at https://gptkb.org/.

</details>


### [195] [MDLMPE: Distribution Aware Positional Encoding for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03769)
*Tong Ling,Hang Lei,Feng Xiao,Changhui Sun,Jiahang Xie,Hao Liu,Lu Liu,Yanlong Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MDLMPE，一种专为掩码扩散语言模型设计的感知分布的位置编码方法，通过将token可用状态表示为二进制序列、应用高斯加权和余弦基投影，生成分布感知特征并调制RoPE相位，在LLaDA和DREAM上多个设置中优于传统位置编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的降噪过程产生动态、非连续的被揭示/被掩码token配置，与自回归模型的连续前缀暴露方式根本不同。传统位置编码（如RoPE）仅捕捉序列顺序和成对位移，无法感知这种不断演变的token可用性结构，限制了MDLM的性能。

Method: MDLMPE将token可用状态编码为二进制序列，施加距离感知的高斯加权，再通过余弦基投影得到分布感知的位置特征。这些特征被加到token嵌入中，并通过轻量级MLP映射为角度偏移，用于调制标准RoPE相位。该方法首次使位置表示显式感知不断变化的被揭示/被掩码配置。

Result: 在LLaDA和DREAM上的大量实验表明，MDLMPE在监督微调、预训练、零样本评估和块扩散设置中普遍优于传统位置编码方法。消融实验证实，可用性状态、高斯局部性、频谱基和嵌入注入的完整组合能取得最强结果。

Conclusion: 不断演变的token可用性分布是掩码扩散语言模型中有用的位置信号，MDLMPE建立了一个新的位置编码范式，为MDLM提供了感知分布的位置表征。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) enable parallel generation and bidirectional context modeling, but their positional context differs fundamentally from that of autoregressive (AR) models. Whereas AR decoding exposes a contiguous prefix, MDLM denoising produces dynamic, non-contiguous configurations of revealed and masked tokens. Conventional positional encodings such as RoPE capture sequence order and pairwise displacement but remain insensitive to this evolving token-availability structure. To address this limitation, we propose MDLMPE, a positional encoding designed specifically for masked diffusion. To the best of our knowledge, MDLMPE is the first method to make positional representations explicitly aware of the changing revealed/masked configuration. It represents token availability as a binary sequence, applies distance-aware Gaussian weighting, and projects the resulting pattern through a cosine basis to obtain distribution-aware positional features. These features are added to token embeddings and mapped by a lightweight MLP to angular offsets that modulate the standard RoPE phases. Extensive experiments on LLaDA and DREAM demonstrate that MDLMPE generally outperforms conventional positional encoding methods across supervised fine-tuning, pretraining, zero-shot evaluation, and block-diffusion settings. Further ablations show that the complete combination of availability state, Gaussian locality, spectral basis, and embedding injection yields the strongest result. These results establish the evolving token-availability distribution as a useful positional signal for masked diffusion language models.

</details>


### [196] [Efficient Knowledge Distillation for LLMs: Offline Top-K Logits and a Fused Chunked KL Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03796)
*Bakbergen Ryskulov,Iker García-Ferrero,David Montero,David Jansen,Ali Hashemi,Jezabel R. Garcia,Antonio Tiene,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两个系统级贡献来提升小语言模型知识蒸馏训练的效率：离线KD（缓存教师模型的top-K logits并针对缓存进行训练）相比在线蒸馏每次迭代快约29%、吞吐量提升最高达41%；以及提出融合的分块KL损失，避免物化完整词表大小的logit张量，使峰值显存与序列长度呈线性关系，从而在单GPU上支持4倍上下文长度（32,768 token）的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在低延迟、低成本、本地部署等约束下往往是唯一选择，但这类模型很少从零训练，通常通过知识蒸馏（KD）从压缩模型恢复。恢复步骤在很大程度上决定了最终模型质量，但蒸馏训练本身成本高昂，亟需提高其效率。

Method: 方法包括两部分：(1) 离线KD：一次性缓存教师模型的top-K logits，学生模型仅针对缓存进行训练，无需教师在内存中，从而节省显存并提升迭代速度；(2) 融合的分块KL损失（fused, chunked KL loss）：通过分块计算KL损失，避免物化完整词表大小的logit张量，使峰值显存随序列长度线性增长而非随词表大小爆炸。

Result: 离线KD在训练损失上与在线蒸馏几乎一致，但每次迭代快约29%，在单个H200 GPU上吞吐量提升最高达41%；融合分块KL损失在单GPU上支持4倍上下文长度（32,768 tokens），并通过输出头玩具基准验证了从4K到256K tokens的显存和迭代率扩展性。

Conclusion: 两项系统贡献相结合，使得大规模蒸馏训练修复（healing）和数百次消融实验变得负担得起。论文还报告了损失设计和序列打包方面的辅助消融实验，并开源了分块损失的实现。

Abstract: Small language models are often the only option for deployment under tight latency, cost, and on-premises constraints, but they are rarely trained from scratch: a compressed model is usually recovered through knowledge distillation (KD). This recovery step largely decides the final quality, yet it is expensive. We present a practitioner's study of how to make distillation training efficient, organised around two systems contributions. First, we show that offline KD (caching the teacher's top-$K$ logits once and training the student against the cache) matches online distillation at near-identical training loss while removing the teacher from memory, running about 29\% faster per iteration, and reaching up to 41\% higher throughput on a single H200 GPU. Second, we introduce a \emph{fused, chunked KL loss} that never materialises the full vocabulary-sized logit tensor, making peak memory linear in the sequence length. This removes the memory spike that otherwise caps context length and lets us train at four times the context (32{,}768 tokens) on a single GPU. A separate output-head-only toy benchmark isolates the loss kernel and confirms its memory and iteration-rate scaling from 4K to 256K tokens. Together these make large-scale healing and hundreds of ablations affordable. We also report supporting ablations on loss design and sequence packing. We release our chunked-loss implementation: https://github.com/CompactifAI/Full-Chunked-KL-Loss.

</details>


### [197] [M-GATE: Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03803)
*Tomáš Burkert,Angelika Peljak-Łapińska,David Zelený*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M-GATE基准，用于测评多语言模型的语言掌握能力（而非仅任务能力），包含语法错误检测、往返翻译和分词效率三个任务，覆盖30种语言。评估50多个模型后发现：流畅性与熟练度严重分离，翻译质量与预训练数据占比高度相关，推理增强对翻译有效但对错误检测效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅测试模型能否用某种语言执行任务，混淆了语言流畅性（fluency）与实际语言掌握能力（proficiency）。作者认为需要一种能够直接测评模型对语言本身掌握程度的指标，以区分"会说"与"真懂"。

Method: 构建M-GATE基准：（1）基于语言学家手工构造并对抗性筛选的句子进行语法错误检测；（2）将共享英语源文本往返翻译至29种目标语言，由三供应商LLM评审团打分（与专业标注者验证过一致性）；（3）补充tokenizer效率指标。对50多个模型、80余种配置进行评估。

Result: 流畅性与熟练度显著分离：最佳模型的对抗性语法项MCC仅0.36，接近随机；错误偏向漏报。翻译质量与语言预训练数据占比强相关（r=0.86），低资源惩罚显著但随模型迭代在缩小。启用推理稳定提升翻译效果，但对错误检测影响较小甚至为负。

Conclusion: 多语言模型的翻译能力与语法掌握能力并不对等，最佳配置因任务而异。为抵御数据污染，测试项保持私有，通过持续更新的公共排行榜发布，附有示例。

Abstract: Multilingual language models are deployed across a hundred or more languages, yet most benchmarks test whether a model can perform a task _in_ a language rather than whether it commands the language itself, conflating fluency with proficiency. We introduce M-GATE (Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency), a benchmark of linguistic proficiency spanning 30 typologically diverse languages from high- to low-resource. M-GATE comprises three tasks: grammatical error detection on linguist-crafted, adversarially selected sentences that turn on hard, language-specific phenomena; round-trip translation of shared English sources across 29 target languages, scored by a three-provider LLM judge panel validated against professional annotators; and a supplementary tokenizer-efficiency measure. We evaluate over 50 models in more than 80 configurations. Fluency and proficiency come apart sharply: models that translate competently sit near chance on the adversarial grammar items, the best reaching a Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) of only 0.36, and their errors lean systematically toward under-flagging, accepting ungrammatical text rather than raising false alarms. Translation quality closely tracks a language's share of pretraining data (r = 0.86 against log Common Crawl share), producing a steep low-resource penalty that is nonetheless narrowing with successive model releases. Enabling reasoning reliably improves translation, while its effect on error detection is smaller and for some models negative, so the best configuration is task-dependent. To resist contamination, test items are kept private behind a continuously updated public leaderboard, with illustrative examples released (https://m-gate.ai).

</details>


### [198] [VIBE: A VAD-Informed Benchmark for Entity-Centered Affective Profiling of Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03810)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Alexander Evseev,Timofei Sivoraksha,Stepan Ukolov,Mikhail Solovev,Danil Sazanakov,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出VIBE基准，用于对LLM输出进行以实体为中心的情感画像分析，在效价-唤醒-支配（VAD）空间中测量，核心贡献是一个测量契约：将生成与外部评分分离，区分标量偏好、响应级VAD和目标定向VAD，并通过"情感护照"报告格式输出结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（情感、偏好度、情绪）只能捕捉情感空间的一部分，但没有一个能同时结合目标定向的VAD归因、明确的评分者契约和护照式报告格式。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立更完整、可复现的实体情感测量框架。

Method: VIBE引入了三项核心机制：(1)测量契约：生成与外部评分分离，区分三种测量类型（标量偏好、响应级VAD、目标定向VAD），通过Affective Passport报告；(2)三层实证验证：H1验证标量偏好无法涵盖唤醒和支配维度，H2验证整体响应VAD与目标定向VAD是不同的契约，H3作为协议漂移诊断；(3)通过评分者身份、覆盖率、协议和解释限制等元数据增强报告。

Result: H1：效价值经交叉验证（评分者-人类rV=0.944，评分者间rV=0.954），但唤醒和支配维度是单评分者方向性估计而非精确点值（人类标注者间rA=0.495，rD=0.702）。H2：同文本整体情感色调与具体目标的情感画像不同。H3：诱导条件会改变情感画像，证明需要在上下文元数据中记录协议。

Conclusion: VIBE研究结果为实体中心的情感画像分析提供了一种可文档化的实践：情感画像应附带评分者身份、覆盖率、协议和解释限制发布。这为LLM情感评估提供了更严谨、更可复现的标准，也提醒研究者该领域存在固有的测量局限。

Abstract: Large language models routinely describe socially salient targets, including political figures, countries, religions, organizations, historical events, and social groups, encoding affective framing alongside factual content: a target may appear favorable or threatening, calm or conflictual, powerful or vulnerable. Existing work captures parts of this space through sentiment, favorability, and emotion benchmarks, but none combines target-directed VAD attribution, an explicit scorer contract, and a passport reporting format. We introduce VIBE, a benchmark for entity-centered affective profiling of LLM outputs in Valence-Arousal-Dominance (VAD) space. Its core contribution is a measurement contract: VIBE separates generation from external scoring, distinguishes scalar favorability, response-level VAD, and target-directed VAD, and reports profiles through an Affective Passport. Three empirical layers support the contract. H1 shows scalar favorability does not subsume arousal and dominance: valence findings are cross-validated (rV = 0.944 judge-human, rV = 0.954 inter-scorer); arousal and dominance are single-scorer directional estimates, not point-precise, consistent with known inter-annotator difficulty on these axes (rA = 0.495, rD = 0.702 among human annotators). H2 shows whole-response and target-directed VAD are different contracts: the same text can carry one affective tone overall while representing the named target differently. H3 is a protocol-drift diagnostic: elicitation conditions shift profiles, motivating context metadata in every affective report. These results motivate entity-centered affective profiling as a documented practice: profiles should be released with scorer identity, coverage, protocol, and interpretation limits.

</details>


### [199] [Sensitivity, Causality, and Repair Dissociate: A Layer-Wise Analysis of Perturbation Robustness and Its Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03842)
*Nathan Labiosa,David Buff,Ena Nayak,Erica Donno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型在表面扰动输入（打字错误、OCR噪声、同音词）下"哪个层负责"的三种操作化定义——敏感性（表示分歧最大的层）、因果性（恢复干净激活能恢复预测的层）和补偿能力（小适配器能修复损伤的层），并证明这三者相互分离。在五个模型中发现两种传播机制（尖峰-抑制型和晚期累积型），且敏感性与因果性在满足身份补丁门槛的两个模型上呈负相关（rho=-0.72至-0.88）。规模扩展实验表明晚期累积特征随模型尺寸单调增强。论文提出"级联破坏"机制解释该分离：在因果性指示的早期层放置适配器会破坏完整的下游计算，使诊断标记位置成为最差的适配器放置点。通过固定测试框架在不同模型和GSM8K思维链任务上验证了这一预测，并提供实用指南（免训练LRD预筛选和默认最深层放置规则），同时揭示基于思维链任务评估干预时的截断偏差方法学警告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当语言模型在表面扰动输入（如拼写错误、OCR噪声、同音词）下失败时，"哪一层负责"这一问题的答案取决于如何操作化定义。作者识别出三种自然但不同的操作化方式：敏感性（表示分歧最大的层）、因果性（恢复干净激活可恢复预测的层）和补偿能力（小适配器能修复损伤的层）。核心动机是探究这三种层映射是否一致，以及它们对未来模型诊断和修复策略（如适配器放置）的影响。

Method: 作者在五个模型（Phi-3.5、Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3、Mistral、Qwen2.5-7B）上定义了三种层映射：敏感性（表示分歧）、因果性（激活恢复）和补偿能力（适配器修复）。通过设定80%身份补丁门槛，分析了敏感性与因果性的相关性。在Qwen2.5家族（1.5B至14B）内进行规模扩展实验。提出"级联破坏"假设——在因果性早期层放置适配器会破坏完整的下游计算。使用固定测试框架在四个模型（3.8-8B）上进行层遍历，在GSM8K思维链任务和多项选择对照任务上验证预测。最后评估了表示稳定性损失的干预效果，并检查了思维链截断评估带来的偏差。

Result: 三种层映射相互分离，揭示出两种传播机制（尖峰-抑制型和晚期累积型）。敏感性与因果性呈负相关（rho=-0.72至-0.88）。规模扩展显示晚期累积特征随模型尺寸单调增强。级联破坏预测得到验证：诊断标记的层成为最差的适配器窗口，在GSM8K思维链任务上所有可裁决模型均确认，在多项选择对照中符号一致但强度衰减。实用建议包括免训练LRD预筛选和默认最深层放置规则，但相比无适配器基线的绝对收益较小。表示稳定性损失看似有效，但在充足生成预算下效果逆转——之前因思维链被截断计为空而得出错误结论。

Conclusion: 论文证明敏感性、因果性和补偿能力是三种分离且不可互换的层映射，用于理解模型在表面扰动下的失败模式。"级联破坏"机制解释了为何被诊断标记为因果性的层反而是最糟糕的适配器放置位置。研究还提供了一项重要的方法学警告：在思维链任务上评估干预措施时，必须确保充足的生成预算，否则截断的思维链（被计为空）会产生误导性的看似收益。这项研究对模型诊断和鲁棒性修复策略的设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: When a language model fails on surface-perturbed input (typos, OCR noise, homophones), "which layer is responsible" has three natural operationalizations: where representations diverge most (sensitivity), where restoring clean activations recovers the prediction (causality), and where a small adapter can repair the damage (compensatory capacity) - and we show these three layer maps dissociate. Across a five-model panel we identify two propagation regimes - spike-and-suppress (Phi-3.5, Gemma-2-9B) and late-accumulation (Llama-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5-7B) - and on the two models meeting an 80% identity-patch gate, sensitivity and causality are anti-correlated (rho = -0.72 to -0.88). Within-family scaling on Qwen2.5 (1.5B to 14B) shows the late-accumulation signature strengthening monotonically with scale, corroborated on a second family. We propose cascade disruption as the mechanism behind the dissociation: adapters placed at causally implicated early layers break intact downstream computation, making diagnostic-flagged sites the worst adapter placements. A fixed-harness layer sweep across four models (3.8-8B) confirms the core prediction on chain-of-thought GSM8K - the flagged sites are the most damaging adapter windows on every adjudicable model - and is sign-consistent but strongly attenuated on a multiple-choice control, consistent with damage that compounds with generation length. The sweep yields practical guidance: a training-free LRD pre-screen and a default-deepest placement rule, though absolute gains over no-adapter baselines remain small. Finally, apparent gains from a representation-stability loss reverse under an adequate generation budget - truncated chain-of-thought had been scored as empty - a methodological warning for any intervention evaluated on chain-of-thought tasks.

</details>


### [200] [Beyond Representational Similarity: Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain for Generative Plagiarism Detection and Candidate Source Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03859)
*Peijia Guo,Wenxuan Xie,ZiGuang Li,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCDG（Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain），一种无需训练的有向框架，通过对比可疑文档在有无候选源条件下的描述长度差异，来检测生成式剽窃中的源重用。在PAN基准上超越了所有基线，并在多源检索任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对学术诚信和同行评审构成挑战，但生成式剽窃检测仍未被充分探索。现有方法要么只检测AI参与（可能被允许）而非源重用，要么基于相似度的方法在大量重写和多源综合后失效。亟需一种能检测源特定内容重用的统一框架。

Method: 基于概率预测的描述长度视角，引入SCDG框架。其核心是对冻结语言模型在有无候选源S的条件下，对可疑文档P的描述长度进行对比，得到token级对数似然增益，从而衡量S提供的增量预测证据。该方法无需训练，具有方向性，且可分解到token级别。

Result: 在PAN 2025派生配对基准上取得0.92精确率、0.97召回率、0.94 F1分数，优于所有基线；在PAN 2026多源检索任务上达到0.83 nDCG@10和0.96 Recall@100，超越所有基线。在同主题同事件的Multi-News测试中，仅对0.125%的配对预测源重用，展现出对主题重叠的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SCDG被确立为一种统一且可token分解的信号，用于在大量变换条件下检测源特定内容重用，解决了生成式剽窃检测中源重用这一核心难题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) pose challenges to academic integrity and peer review. Yet generative plagiarism detection remains an underexplored and largely unresolved challenge. Prior work on LLM-generated-text detection targets AI involvement, which may be permissible, rather than source reuse, while similarity-based methods struggle after extensive rewriting and multi-source synthesis. Motivated by the description-length view of probabilistic prediction, in which relevant side information can reduce a target sequence's code length, we introduce Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain (SCDG), a directional, training-free framework that contrasts a frozen language model's description length of a suspicious document $P$ with and without a candidate source $S$. This contrast yields token-level log-likelihood gains that measure the incremental predictive evidence supplied by $S$. We evaluate SCDG on the PAN at CLEF benchmarks for generative plagiarism. On a PAN 2025-derived pairwise benchmark, SCDG achieves 0.92 Precision, 0.97 Recall, and 0.94 F1, outperforming all baselines; on PAN 2026's multi-source retrieval task, it reaches 0.83 nDCG@10 and 0.96 Recall@100, surpassing all baselines. On a same-topic, same-event Multi-News test, the calibrated gain-distribution SCDG classifier predicts source reuse for only $0.125\%$ of pairs, supporting robustness to topical overlap under this evaluation protocol. These results establish SCDG as a unified and token-decomposable signal for source-specific content reuse under extensive transformation.

</details>


### [201] [SciRet: A Compute-Aware Empirical Study of Retrieval and Reranking for Scientific RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03860)
*Kaysarul Anas Apurba,Md. Hasibul Hasan,Rofiqul Alam Shehab,Asab Azad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciRet，一个针对CORD-19科学问答任务的算力感知型检索增强生成（RAG）实证研究。该研究不提出新模型，而是固定一个科学RAG流水线，在三个语料规模（1K、5K、15K论文）下评估其性能。流水线结合了句子窗口分块、BM25稀疏检索、BGE-M3稠密检索、倒数排名融合、可选交叉编码器重排序和接地答案生成。结果显示混合检索比单一检索更稳健，MS MARCO训练的交叉编码器因领域不匹配而降低精度，生成忠实度随语料规模增大而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG研究通常只在小规模或单一语料上评估模型和检索方法，缺乏对计算资源与语料规模如何影响科学问答RAG系统性能的系统性理解。作者希望通过算力感知的实证研究，揭示不同语料规模下检索策略和生成质量的演变规律，为实际科学问答系统的设计提供依据。

Method: 采用固定的科学RAG流水线：句子窗口分块、BM25稀疏检索、BGE-M3稠密检索、倒数排名融合（RRF）、可选交叉编码器重排序、grounded答案生成。在CORD-19上构建三个语料规模（1,034块/1K论文、5,160块/5K论文、15,480块/15K论文），系统比较稀疏/稠密/混合检索的Recall@10、交叉编码器重排序的影响（使用MS MARCO预训练模型），以及RAGAS生成忠实度随规模的变化。检索评估采用基于混合系统生成的伪相关性标签。

Result: 混合检索在三种语料规模下均比单一稀疏或稠密检索更稳健，Recall@10在1K和15K时达到1.000。MS MARCO训练的交叉编码器在科学语料上反而降低了精度，说明领域不匹配可能抵消强查询-段落交互带来的收益。RAGAS测量的生成忠实度随语料规模增大而提高。

Conclusion: 混合检索是科学RAG的首选策略；领域不匹配是交叉编码器重排序的主要障碍，需要领域自适应；更大的语料规模有助于提升生成忠实度，支持在资源允许时扩大语料。同时，由于使用伪相关性标签，结果应视为受控比较证据而非基准声明，作者开源代码和索引以支持复现。

Abstract: We introduce SciRet, a compute-aware empirical study of retrieval-augmented generation for scientific question answering over CORD-19. Rather than proposing a new model, we evaluate a fixed scientific RAG pipeline across three corpus scales: 1,034 chunks (1K papers), 5,160 chunks (5K papers), and 15,480 chunks (15K papers). The pipeline combines sentence-window chunking, BM25, BGE-M3 dense retrieval, reciprocal rank fusion, optional cross-encoder reranking, and grounded answer generation.
