Across these settings, hybrid retrieval is more robust than either sparse-only or dense-only retrieval in our setting, reaching Recall@10 of 1.000 at 1K and 15K. In contrast, an MS MARCO-trained cross-encoder reranker reduces precision on the scientific corpus, suggesting that domain mismatch can outweigh the benefits of stronger query-passage interaction. Generation faithfulness measured with RAGAS increases with corpus scale in our setup. Retrieval evaluation uses pseudo-relevance labels derived from the hybrid system, so we treat the results as controlled comparative evidence rather than a benchmark claim. We release code, indexes, and evaluation outputs to support replication and follow-up studies.

</details>


### [202] [MultiGlobeQA: A Multilingual and Globally Diverse Benchmark for Geospatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03882)
*Martin Böckling,Elizaveta Nosova,Heiko Paulheim,Andreea Iana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiGlobeQA，一个包含46,060个问答对、覆盖14种空间函数族和15种答案格式的多语言地理空间推理基准，基于三个知识图谱构建执行级真实答案。实验发现LLM在网格索引和形状计算任务上表现瘫痪，在拓扑关系和方向任务上表现最佳；即使提供黄金事实，性能仍低于三分之二，表明计算而非知识获取是瓶颈，且模型对低收入地区表现更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅能部分定位LLM在空间推理上的失败：它们要么是合成或小规模数据，大多为单语言，且对地理覆盖的控制有限。需要一个大规模、多语言、可控地理覆盖的基准来全面评估和定位LLM的地理空间推理能力缺陷。

Method: 构建MultiGlobeQA基准：46,060个问答对，涵盖14种空间函数族和15种答案格式；基于三个知识图谱生成执行级真实答案；通过收入和人口密度分层采样覆盖201个国家和地区；提供英语及另外16种高/低资源语言的平行问题。在参数化、推理和智能体三种设置下进行评估。

Result: LLM在需要网格索引和形状计算的任务上表现崩溃，而拓扑关系和方向任务表现最佳。检索和工具使用带来显著提升，但即使提供黄金事实，性能仍低于三分之二，表明计算是瓶颈。模型在低收入地区表现更差，且提供黄金事实反而扩大而非缩小了这一差距。

Conclusion: 地理空间推理的瓶颈在于计算能力而非知识获取。即使给予完整的地理知识（黄金事实），LLM仍然无法完成所需的几何和拓扑计算。同时，模型在不同收入水平地区之间存在不平等表现，且该差距不能通过提供知识来弥补，需要更根本的计算能力改进。

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning, i.e., computing distances, containment, and other spatial relations over real-world entities, is central to navigation and logistics, yet large language models (LLMs) struggle with the required geometric and topological computation despite storing considerable geographic knowledge. Existing benchmarks localize these failures only partially: they are synthetic or smallscale, largely monolingual, and offer limited control over geographic coverage. We introduce MultiGlobeQA, a multilingual benchmark of 46,060 question-answer pairs spanning 14 spatial-function families and 15 answer formats, with execution-based ground truth over three knowledge graphs. It covers 201 countries and territories via income- and density-stratified sampling, with parallel questions in English and 16 additional high- and low-resource languages. Across parametric, reasoning, and agentic settings, LLMs collapse on tasks requiring grid indexing and shape computation, while topological relations and directions fare best. Retrieval and tool use yield considerable gains, yet performance plateaus below two thirds even when gold facts are supplied, indicating that computation, not access to knowledge, is the bottleneck. Models also underperform on low-income regions, a gap that gold facts widen rather than close.

</details>


### [203] [DS@GT-ARC at eRisk 2026 Task 3: Sparse, Semantic, and LLM Reranking for ADHD Symptom Sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03883)
*David Guecha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了作者团队在eRisk 2026任务3（ADHD症状句子排序）中的提交方案。由于该任务首次发布且未提供标注训练数据，作者采用了零样本实验、人工验证和无监督/弱引导的检索管道，组合了BM25稀疏检索、证据感知重打分、嵌入重排序、查询原型扩展和LLM重排序等多种技术。所有提交系统采用分阶段检索设计，其中LLM重排序器取得最佳官方成绩，人工top-10分析与官方专家评分趋势一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eRisk 2026任务3要求系统根据ASRS-v1.1中18种成人ADHD症状的相关性对Reddit候选句子进行排序。由于这是该任务的首次发布，没有标注训练数据可用，因此需要在零样本条件下开发有效的检索与重排序方案。

Method: 采用分阶段检索（staged retrieval）设计：先用BM25在语料规模上进行候选句子的初始检索，然后用语义重排序器（嵌入重排序）、LLM重排序器、证据感知重打分（针对自我指涉的症状报告）以及查询原型扩展等方法进行精细排序。所有提交系统遵循统一的BM25召回+语义/LLM重排序的分阶段框架。

Result: 在提交的所有系统中，LLM重排序器取得了最强的官方分数，其次是原型查询扩展的运行版本。人工top-10分析结果与官方专家评分趋势一致，验证了方法的一致性。

Conclusion: 分阶段重排序是ADHD症状句子排序任务中有前景的发展方向，LLM重排序在零样本条件下表现最优，未来可在此基础上进一步优化。

Abstract: This paper describes our submissions to eRisk 2026 Task 3, ADHD Symptom Sentence Ranking. The task requires systems to rank candidate Reddit sentences according to their relevance to each of the 18 symptoms in the Adult ADHD Self-Report Scale (ASRS-v1.1). Because no annotated training data were released for this first edition of the task, we relied on zero-shot experimentation, manual validation, and unsupervised or weakly guided retrieval pipelines. Our systems combine sparse BM25 retrieval, evidence-aware rescoring for self-referential symptom reports, embedding-based reranking, query-prototype expansion, and LLM-based reranking. All submitted systems follow a staged retrieval design in which BM25 retrieves candidates at scale and semantic or LLM rerankers refine the final rankings. Among our submissions, the LLM reranker achieved the strongest official scores, followed by the prototype query-expansion run. Our manual top-10 analysis aligned with the official expert scoring trend, suggesting that staged reranking is a promising direction for further development.

</details>


### [204] [ANNOTARES: A Dataset for Extracting Logical Structures from German Statutory Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03898)
*Ronja Schwarz,Jannik Strötgen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了德语法规文本中法律要件（Tatbestand）与法律后果（Rechtsfolge）的识别与切分任务，并发布了包含跨度级标注的新型数据集ANNOTARES，涵盖三个法律法典。通过对规则基线、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF以及BERT变体和LLM等多种方法进行基准测试，发现BERT和基于LLM的模型在捕捉法律语言复杂句法结构方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本的自动结构分析是法律技术的基石，但从法律文本中提取逻辑组成成分（即法律要件与法律后果）仍然是一个重大挑战，目前缺乏针对这一任务的系统性研究及相应数据集。

Method: 构建ANNOTARES数据集，包含德语法律文本的跨度级标注，覆盖三个不同法典以评估领域内表现与跨法规泛化能力；随后对不同架构进行基准测试，包括基于规则的方法、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF以及基于Transformer的模型（BERT变体和LLM方法）。

Result: BERT和基于LLM的模型在识别法律要件与法律后果方面取得了最佳性能，显著优于传统序列标注方法和规则基线，能够更好地捕捉法律语言的复杂句法结构。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的预训练模型（尤其是BERT和LLM）是处理法律文本逻辑结构抽取任务的最有效方法；数据集已公开以促进自动化法律推理领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: The automatic structural analysis of legal texts is a cornerstone of legal technology, yet the extraction of their logical components remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we introduce the task of identifying and segmenting legal conditions (Tatbestand) and legal consequences (Rechtsfolge) within German statutory texts. To support this task, we present ANNOTARES (Annotations of Tatbestand-Rechtsfolge Sequences), a novel dataset comprising German law texts with span-level annotations. Spanning three distinct legal codes, the dataset is designed to evaluate both domain-specific performance and cross-statute generalizability. We benchmark diverse architectural approaches: a rule-based baseline, CRFs, BiLSTMs, BiLSTM-CRF, and modern Transformer-based models, including BERT variants and LLM-based methods. Our results demonstrate that BERT and LLM-based models achieve superior performance in capturing the complex syntactic structures of legal language. We release our dataset to facilitate further research in automated legal reasoning.

</details>


### [205] [Logic Before Language: Pre-pretraining on Formal Derivations Fosters Skill Acquisition and Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03930)
*Jo-Ku Cheng,Nikolaos Aletras,Marco Valentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出逻辑预预训练（Logic-PPT）策略，利用形式化推导为语言模型注入丰富的结构与语言先验，在100B token规模下显著加速语言技能习得，并揭示了形式化推导诱导的低秩、谱集中表示空间与模型可压缩性之间的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预预训练任务（如Dyck语言和程序化算法）依赖狭窄的原始操作，无法捕捉自然语言的表达能力；且以往研究局限于较小的token预算，难以观察技能涌现与表征动态。

Method: 提出Logic-PPT初始化策略，利用形式化推导（formal derivations）作为预预训练数据，其要求同时绑定变量、连接量词与关系依赖、在长上下文中组合谓词-论元结构，从而赋予模型更接近自然语言的抽象机制。

Result: 在100B token规模下，Logic-PPT使模型在语言任务上以比标准初始化少36B token即达到80%准确率，优于其他预预训练基线。机制层面，形式化推导诱导了持续的、低秩且谱集中的表示空间重组，且该几何特性使模型在约33%稀疏度下通过剪枝仍能匹配稠密基线性能。

Conclusion: 逻辑预预训练是一种原则性的初始化方法，不仅能显著加速语言技能习得，其诱导的表示几何还提升了模型的可压缩性，为理解预预训练的内部表征机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Pre-pretraining language models (LMs) on symbolic data can accelerate and improve natural language acquisition. However, existing pre-pretraining tasks, such as Dyck and procedural algorithms, rely on narrow primitives that fail to capture the expressive capacity of natural language. Moreover, prior studies remain restricted to relatively small token budgets, offering limited insight into skill emergence and representational dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose logic pre-pretraining (Logic-PPT) as a principled initialization strategy, leveraging formal derivations to impart richer structural and linguistic biases. Formal derivations require abstract mechanisms that are central to natural language, simultaneously binding variables, connecting quantifiers and relational dependencies, and composing predicate-argument structures over long contexts. Scaling our evaluation to a 100B-token regime, logic pre-pretraining substantially accelerates skill acquisition in LMs, achieving 80\% accuracy on linguistic tasks with 36B fewer tokens than standard initialization, and outperforming alternative pre-pretraining baselines. Mechanistically, formal derivations induce persistent structural reorganization, distinctively characterized by a lower-rank, spectrally concentrated representation space. Crucially, we show that this internal geometry enables improved model compressibility via pruning, matching the dense baseline performance even at $\approx$33\% sparsity.

</details>


### [206] [HalluTruthQA-4K: A Fine-Grained Corpus and Annotation Process for Arabic Hallucination Detection and Truth Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03966)
*Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Hichem Telli,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Abdenour Hadid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HalluTruthQA-4K是一个包含4,000条阿拉伯语问答实例的专家策划幻觉检测资源，涵盖伊斯兰知识、历史、科学和地理四个知识密集型领域。每个实例包含阿拉伯语问题、模型生成回复、参考答案和五个干扰项，幻觉响应额外标注了字符级别的错误跨度、人工解释和分层幻觉类型。该语料库作为HalluScoring 2026共享任务Track 2的官方数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阿拉伯语幻觉资源仅对整个响应分配二元标签（幻觉或非幻觉），无法提供关于确切错误内容、错误原因或正确事实答案的详细信息，难以满足幻觉检测、定位和验证的细粒度需求。

Method: 通过问题选择、受控答案生成、候选构造、专家注释、独立验证、裁决和质量控制等流程构建资源。注释包括字符级错误跨度、人工解释和分层幻觉类型，并记录了注释指南、分类体系、数据格式和注释者间一致性。

Result: 语料库包含1,643条幻觉响应和2,357条非幻觉响应，共标注了1,843个错误跨度，扩展到4,000条实例。

Conclusion: HalluTruthQA-4K为阿拉伯语语言模型提供了可复用的幻觉检测、跨度级错误定位、解释生成、事实验证以及事实可靠性评估的资源。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent Arabic answers while introducing factual errors that are difficult to identify and verify. Existing Arabic hallucination resources often assign a binary label to an entire response, indicating whether it is hallucinated or non-hallucinated, but provide limited information about the exact erroneous content, the reason for the error, or the correct factual answer. We present HalluTruthQA-4K, an expanded version of the HalluTruthQA resource containing 4,000 expert-curated Arabic question-answering instances across four knowledge-intensive domains: Islamic knowledge, history, science, and geography. Serving as the official dataset for Track 2 of the HalluScoring 2026 shared task, HalluTruthQA-4K extends our original corpus to 4,000 instances. Each instance pairs an Arabic question with a model-generated response, a verified reference answer, and five plausible distractors. Hallucinated responses are additionally annotated with character-level erroneous spans, human-written explanations, and hierarchical hallucination types. The corpus contains 1,643 hallucinated and 2,357 non-hallucinated responses, with 1,843 annotated erroneous spans. We describe the resource construction and annotation methodology, including question selection, controlled answer generation, candidate construction, expert annotation, independent verification, adjudication, and quality control. We also document the annotation guidelines, taxonomy, data format, inter-annotator agreement, and corpus statistics. HalluTruthQA-4K provides a reusable resource for hallucination detection, span-level error localization, explanation generation, factual verification, and the broader evaluation of factual reliability in Arabic language models.

</details>


### [207] [string2string Studio: An Interactive, In-Browser Platform for String-to-String Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03984)
*Mirac Suzgun,James Zou,Stuart M. Shieber,Dan Jurafsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 string2string Studio，一个基于浏览器的交互式字符串到字符串分析平台，支持自然语言处理、计算生物学和数字人文领域的对齐、距离、相似度、搜索、生成指标和 BLAST 同源搜索六大模块，其 C++ 算法编译为 WebAssembly 在本地运行，无需安装或上传数据，性能较 Python 前身提升最高 2500 倍，且与独立参考实现精确一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字符串分析方法分散于不同工具和编程环境中，缺乏一个统一的、可交互的、可检查的、可比较的平台来展示和分析各类字符串到字符串算法，同时需要兼顾性能、可访问性与数据隐私。

Method: 构建一个开源浏览器内平台，将核心字符串算法以 C++ 实现并编译为 WebAssembly，使其默认在本地运行；系统包含六大模块（对齐、距离、相似度、搜索、生成指标、BLAST 同源搜索），支持字符、词、token、行和残基五个粒度级别；界面呈现分数及其"证据"（对齐、编辑路径、指标匹配、搜索命中和同源轨迹），并提供 Learn 模式和展示案例。

Result: 内部基准测试显示，相较 Python 前身实现最高提速 2500 倍；全局/局部对齐速度优于通用原生 C 对齐器；在声明设置下与独立参考完全一致；本地 blastn 路径在匹配参数下与 NCBI BLAST+ 的排名和统计紧密吻合。

Conclusion: string2string Studio 是一个开源、自由可得、跨领域可用的交互式字符串分析平台，兼具高性能、可检查性和可复现性，适用于教育、研究与开发场景。

Abstract: We present string2string Studio, an interactive in-browser platform for string-to-string analysis across natural language processing, computational biology, and the digital humanities. The system integrates six main modules (alignment, distance, similarity, search, generation metrics, and BLAST homology search), operating at character, word, token, line, and residue levels. Its C++-based algorithms compile to WebAssembly, so core operations run locally by default without any installation or data upload. The interface reports scores with their "evidence" (alignments, edit paths, metric matches, search hits, and homology traces), making methods inspectable, debuggable, and comparable on shared inputs. Internal benchmarks show speedups of up to 2,500x over the Python predecessor, faster global/local alignment than a general-purpose native C aligner, and exact agreement with independent references under declared settings. For homology search, the scoped client-side blastn path closely matches NCBI BLAST+ rankings and statistics under matched parameters. A curated showcase and Learn mode present canonical algorithms and metrics as reusable demonstrations. string2string Studio is open-source and freely available at string2string.org.

</details>


### [208] [When Attention Goes Blind: Numerical Failure in ALiBi Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03994)
*Christopher Schröder,Lukas Gienapp,Ferdinand Schlatt,Martin Potthast,Gerhard Heyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示ALiBi位置编码的线性偏置缩放存在浮点精度下溢问题，导致大量注意力权重归零，使受影响注意力头部分失明。作者通过148M参数解码器模型的全面预训练实验，分离该失效模式与上下文外退化，提出四种训练时缓解策略（对数缩放距离效果最佳），并发现默认ALiBi斜率在passkey检索中仍是强基线，最终给出具体训练建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALiBi位置编码存在一个被忽视的失效模式：其线性偏置缩放会因浮点精度下溢而将大量注意力权重置零，导致注意力头"部分失明"，影响token检索能力。此前研究未系统分析该问题及其影响。

Method: 1) 分析并刻画ALiBi下溢失效模式的成因与影响；2) 在148M参数解码器模型上进行全面预训练实验，将失效模式与上下文外(out-of-context)退化效应分离；3) 提出四种训练时缓解策略（对数缩放距离等），并单独及组合评估。

Result: 发现ALiBi失效模式会显著损害token检索，但对标准解码器基准测试影响较小；对数缩放距离策略在passkey检索中带来最一致的改进；默认ALiBi斜率仍是令人惊讶的强基线，尤其在needle-in-a-haystack检索场景中。

Conclusion: 尽管ALiBi存在下溢失效问题，但默认斜率在部分场景下仍表现优异。作者基于实验发现，为使用ALiBi训练模型提供了具体建议，指出训练时缓解策略（尤其对数缩放距离）可以有效改善token检索性能。

Abstract: We identify a previously overlooked failure mode of ALiBi positional encoding: its linear bias scaling underflows floating-point precision, which zeroes out a large fraction of attention weights and renders the affected attention heads partially blind. We analyze this failure mode, characterize its impact, and examine four mitigation strategies. We further demonstrate its occurrence in state-of-the-art pretrained models based on ALiBi. Comprehensive pretraining experiments with 148M-parameter decoder models help us to disentangle its effects from out-of-context degradation. We find that ALiBi's failure mode can substantially impair token retrieval while having only a minor effect on standard decoder benchmarks. We propose four training-time mitigation strategies and evaluate them individually and in combinations, finding that log-scaled distances yield the most consistent improvements in passkey retrieval. Despite this problem, default ALiBi slopes remain a surprisingly strong baseline, particularly for needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. Based on these findings we provide concrete recommendations on how to train models with ALiBi.

</details>


### [209] [PAST-Bench: Benchmarking the Foundations of Recursive Self-Improvement in Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04003)
*Shuhan Xue,Zixin Ding,Yichen Shen,Yinjie Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Yuxin Chen,Mengdi Wang,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAST-Bench基准，用于系统性评估个人AI智能体能否通过跨会话保留的经验实现递归自我改进，并基于发现的"改进真实但不均衡"问题开发了Hermes+方法，在保留经验的平均增益和路径证据方面取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进要求智能体将累积经验转化为更好的未来行为，但"保留的经验是否真的能随时间提升智能体性能"这一问题尚未被系统性地测试。个人AI智能体虽然天然具备跨会话的经验保留能力，却缺乏一个能隔离和量化这种改进效果的标准化评估框架。

Method: 提出PAST-Bench基准，让每个智能体在匹配条件下（开启/关闭经验保留）运行有序的、全新会话的任务序列，跨越26个场景和204个episode，覆盖记忆、程序复用、信息收集和更新四类能力。同时报告后期任务增益及增益是否遵循"保存-检索-更新"的预期路径。基于PAST-Bench的发现，开发Hermes+，在Hermes基础上针对智能体循环的各个阶段引入五项针对性干预。

Result: 在7个基础模型和4个智能体框架上，改进是真实存在的但不均衡（因能力而异）。具有相同总体增益的智能体在增益是否由预期路径的证据支撑方面可能差异显著。Hermes+提高了保留经验的平均增益，提供了更清晰的路径证据，在需要替换过时状态的任务上增益最强，但效果仍依赖具体能力和模型。

Conclusion: PAST-Bench和Hermes+共同为研究"持久智能体如何从保留经验走向系统性改进"提供了评估与诊断基础，揭示了递归自我改进中"真实但能力/模型依赖"的特性，为后续工作奠定了方向。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires agents to turn accumulated experience into better future behavior. Personal AI agents offer a concrete setting for studying this capability because they retain preferences, task histories, tool routines, and learned skills across sessions. Yet whether retained experience actually improves them over time has not been systematically tested. We introduce PAST-Bench, a benchmark designed to isolate this question. Each agent runs through ordered sequences of fresh-session tasks under matched conditions that turn retained experience on and off. It spans 26 scenarios and 204 episodes across memory, procedural reuse, information gathering, and update. We report both later-task gains and whether those gains follow the intended save, retrieve, and update pathway. Across seven base models and four agent frameworks, improvement is real but uneven across capabilities. Agents with the same headline gain can differ markedly in whether that gain is supported by evidence of the intended pathway. Guided by these findings, we develop Hermes+, which extends Hermes with five targeted interventions across stages of the agent loop. Hermes+ raises the average gain from retained experience and provides clearer pathway evidence, with its strongest improvement on tasks requiring outdated state to be replaced, although the effect remains capability- and model-dependent. Together, PAST-Bench and Hermes+ provide an evaluation and diagnostic foundation for studying how persistent agents can progress from retaining experience to systematically improving through it. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/PAST-Bench

</details>


### [210] [TurnSight: Turn-Level Hindsight Self-Distillation for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04007)
*Changle Qu,Sunhao Dai,Hengyi Cai,Yuqi Zhou,Xinran Chen,Simon,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TurnSight，一个基于回合级事后自我蒸馏的框架，用于工具集成推理中的强化学习，通过执行条件事后视角构建多种前瞻视野的监督信号，利用跨视野方向一致性筛选可靠监督，并在兄弟轨迹间归一化后自适应调制RL优势，在三个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具集成推理强化学习方法依赖轨迹级监督，无法在长时程场景中进行细粒度信用分配；基于策略的自我蒸馏虽能提供更密集的信号，但通常从地面真值答案或检索技能中获取教师上下文，无法反映代理实际访问的状态；此外，token级监督无法捕捉工具交互的回合级结构。

Method: 提出TurnSight框架：(1) 从执行条件事后视角直接推导回合级监督信号；(2) 构造不同前瞻视野的多种事后视角，通过跨视野方向一致性选择可靠监督；(3) 将选定的事后信号在兄弟rollout之间归一化，用于自适应调节RL优势，同时保持原始优化方向。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了TurnSight的有效性。

Conclusion: TurnSight通过回合级事后自我蒸馏解决了现有方法在信用分配、上下文匹配和结构捕捉方面的不足，为工具集成推理中的强化学习提供了更有效的监督信号。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) enables LLMs to solve complex tasks through iterative tool interactions. However, existing reinforcement learning methods often rely on trajectory-level supervision, limiting fine-grained credit assignment in long-horizon TIR scenarios. On-policy self-distillation offers denser signals through teacher branches with privileged context, but existing approaches typically derive such context from ground-truth answers or retrieved skills, which may not reflect the states actually visited by the agent. Moreover, token-level supervision fails to capture the turn-level structure of tool interactions. To address this, we propose TurnSight, a turn-level hindsight self-distillation framework that derives supervision directly from execution-conditioned hindsight. It then constructs multiple hindsight views with different lookahead horizons and selects reliable supervision through cross-horizon directional agreement. Finally, the selected hindsight signal is normalized across sibling rollouts and used to adaptively modulate RL advantages while preserving their original optimization direction. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of TurnSight. Our codes are available at https://github.com/quchangle1/TurnSight.

</details>


### [211] [WorldCup Arena: Prospective, Leakage-Free Evaluation of Frontier LLMs on a Live Tournament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04008)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个前瞻性（非回顾性）的LLM预测能力基准：在2026年FIFA世界杯的39天赛程中，六个前沿LLM在每场比赛前被要求预测104场比赛的七个市场盘口、12个小组出线队及赛前冠军池，共产生4,494个已计分的预测。由于问题提出时答案尚不存在，该基准结构上天然无数据泄漏。结果显示这些系统在比赛结果上平均63.9%的正确率，与押注博彩公司热门持平；模型间一致性强于正确率、多数投票无增益；对平局和进球数预测保守、比分预测过于集中；准确率与赛程悬殊度相关而非信息量；六模型整体差异化不大，且该基准（含简报、赛程、官方结果与计分代码）已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有衡量LLM预测能力的基准几乎都是回顾性的——事件已经发生、答案已在网络上，评估必须费力防记忆污染。本文旨在设计一种反其道而行的评估方式：在问题提出时答案尚不存在，从而在结构上（而非过滤上）实现零泄漏，真正检验LLM的前瞻性预测能力。

Method: 采用FIFA世界杯赛事作为前瞻性预测场景。六个具备扩展推理和原生服务端网络搜索的前沿LLM，在39天内每场比赛开球前被逐一询问，要求填写7个市场盘口预测卡，覆盖全部104场比赛、12个小组出线队及赛前冠军池。所有预测在赛前冻结存档，共4,494个预测，答案在提问时不存在，保证测试无泄漏。

Result: 比赛结果的预测平均准确率为63.9%，与直接押注博彩公司热门持平（这通常正是它们所做的）。模型间一致程度远高于正确率，多数投票无增益。系统对平局和进球数预测不足，比分预测过度集中于单一原型结果。准确率随赛程悬殊度变化而非信息丰富度，在最胶着的比赛中准确率暴跌（尽管此类比赛背景资料最丰富），而对整个赛事的宏观问题回答良好。六个模型间差异不显著：顶部和底部排名稳定、中间排名浮动，且全程差距狭窄。

Conclusion: 当前一代前沿LLM在此预测任务上未被显著区分，其表现本质上接近简单跟注博彩热门策略，而非真正的独立预测能力。本文同时发布了包含简报、赛程、官方结果和计分代码的完整基准数据集，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Benchmarks that measure the forecasting ability of large language models are almost always retrospective: the event has happened, the answer is somewhere on the Web, and the evaluation must defend itself against memorisation. We report the opposite design. Over the 39 days of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, six frontier LLMs -- all with extended thinking and native server-side web search -- were asked before every kickoff, one match at a time, to fill in a seven-market prediction card for all 104 matches, plus 12 group winners and a pre-tournament outright pool; no answer existed when the question was asked, so the evaluation is leakage-free by construction rather than by filtering, and the frozen archive holds 4,494 scored predictions. What the tournament establishes is a set of behaviours the six systems share. On match outcome they average 63.9%, level with backing the bookmaker's favourite -- which is in fact what they usually do. They agree with one another far more often than they are right, so a majority vote adds nothing. They under-commit to draws and to goals, and crowd their scoreline picks onto a single prototypical result. Accuracy tracks how lopsided a fixture is rather than how much is known about it: it collapses in the closest ties, where the dossiers are richest, while questions about the tournament as a whole are answered well. On this task the current generation of frontier systems is not sharply differentiated: the standings hold up at the top and the bottom across the run and churn in the middle, and the margins stay narrow throughout. The briefing dossiers, fixtures and official results are released as a benchmark, together with the scoring code.

</details>


### [212] [SocietyBench: Forecasting Counterfactual Social-World Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04009)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SocietyBench是一个端到端的社会事件预测基准，从网页新闻和社交媒体帖子中构建日期索引的时间线，并生成经过审计的预测问题库。通过对真实事件进行实体替换和日期平移来构建反事实社会世界，在两种正交轴（概率校准和时间准确性）上评估模型。最强的前沿LLM仅达到75.0/100分，代理框架未能超越基础模型，且不同事件间性能差距显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准主要衡量任务完成能力（修bug、操作系统等），但缺乏对模型理解与预测真实社会事件演变这一互补社会能力的评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立衡量模型社会事件预测能力的标准化基准。

Method: SocietyBench采用端到端流程：输入一行事件主题，从五个平台的Web新闻和社交媒体帖子中收集数据，提炼为按日期索引的时间线（区分事实事件层与公众舆论层），每个截止日期转化为经过审计的预测问题库。通过三阶段流程替换所有命名实体并按事件常数平移日期，将真实事件弧转化为反事实社会世界，防止模型通过预训练记忆匹配表面标签。评分采用两个正交的100分轴：概率校准和时间准确性。

Result: 在5个异构事件和125个预测点上（中英文版本），六个前沿LLM中最强者仅达到75.0/100分（简单锚点50分）。两个轴可分离：模型可能校准强但时间弱，或反之。基于共享基础模型构建的三个代理框架未能超越基础模型，两个无模型启发式方法落后于所有LLM。单一轴上的事件间差距可达21.4分。

Conclusion: 当前最强LLM的社会事件预测能力仍远未饱和，代理框架并未带来增益，且跨事件评估至关重要（单一事件评估可能产生误导性结论）。所有匿名化时间线、问题库、真实答案和评分代码均已开源发布。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), and the agents built on top of them, are now benchmarked heavily on whether they can finish a task -- fix a bug, drive a browser, operate a GUI. A complementary social ability, namely how well a model understands and forecasts the way real social events unfold, has barely been measured. We introduce SocietyBench, an end-to-end benchmark that takes a one-line event topic, collects Web news and social-media posts across five platforms, distills them into a date-indexed timeline that keeps factual events and a public-opinion layer separate, and then turns every cutoff date on that timeline into an audited bank of forecasting questions. Questions are scored on two orthogonal 100-point axes: probability calibration and temporal accuracy. Before any model sees a timeline, a three-phase procedure replaces every named entity and shifts every date by a per-event constant, turning a real arc into a counterfactual social world -- structurally identical to what happened, but stripped of the surface labels a model could match against pre-training memory. On five heterogeneous events and 125 prediction points in Chinese and English editions, the strongest of six frontier LLMs reaches only 75.0 out of 100, against a trivial anchor of 50. The two axes come apart: a model can be calibration-strong but time-weak, or the reverse. Three agent frameworks built on a shared base model fail to improve on that base, and two model-free heuristics trail every LLM. Per-event gaps reach 21.4 points on a single axis, which is our main argument for evaluating on several events rather than one. All anonymized timelines, question banks, ground truth, and scoring code are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [TraceCAD: Trace-Guided Repair for Agentic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03062)
*Fengxiao Fan,Jingzhe Ni,Fan Sang,Xiaolong Yin,Yu Liu,Ruofeng Tong,Min Tang,Peng Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceCAD是一个针对LLM驱动的CAD智能体设计的恢复层，通过持久化状态将请求特征、建模步骤、失败证据和候选结果关联起来，实现可诊断、可定位、可验证和可复用的修复机制。在DeepCAD基准上，TraceCAD在IoU、Chamfer距离和Hausdorff距离等几何指标上达到了竞争性质量；移除持久化状态会使恢复分数减半，移除局部搜索会使几何回归翻倍并使代码智能体调用翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的CAD智能体虽然能生成可执行的参数化程序，但其纠错循环会丢失关于已满足需求、故障操作和先前修复的关键证据，导致修复过程缺乏依据、效率低下，无法有效定位和解决问题。

Method: TraceCAD提出一个恢复层，包含四个核心组件：1) 持久化状态，将请求特征、建模步骤、失败证据和候选结果关联；2) 诊断机制，识别可能出错的可行操作；3) 局部搜索，在故障操作的依赖区域内进行边界受限编辑；4) 验证流程，通过执行和保留检查验证候选方案，并将成功与失败的修复结果存入可复用的技能记忆库。

Result: 在DeepCAD推导的基准上（200模型消融实验和1000模型对比实验），TraceCAD在IoU、Chamfer距离和Hausdorff距离等几何质量指标上达到竞争性水平。消融实验表明：移除持久化状态使恢复分数几乎减半；移除局部搜索使几何回归指标翻倍且代码智能体调用次数翻倍；在不相交训练模型上初始化技能库可进一步减少重试次数、降低token成本和延迟。

Conclusion: 持久化、局部化和可复用的恢复机制能够显著提升最终CAD质量与修复可靠性，验证了TraceCAD作为CAD智能体恢复层的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based CAD agents produce executable parametric programs, but their correction loops may lose evidence about satisfied requirements, faulty operations, and prior repairs. We introduce TraceCAD, a recovery layer that links requested features, modeling steps, failure evidence, and candidate outcomes as persistent state. TraceCAD diagnoses likely faulty operations, searches bounded edits in their dependency regions, validates candidates through execution and preservation checks, and retains successful and failed repair outcomes in reusable skill memory. On DeepCAD-derived benchmarks with 200-model ablations and a 1K-model comparison, TraceCAD achieves competitive geometric quality in terms of IoU, Chamfer distance, and Hausdorff distance. Removing persistent state nearly halves recovery score; removing localized search more than doubles geometric regression and doubles code-agent invocations. Initializing the skill store on disjoint training models further reduces retries, token cost, and latency. These results demonstrate that persistent, localized, and reusable recovery improves final CAD quality and repair reliability.

</details>


### [214] [ISEE: Interactive Semantic Enrichment for Database Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02604)
*Yuan Tian,Yiru Chen,Rakesh R. Menon,Zifan Liu,Ting Cai,Fei Wu,Anudeep Chimakurthi,Prashanthi Ramamurthy,Sridevi Aishwariya Ganesan,Kun Qian,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交互式语义增强系统ISEE，用于解决LLM智能体在数据任务中因字段描述模糊或不完整而导致的性能下降问题。ISEE通过评分系统衡量描述质量，收集领域知识并与用户协作增强数据语义，实验证明其能显著降低认知负荷、提高描述质量并改善下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在数据相关任务（如数据理解、探索和检索）中的表现高度依赖数据语义的清晰度和完整性。然而实际中许多字段描述模糊或不完整，因为关键的上下文信息（如自定义字段的含义）来源于用户的领域知识，很少被公开记录。这种语义缺口限制了智能体在下游任务（如实体链接）中的性能表现。

Method: 提出ISEE（交互式语义增强系统），包含三个核心模块：1）评分系统用于衡量数据字段描述的质量；2）领域知识收集机制；3）与用户协作的语义增强流程。评估方法包括用户研究、自动化用户模拟、量化评估和案例研究。

Result: 实验结果表明ISEE系统能够显著降低用户的认知负荷，提升字段描述质量，并增强下游任务（如实体链接）的性能表现。

Conclusion: ISEE通过人机协作的方式有效弥合了数据语义缺口，为LLM智能体在数据任务中的应用提供了语义层面的支撑，验证了交互式语义增强方法的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed for data-related tasks, including data sense-making, exploration, and retrieval. However, their performance heavily depends on the clarity and completeness of data semantics. In practice, many field descriptions remain ambiguous or incomplete, as much of the essential context (e.g., the meaning of a customized field) originates from users' domain knowledge and is rarely documented publicly. This gap restricts the agents' task performance in downstream tasks, such as entity-linking. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel and comprehensive Interactive SEmantic Enrichment system (ISEE). Given a data field description, ISEE measures its quality through a scoring system, gathers domain knowledge, and collaboratively enriches the semantics with users. Through a user study, automated user simulation, quantitative evaluation, and case study, we demonstrate that ISEE significantly reduces cognitive load, improves description quality, and enhances downstream task performance.

</details>


### [215] [Self-Organising Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02606)
*Marcello Barylli,Gabriel Béna,Alexander Mordvintsev,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自组织数字电路（Self-Organising Digital Circuits），将功能逻辑生成与维护建模为图上的元学习问题。采用拓扑掩码Transformer配置电路布尔门的查找表（LUT），扩展神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式，从零开始自组装功能电路，并能快速在永久性硬件故障周围重新路由逻辑。对软错误策略实现了超过99.99%准确率的近乎完美恢复，且泛化到远超训练条件的更宽电路图上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经典计算的容错依赖静态策略（如硬件冗余和纠错码），而生物系统通过动态重组实现自适应可塑性。作者希望借鉴生物自组织原理，构建能在硬件损坏后自主重新配置的适应性数字电路，从而突破静态容错方案的局限。

Method: 将电路功能逻辑生成与维护形式化为图上的元学习问题；采用拓扑掩码Transformer架构来配置布尔门的LUT；扩展NCA模式生成范式，在退化布尔搜索空间中导航以满足计算任务而非再生固定目标状态。

Result: 模型能从零开始自组装功能电路；能针对永久性未知硬件故障快速重新路由逻辑；对于软错误，在损伤尺寸远超训练条件的情况下实现>99.99%准确率的恢复；在比训练图更宽的电路图上表现出跨尺度泛化，精度反而提升。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了生物自组织原理与数字硬件实际应用之间的鸿沟，展示了自适应、可自我修复的数字电路的可能性，为未来鲁棒硬件设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in classical computing has traditionally relied on static strategies like hardware redundancy and error-correcting codes. Biological systems, in contrast, exhibit adaptive plasticity, maintaining function through dynamic re-organisation around damage. Inspired by this principle, we introduce Self-Organising Digital Circuits, framing functional logic generation and maintenance as a meta-learning problem on graphs. Our architecture employs a topology-masked Transformer that configures the Lookup Tables (LUT) of a circuit's Boolean gates. Extending the pattern-generation paradigm of Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), it navigates the degenerate Boolean search space to satisfy a computational task, rather than regenerating a fixed target state. We demonstrate that it can self-assemble functional circuits from scratch and rapidly re-route logic around permanent, previously unseen hardware faults. For soft errors, the policy achieves near-perfect recovery (>99.99\% accuracy) from damage sizes far exceeding training conditions. We further observe generalisation across circuit scales: accuracy improves on graphs substantially wider than those seen during training. This work bridges the principles of biological self-organisation with the practical domain of digital hardware.

</details>


### [216] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"元人格锚定结合过滤温度缩放（FTS）"的两阶段框架，用于缓解大语言模型中的"人工蜂巢思维"效应（即模型输出过度同质化的语义坍缩问题），将平均余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，显著提升AI输出的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM存在"人工蜂巢思维"效应，即即使在高采样温度下，模型对开放性问题也会收敛到狭窄、同质化的共识输出，导致inter-response相似度高达0.80-0.90。这种语义坍缩限制了AI的多样性，与人类层面的类型学多样性存在差距。

Method: 提出两阶段生成框架：(1) 元人格锚定阶段——提示模型自我选择独特的个性化人格来锚定生成起点；(2) 过滤温度缩放（FTS）阶段——采用双重采样筛子，先用Top-p过滤保留语法有效性，再对幸存候选应用极端温度缩放（T>=4.0）以探索拓宽的概率分布。

Result: 在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上，对约20B参数的先进开源模型进行评估，平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，大多数问题的相似度降至0.7阈值以下，显著减少了语义收敛。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缩小了人工模式坍缩与人类类型学多样性之间的差距，并以开源框架形式发布，旨在支持更多样化、更具创造性的AI部署。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [217] [PULSE: An Executable Contract Language for Spatiotemporal Knowledge Graph Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02630)
*Dongxu Yang,Ziyi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PULSE是一种受OPM（对象-过程方法论）启发的语言，将知识图谱工程中的四种操作角色及其写入效应本地化到一个类型化运行时中，通过核心演算证明效果隔离引理和六个安全性质，并在多个实际数据集上验证了追踪一致性和安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱工程中，可接受状态、观测、约束、过程和假设场景通常分布在多个构件中，使得组合执行契约外部化而不明确。作者希望将这些操作角色及其写效应集中在一个统一的类型化运行时中，以契约本地化方式来保证安全性和一致性。

Method: 提出PULSE语言，借鉴OPM（对象-过程方法论）设计四种操作角色（mode），在单一类型化运行时中实现：证据不可覆写、分支隔离、基于多主体的接地定时器、受保护状态变更、按声明级别排序的事件调度。外部运行器决定证据是否成为权威动作；GeoSPARQL、SOSA和SHACL作为生成视图。论文定义了核心演算，给出效果隔离引理与六个安全性质，并通过Lean 4检查内核模拟、88个测试、3,534个有界检查和32个Lean/Python运行时-内核案例进行验证。

Result: 在标准组合和Sismic状态图实现中复现了测试冷链追踪。在37,440条生成的时序轨迹中与独立工作流匹配并区分了十个单字段变异体。在NOAA IBTrACS自1980年以来的完整子集上与GEOS和事件扫描在1,476,290个过渡区对上一致，包括4,800个抽样和12,831个持续限定的事件。特定项目的GeoSPARQL探针衡量接口覆盖率。

Conclusion: 结果表明PULSE支持契约本地化、安全论证和测试片段的追踪对等性；但语言优越性和可用性不在评估范围内。

Abstract: Knowledge graph engineering often distributes accepted state, observations, constraints, processes, and hypothetical scenarios across artifacts whose combined execution contract remains external. We present PULSE, an Object-Process-Methodology-inspired language that localizes four operational roles and their write effects in one typed runtime. Here, modes denote operational roles rather than modal or deontic logic. The implemented contract fixes evidence non-overwrite, branch isolation, grounded multi-subject timers, guarded state change, and declaration-ranked event ordering over time and space; an external runner still decides whether evidence becomes an authoritative move. GeoSPARQL, SOSA, and SHACL remain generated views. A core calculus gives an effect-confinement lemma and six safety properties. Lean 4 checks kernel analogues for positions, evidence, clocks, monitors, atomicity, and branch source retention; 88 tests, 3,534 bounded checks, and 32 Lean/Python runtime-kernel cases bound the implementation claim to the checked cases. First-author implementations of a standards composition and a separate Sismic statechart reproduce the tested cold-chain trace. Across 37,440 generated temporal traces, PULSE matches a separate workflow and distinguishes ten single-field mutants. On the complete NOAA IBTrACS since1980 subset it agrees with GEOS and an event sweep on 1,476,290 transition-zone pairs, including 4,800 sampled and 12,831 duration-qualified events. Project-specific GeoSPARQL probes measure interface coverage. Overall, the results support contract localization, safety arguments, and trace parity for the tested fragment; language superiority and usability remain outside the evaluation.

</details>


### [218] [HyperAgent: Planning and Acting over Tool-Schema Hypergraphs for Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02650)
*Zian Zhai,Xingyu Tan,Gaowang Zou,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HyperAgent框架，通过构建工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph）来建模工具间的关系，引导LLM智能体进行动态规划与执行，在AppWorld基准上提升了任务完成性能并降低了API调用、LLM交互和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体工具使用规划面临挑战：隐式推理能力有限，且真实世界的执行环境是动态演化的。已有方法通常依赖LLM从文本描述中推断工具组合，在复杂任务中会导致低效探索和不可靠执行。

Method: 1) 在模式（schema）层面建模工具关系，构建有向Tool-Schema Hypergraph，工具表示为从所需输入模式节点到输出模式节点的超边。2) 提出HyperAgent框架：给定任务，先提取任务相关的工具上下文图，用于引导构建模式感知的任务DAG（有向无环图）。3) 执行阶段，通过基于缺陷扩展（deficit-oriented expansion）动态构造状态条件化的工具支持图，识别未解决的需求并根据当前智能体状态检索支持性生产者工具，从而实现每个子任务的动态实现。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的实验表明，HyperAgent相比现有智能体基线方法，提升了任务完成性能，同时减少了冗余的API调用、LLM交互次数和token消耗。

Conclusion: 通过从工具模式层面建模关系并利用超图引导动态规划与执行，可以显著提升LLM智能体在复杂工具使用任务中的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external tools to complete complex real-world tasks. However, reliable tool-use planning remains challenging due to the limitations of implicit reasoning and the evolving nature of real-world execution environments. Existing tool-use agents typically rely on LLMs to infer tool compositions from textual descriptions, which can lead to inefficient exploration and unreliable execution in complex tasks. To address these challenges, we model tool relations at the schema level and construct a directed Tool--Schema Hypergraph, in which tools are represented as hyperedges from their required input-schema nodes to their output-schema nodes. Furthermore, we propose HyperAgent, a Tool--Schema Hypergraph-guided framework for dynamic planning and execution. Given a task, HyperAgent first extracts a task-relevant tool context graph and uses it to guide the construction of a schema-aware Task DAG. During execution, HyperAgent dynamically realizes each subtask by constructing a state-conditioned tool support graph through deficit-oriented expansion, which identifies unresolved requirements and retrieves supporting producer tools according to the current agent state. Experiments on AppWorld demonstrate that HyperAgent improves task completion performance while reducing redundant API calls, LLM interactions, and token consumption compared with existing agent baselines.

</details>


### [219] [Explainable AI for the EU Right to Explanation: A Systematic Review of the Law-XAI Translation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02699)
*Benjamin Fresz,Elena Dubovitskaya,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对欧盟《通用数据保护条例》第15(1)(h)条和《人工智能法案》第86条规定的"解释权"，对可解释人工智能（XAI）进行了系统性文献综述。从2643篇初始记录中筛选出57篇全文，其中仅19篇真正实现了法律与技术视角的跨学科整合。论文识别出三个问题模式：多数文献误认GDPR法律依据、很少涉及CJEU的Dun & Bradstreet判决、混淆解释形式与内容的区别，并提出"受众/目的框架"和四阶段操作化蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟法律赋予受算法影响决策的个人"解释权"，但可解释人工智能（XAI）能否在实践中满足这一权利尚不清楚。作者旨在系统梳理XAI研究如何对接欧盟解释权的法律要求，揭示当前跨学科研究中的关键缺口，并为实现该权利提供可操作路径。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，以2024年7月AI法案最终版发布（第86条后期新增）为时间界限，针对2024年以来的论文进行广泛搜索，从2643条初始记录中筛选出57篇全文进行深入评审，基于法律与技术双重视角评估其整合质量。

Result: 57篇全文中仅19篇（约33%）实现了法律与技术视角的实质性整合。发现三个问题模式：多数文献错误识别GDPR法律依据；鲜有文献涉及CJEU的Dun & Bradstreet判例（可能受发表时间限制）；解释的形式（取决于受众）与内容（取决于法律目的）常被混淆。据此提出"受众/目的框架"，并给出四阶段操作化蓝图和六个开放研究问题。

Conclusion: 当前XAI研究与欧盟解释权法律框架之间的跨学科整合严重不足，解释权面临沦为"形式义务而无技术上可实现合规路径"的风险。需要法律与技术领域的进一步协作，以落实受算法影响个人的解释权。

Abstract: When algorithms make or influence consequential decisions---about loan eligibility, hiring, or healthcare---EU law grants affected individuals a Right to Explanation. Yet whether (and how) Explainable AI (XAI) can satisfy this right in practice remains poorly understood, with direct implications for individuals' ability to contest automated decisions that affect their lives. This paper presents a systematic literature review of XAI in the context of the EU Right to Explanation, with particular focus on Art. 15(1)(h) GDPR, Art. 86 AI Act (AIA), and related instruments. We consider papers published from 2024 onwards, as the final version of the AIA was published in July 2024---with Art. 86 being added late. From 2643 initial records identified by a deliberately broad search, we review 57 full texts, of which only 19 papers demonstrate substantive integration of both legal and technical perspectives, showing gaps in the interdisciplinary synthesis of the current regulatory framework. We document three problematic patterns across the corpus: Most misidentify the GDPR legal basis; few engage with the CJEU's Dun & Bradstreet judgment (likely due to publication timing); and the distinction between explanation form (governed by addressee) and content (governed by legal purpose) is often conflated. We conceptualize this as the Addressee/Purpose Framework, propose a four-phase blueprint for operationalization, and identify six concrete open research questions. Without further progress, the Right to Explanation risks remaining a formal obligation without a technically realizable path to compliance.

</details>


### [220] [Predictive Set Theory: A Generative Framework for Cognitive Architecture with Operationalized Core Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02704)
*Yiyang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出预测集合论（PST）——一个从第一性原理重建认知架构的形式化生成框架，以最小操作集（恒等函数传感器、集合论状态刷新、三种引用链）推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率及有限时域概率规划等核心认知功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测加工理论缺乏对"预测"结构的操作性定义、未标准化预测误差的响应机制，也无法保证连续更新的一致性；贝叶斯认知科学预设封闭假设空间，未能解释概率分配对象如何成为离散可辨识指涉物。两者均存在根本性理论缺口。

Method: PST从最小操作集合出发——将传感器形式化为恒等函数、集合论状态刷新、以及引用/反引用/半引用三种基本引用链——通过形式化推导重建认知体系，包括状态序列、需求、比较、效率及有限时域概率规划等核心认知功能。

Result: 该框架为罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性的认知地位、负反馈的奠基以及电影剪辑的理解等经典问题提供了新颖解法，并构成任何系统在信息不完整且风险不可逆条件下维持内部一致性的设计规范。

Conclusion: 本文的主要目的是通过公开学术记录确立预测集合论框架的原创性与完备性，而非对神经机制进行建模。

Abstract: Predictive processing theories portray the brain as a hierarchical prediction engine that minimizes prediction error, yet they lack operational definitions for the structure of a "prediction," the standardized response to a prediction error, and the mechanism that maintains consistency across successive updates. Bayesian cognitive science attempts to subsume all uncertainty under probabilistic belief updating, but it presupposes a closed hypothesis space and provides no generative account of how the objects over which probabilities are distributed become discrete, identifiable referents in the first place. This paper introduces Predictive Set Theory (PST), a formal generative framework that reconstructs cognitive architecture from first principles. PST anchors cognition in a minimal set of operations---a sensor formalized as an identity function, set-theoretic state refresh, and three fundamental forms of reference chains (reference, counter-reference, and semi-reference)---and rigorously derives core cognitive functions including state sequences, demand, comparison, efficiency, and finite-horizon probabilistic planning. Rather than modeling neural mechanisms, PST constitutes a design specification for any system that must maintain internal consistency while acting under incomplete information and irreversible risk. The framework offers novel resolutions to classical problems such as Russell's paradox, the cognitive status of Gödelian incompleteness, the grounding of negative feedback, and the comprehension of film editing. The primary purpose of this paper is to establish, through the public academic record, the originality and completeness of the Predictive Set Theory framework.

</details>


### [221] [Towards a new paradigm of scientific discovery with socialized artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02775)
*Xinjie Yao,Xingxin Xu,Xiyuan Gao,Zhoupeng Guo,Kunlong Yang,Dengyu Zhao,Siqi Zhao,Zhihe Fan,Yichen Dong,Xin Li,Jiekang Feng,Jiahe Wu,Sen Wang,Beiming Yu,Kejia Zhao,Ruipu Zhao,Jiaqi Zhou,Heyang Li,Jianjun Chen,Anbo Dai,Xin Liu,Zhengtao Yu,Qinghua Hu,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BLAZE（Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation）范式，将AI从孤立研究任务的助手重塑为科学发现的组织化基础设施，通过连接持久知识、集体推理、实证验证与人类判断，构建持续的研究生命周期，实现社会化科学智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现经历了观察实验、理论、计算、数据驱动等多次知识组织方式的变革。当前科学面临的核心挑战不再是单纯产生更多信息，而是如何将不断扩展的知识、推理和证据组织为一个连贯的发现过程。现有AI多作为孤立任务的辅助工具，缺乏将碎片化活动整合为累积性科学探究的基础设施。

Method: BLAZE提出社会化科学智能（Socialized Scientific Intelligence）范式，将AI定位为科学发现的组织基础设施而非孤立任务助手。其核心机制是将知识、假说、实验和集体验证置于持续的研究生命周期中，通过持久知识库、集体推理、实证验证与人类判断的持续互动，将碎片化活动转化为累积性的探究—批判—修正过程。

Result: BLAZE使科学发现更具可追溯性、可重复性和累积性，同时保留人类的创造力、判断力与责任感。它不替代人类发现，而是扩展集体科学探究的规模、深度与连续性。

Conclusion: 科学智能并非仅由计算产生，而是源于知识、假说、实验与集体验证之间的持续互动。社会化科学智能可能为下一代科学奠定基础，将人类与机器组织在共享的科学过程中，实现更深远、更连续的科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific discovery has advanced through successive transformations in the organization of knowledge. Observation and experimentation established the empirical foundations of science. Theory made it possible to derive general principles from particular phenomena. Computation extended inquiry into systems beyond direct observation, while data-intensive methods opened new spaces of pattern and prediction. Science now confronts a different frontier. The central challenge is no longer simply to produce more information, but to organize expanding knowledge, reasoning, and evidence into a coherent process of discovery. Here, we introduce Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation (BLAZE), a paradigm of socialized scientific intelligence. BLAZE conceives AI not as an assistant for isolated research tasks, but as an organizational infrastructure for scientific discovery. It connects persistent knowledge, collective reasoning, empirical validation, and human judgment within a continuous research lifecycle, transforming fragmented activities into a cumulative process of inquiry, criticism, and revision. The central premise of BLAZE is that scientific intelligence does not arise from computation alone. It emerges from the sustained interaction among knowledge, hypotheses, experiments, and collective verification. By organizing humans and machines within a shared scientific process, BLAZE makes discovery more traceable, reproducible, and cumulative while preserving human creativity, judgment, and responsibility. Socialized scientific intelligence may provide a foundation for the next era of science. Its purpose is not to replace human discovery, but to extend the scale, depth, and continuity of collective scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [222] [BAP-SQL: Budget-Aware Observation Planning for Agentic Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02876)
*Chong Peng,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Varun Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BAP-SQL 将观测形成视为预算控制阶段，通过查询风险评估、SQL重写和独立运行时硬限制，提升智能体文本到SQL在紧预算下的成功率，在BIRD基准上增益3.4/3.6个百分点且节省约5% token，但收益随模型能力与预算增加而衰减，在最宽松设置下甚至反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用智能体不只是消费观测，其动作决定后续输入。在智能体文本到SQL场景中，宽泛查询会浪费上下文和数据库计算资源才产生有用证据，而事后压缩无法恢复被省略的行或已消耗的工作量。这促使将观测形成提升为预算控制阶段。

Method: BAP-SQL包含三个环节：(1) 估计查询风险；(2) 必要时重写SQL；(3) 将硬限制委托给独立的运行时防护层（runtime shield）。在一般4B、专用FINER-SQL 4B和7B骨干模型上进行评估。

Result: 在主要BIRD衍生设置上，BAP-SQL相比匹配的SFT基线在紧预算成功率上提升3.4/3.6个百分点，同时减少4.5/5.0%的token使用。匹配重训练和任务级迁移表明增益源于策略可见的规划和预算敏感救助。收益随模型能力和预算增加而衰减，在最宽松设置下逆转，且未减少数据库工作量。

Conclusion: BAP-SQL将观测形成作为预算控制阶段是有效策略，尤其适用于紧预算场景；但其收益具有条件性——在模型能力增强或预算充裕时优势减弱甚至反转，且不能减少底层数据库计算量。

Abstract: Tool-using agents do not merely consume observations: their actions determine what arrives next. In agentic text-to-SQL, a broad query can spend context and database work before useful evidence appears, while post-hoc compression cannot recover omitted rows or expended work. We present BAP-SQL, which treats observation formation as a budget-control stage: it estimates query risk, rewrites SQL when useful, and delegates hard limits to an independent runtime shield. Across general 4B, specialized FINER-SQL 4B, and 7B backbones, BAP-SQL improves tight-budget success. On the primary BIRD-derived setting, it gains 3.4/3.6 percentage points over matched SFT while using 4.5/5.0% fewer tokens. Matched retraining and task-level transfer associate the gain with policy-visible planning and budget-sensitive rescue. The benefit attenuates as model capability and budget increase, reverses at the loosest setting, and does not reduce database work.

</details>


### [223] [VeriTrace: Human-Like Temporal Exploration Completes Agentic Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02878)
*Yu-Tung Liu,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriTrace是一个多智能体系统，通过赋予Inspector智能体完整的调试动作空间（独立控制信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度），实现Agentic Temporal Exploration能力，在VerilogEval-V2基准上首次达到100% Pass@1的完美功能正确率，相较最强基线提升5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多智能体系统在标准Verilog RTL生成基准上停滞于约95%的准确率，作者追溯其根源在于调试动作空间不完整：现有系统限制了智能体可检查的信号、可查询的时间窗口或两者兼有，使调试退化为对狭窄预定电路行为视图的模式匹配，而非假设驱动的根因分析。

Method: 提出VeriTrace多智能体系统，其Inspector智能体拥有完整的调试动作空间，可独立控制信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度。该能力被称为Agentic Temporal Exploration，使智能体能够对故障原因形成假设、查询波形获取证据，并迭代地细化解理解，模拟人类验证工程师的探索式调试过程。

Result: VeriTrace在VerilogEval-V2基准上实现100% Pass@1，成为首个在该基准上达到完美功能正确率的系统。在共享Claude Sonnet 4.0骨干模型上，VeriTrace比最强复现基线高出5.1%。

Conclusion: 调试自主性（debugging agency）是弥合最终准确率差距的关键因素。通过提供完整的调试动作空间来支持假设驱动的根因分析，可以突破多智能体系统在硬件描述语言生成任务上的性能天花板。

Abstract: Large language models have shown promise for automated Verilog RTL generation, yet state-of-the-art multi-agent systems plateau at ~95% accuracy on standard benchmarks. We trace this ceiling to an incomplete debugging action space: existing systems restrict which signals the agent can inspect, which time windows it can query, or both, reducing debugging to pattern matching on a narrow, predetermined view of circuit behavior rather than hypothesis-driven root-cause analysis. We present VeriTrace, a multi-agent system whose Inspector agent operates over a complete debugging action space, with independent control over signal selection, time-window bounds, and iteration depth. This capability, which we term Agentic Temporal Exploration, enables the agent to form hypotheses about failure causes, query the waveform for evidence, and refine its understanding iteratively, mirroring the exploratory process of human verification engineers. VeriTrace achieves 100\% Pass@1 on VerilogEval-V2, the first system to attain perfect functional correctness on this benchmark. On a shared Claude Sonnet 4.0 backbone, VeriTrace outperforms the strongest reproduced baseline by +5.1%, demonstrating that debugging agency closes the final accuracy gap.

</details>


### [224] [Interpreting Black-Box Large Language Models with Sentence-Level Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02879)
*Maryam Rezaee,Pooriya Safaei,Maryam Asgarinezhad,Fatemeh Seyyedsalehi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种模型无关、事后、句子级别的归因解释器，通过训练能量模型（EBM）作为替代模型来捕获LLM内部概念一致性，进而指导轻量级解释器网络的训练，实现对提示句影响力的量化分析，且训练完成后无需再查询LLM API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有大型语言模型（LLMs）仅通过封闭API访问，导致缺乏可解释性，给负责任部署带来关键挑战。

Method: 1) 训练一个Energy-Based Model (EBM)作为替代模型，捕获LLM在提示与响应对之间的内部概念一致性。2) 利用该能量景观指导轻量级解释器网络的训练。3) 解释器作为独立工具运行，训练完成后无需进一步API查询即可量化提示句对目标输出的影响。4) 通过全局训练局部解释器，捕获更广泛的生成模式并缓解实例特定偏差。

Result: 实验表明EBM能准确模拟目标LLM，使解释器能够有效识别出对特定目标输出最具影响力的提示句子。

Conclusion: 该方法为封闭API环境下的LLM提供了一种可行的可解释性解决方案，实现了模型无关的句子级归因分析。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) accessed strictly through closed APIs has created a critical challenge for responsible deployment: a fundamental lack of interpretability. To address this, we propose a model-agnostic, post-hoc attribution interpreter operating at the sentence level. Our approach trains an Energy-Based Model (EBM) as a surrogate to capture the LLM's internal conceptual consistency between prompts and responses. This energy landscape guides the training of a lightweight interpreter network. Uniquely, our interpreter operates as a standalone tool; once trained, it quantifies the influence of prompt sentences on a user-specified target output without requiring further API queries to the LLM. By globally training a local interpreter across diverse inputs, our framework captures broader generation patterns and mitigates instance-specific biases. Experiments demonstrate that our EBM accurately simulates the target LLM, allowing the interpreter to effectively identify the prompt sentences most influential in generating specific target outputs.

</details>


### [225] [Hypercubes, Hyperplanes, and Constraint-Induced Complexity Collapse in Atomic Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02930)
*Irene Tsapara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过超立方体与超平面的几何视角重新审视高元原子概念学习，揭示了地面实例空间中复杂性并非均匀分布，而是集中定位在特定结构（如全对角线）上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到高维超立方体的逻辑结构并非均匀：大多数超平面可被有限个初等等价类折叠（与项深度无关），而全对角线例外且类计数无界增长。这种不对称性反映了概念本身的归约结构，但现有框架未能充分解释这一现象。

Method: 基于作者前期的高维框架，通过规范简单概念、最小序和代表性归约重新诠释结果；给出了完整的二元情形、三元超立方体的显式处理，并详细展开了驱动折叠的归约机制。

Result: 建立了高维情形下超平面行为的分类体系，表明复杂性是局部化的而非均匀分布的；三维情形已展现出正交族、部分对角线与例外全对角线的本质现象。

Conclusion: 几何-逻辑视角明确了原子概念学习中复杂性的集中位置，并建议以约束假设空间和结构化分类进行现代诠释。

Abstract: We revisit higher-arity atomic concept learning through the geometry of hypercubes and hyperplanes of ground instances. Our starting point is the observation that the ambient r-dimensional hypercube of ground atoms is not structurally uniform. Its logical complexity is organized by hyperplanes: every hyperplane other than the full diagonal collapses into finitely many elementary-equivalence classes, with a bound independent of the term depth, while the full diagonal is exceptional and its class count grows without bound. This asymmetry is not merely geometric. It reflects the reduction-theoretic structure of the concepts themselves. Building on a higher-dimensional framework developed in the author's earlier work, we reinterpret these results through canonical simple concepts, minimal orderings, and representative reductions. This yields a taxonomy of hyperplane behavior in higher dimensions and shows that complexity is localized rather than spread uniformly through the instance space. The paper includes a fully worked binary case, an explicit treatment of the ternary hypercube, and an unpacked account of the reduction machinery that drives the collapse. The three-dimensional case already exhibits the essential phenomenon of orthogonal families, partial diagonals, and the exceptional full diagonal. This geometric-logical perspective clarifies where complexity is concentrated in atomic concept learning and suggests a modern interpretation in terms of constrained hypothesis spaces and structured classification.

</details>


### [226] [When Compression Scores Cannot Decide: Information Boundaries for Group-Robust LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02940)
*Andrew Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个可复现的压缩统计量仍可能选出错误候选的现象：一个分割半信度为0.906的密集剪枝分数预测16.1%收益，但其选出的端点在两个对照组上分别差6.0%和7.7%。作者通过"信息接口"框架建模这一差距，推导出等权组情形下的锥形定律用于正线性固定候选损伤的精确汇合代价，并通过三种双世界构造和精确观测纤维半径刻画未解析的差异来源。实验显示群解析对角矩阵恢复粗略损伤顺序（Spearman 0.9239），粗粒度深度分配相对均匀分配将三个密集LLM的最差群困惑度膨胀降低12.6–20.9%，模型特定完整掩码端点选择进一步改善2.7–8.0%。在OLMoE中，路由追踪预测单例方向（114/192优于最强重标定的81/192），单层有限菜单决策使留出集最差群KL降低13.7%和7.2%。作者主张：局部测量仅能构造候选，选择需由完整候选端点或经验证的均匀保证授权，且不确定性需针对每个比较进行校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在解决机器学习压缩/剪枝中的一个关键悖论：即使统计量具有高度可复现性（高分割半信度），其选出的候选模型仍可能显著劣于基线。作者试图从信息论角度系统化解释这一"可复现但错误"的现象，并建立严格的数学框架来量化统计量能够区分的"信息接口"边界，从而为模型压缩中的端点选择提供理论基础和实操准则。

Method: 作者提出"信息接口"（information interfaces）概念，界定每个统计量所能区分的区分集。数学上，推导了等权群组场景下正线性固定候选损伤的精确汇合代价的锥形定律，涵盖对角与全PSD二阶矩情形。通过三个"双世界构造"（two-world constructions）和"精确观测纤维半径"（observation-fiber radius）来表征汇合矩、群局部矩及参考路径曲率分别无法解析的差异。实验方面，在三个密集LLM上对比均匀块分配与粗粒度深度分配，测算最差群困惑度膨胀；在OLMoE上用路由器迹预测单例方向，并通过单层有限菜单决策验证留出集最差群KL降幅。

Result: 群解析对角矩阵可恢复粗略损伤顺序（Spearman系数0.9239）但细粒度顺序仍弱；粗粒度深度分配相比均衡均匀分配将三个密集LLM的最差群困惑度膨胀降低12.6–20.9%；模型特定完整掩码端点选择优于参照2.7–8.0%；OLMoE中路由器迹预测单例方向的准确率114/192，显著优于最强重标定下的81/192；单层有限菜单决策使留出最差群KL降低13.7%和7.2%。

Conclusion: 论文的结论是：局部测量（如路由追踪、局部矩统计）只能用于构建候选集，而最终的选择必须由完整的候选端点或经过验证的均匀性保证来授权。任何局部统计量都存在信息接口局限性——即使统计量本身高度可复现，也无法规避其所支持的区分集边界。不确定性必须针对每一个具体比较进行校准，不能依赖单一全局可信度。

Abstract: A reproducible compression statistic can still select the wrong candidate. A dense pruning score with 0.906 split-half reliability predicted a 16.1% gain. Its selected endpoint was 6.0% and 7.7% worse than two controls. We model the gap through information interfaces that delimit which distinctions each statistic supports. For equal-weight groups, a conic law gives the exact pooling price for positive linear fixed-candidate damage, including diagonal and full PSD second moments. Three two-world constructions and an exact observation-fiber radius characterize what pooled moments, group-local moments, and reference-path curvature leave unresolved. A group-resolved diagonal recovers broad damage order (Spearman 0.9239) while fine order remains weak. Relative to balanced uniform allocation, a coarse depth allocation cuts worst-group perplexity inflation by 12.6--20.9% across three dense LLMs. Model-specific complete-mask endpoint selection improves over those references by 2.7--8.0%. In OLMoE, router traces predict singleton direction (114/192 versus 81/192 under the strongest relabeling). Finite-menu decisions on one layer yield held-out worst-group KL reductions of 13.7% and 7.2%. Local measurements construct candidates. Selection is licensed by complete candidate endpoints or a validated uniform guarantee, with uncertainty calibrated to every comparison.

</details>


### [227] [On the missing data layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02949)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DataHub，一个面向拉丁美洲AI数据层的基础设施，通过任务优先的任务/领域/语言三层本体组织，解决数据集发现和供应两个复合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺少AI基础设施的两大基础层：数据集层和基准层。数据集层面临两个复合问题：数据集分散在多个平台缺乏统一索引（发现困难），即使完美索引，总量仍远低于前沿AI开发所需（供应不足）。

Method: 提出DataHub，一种任务优先的数据基础设施，通过本体结构 /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?/ 组织数据，包含数据集发现、元数据、贡献、许可和复用机制。

Result: 本文聚焦数据集层，提出了DateHub这一基础设施的设计方案，具体实验结果未在本摘要中呈现。

Conclusion: DataHub通过任务优先的组织方式和配套机制，为拉丁美洲AI数据集层的发现与供应问题提供了系统性的基础设施解决方案。

Abstract: Latin America is missing two foundational layers of AI infrastructure: the dataset layer and the benchmark layer. This paper targets the dataset layer. The dataset layer faces two compounding problems: discovery and supply. Latin American AI datasets exist but are scattered across platforms with no shared index. Even with perfect indexing, the total volume would remain far below what frontier AI development requires. We propose DataHub: a task-first data infrastructure organized through the ontology /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>, with mechanisms for dataset discovery, metadata, contribution, licensing, and reuse.

</details>


### [228] [Neurosymbolic Reasoning with Incremental Knowledge for Sample Efficient Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02993)
*Subrat Prasad Panda,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号分层强化学习（HRL）框架InK，利用增量知识（InK）替代固定知识编码，通过符号高层规划（如D*算法）在可更新的知识表示上进行规划，配合低层目标条件神经网络模块进行奖励塑形学习运动基元，显著提升稀疏奖励长时域导航任务中的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分层强化学习（HRL）将知识以固定、不可更新的形式（如架构选择）编码，无法在探索过程中利用增量知识进行推理，导致在稀疏奖励、长时域推理环境下样本效率低下。

Method: 提出InK框架：高层采用符号规划（如D*）在可更新的增量知识表示上进行推理；低层采用目标条件神经网络模块，通过奖励塑形从经验中学习运动基元；并针对已有先验知识下的最优符号规划开发了Belief World Tree Search（BWTS）算法。

Result: 在导航任务上的实验表明，融入增量知识（InK）显著提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 神经符号混合的增量知识HRL方法相比固定HRL能有效提升样本效率，展示了将符号规划与神经学习结合的可行性。

Abstract: (Flat) Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents face significant challenges in environments with sparse rewards that require long-horizon reasoning. A compelling approach to improve sample efficiency is to incorporate knowledge into learning and decision-making. In standard Hierarchical RL (HRL), knowledge is encoded in a fixed, non-updatable form, such as architectural choices, and remains unchanged throughout learning. With fixed HRL, reasoning with incremental knowledge learned during exploration is impractical before sufficient environmental knowledge is acquired, leading to poor sample efficiency. In this work, we propose neurosymbolic HRL with {\em Incremental Knowledge (InK)}: symbolic high-level components perform {\em symbolic planning} (e.g. using $D^*$) on an updatable representation of current InK, while low-level goal-conditioned neural modules learn motion primitives through experience using reward shaping. Experiments on navigation tasks demonstrate that incorporating InK substantially improves sample efficiency. Additionally, to perform {\em optimal} symbolic planning given {\em prior} knowledge about the world, we develop Belief World Tree Search. The code is available at https://github.com/CPS-research-group/ink_bwts.

</details>


### [229] [On the missing benchmarks layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02996)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出拉丁美洲缺乏AI基准层，建议建立开放的评估基础设施EvalsHub（首个区域实例为LatamBoard），使公共机构能审计外国AI系统、企业能优化本地AI解决方案，并支持大学、公共机构、专业社区和企业发布、执行、比较和维护各类评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺少用于本土AI开发的基础基准层。该层具有双重功能：一是根据区域社会需求审计AI系统，二是在经济相关环境中指导AI优化。缺少该层导致公共机构无法独立评估外国AI系统，企业也无法以最先进性能优化AI以解决本地问题，造成可审计性和优化方向的双重损失。

Method: 构建EvalsHub，一个开放、任务优先的基准基础设施，以LatamBoard作为首个区域实例。该平台允许大学、公共机构、专业社区和公司发布、执行、比较和维护跨模型、工作流和代理的评估。设计为一次性构建、持续度量，机构可在新AI系统发布时重新运行，行业团队在每次系统更改后重新运行，且开放设计和激励驱动。

Result: 论文提出概念性平台方案，尚未展示实验结果；其"结果"主要体现在基础设施设计本身，即通过开放、可重复运行的基准架构来解决审计和优化方向缺失的问题。

Conclusion: 通过建立EvalsHub/LatamBoard这一开放、任务优先的基准基础设施，可以为拉丁美洲提供AI开发的关键基础层，实现AI系统的可审计性与本地化优化方向的兼顾，从而支撑区域AI生态系统的可持续发展。

Abstract: Latin America is missing a foundational layer for native AI development: the benchmark layer. The benchmark layer does two things no other layer can - it audits AI systems against regional social requirements and it directs AI optimization in economically relevant environments. Without it, public institutions cannot independently evaluate foreign AI systems, and companies cannot optimize AI systems to solve local problems with SOTA performance. The cost of the missing layer is dual: a loss of auditability and a loss of optimization direction over a technology that is increasingly critical infrastructure. We propose an EvalsHub, with LatamBoard as its first regional instance - an open, task-first benchmark infrastructure where universities, public institutions, professional communities, and companies can publish, execute, compare, and maintain evaluations across models, workflows, and agents. Built once, measured forever - re-run by institutions as new AI systems ship and by industry teams after every system change. Open by design and incentive-driven by construction.

</details>


### [230] [ProPRL: Property-Aware Prerequisite Relation Learning in Educational Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03006)
*Xinghe Cheng,Jiapu Wang,Chaobo He,Ruihai Dong,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProPRL是一个属性感知的前置关系学习框架，通过概念-资源超图和有向学习行为图学习互补的概念表示，利用成对条件门控自适应融合两个视图，并引入不可逆约束防止矛盾的反向预测，在多个真实教育数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前置关系学习方法通常将其建模为传统的链接预测任务，存在两个局限：(1) 无法针对每个候选概念对自适应地整合互补的教育证据；(2) 容易产生矛盾的反向预测（即同一概念对两个方向同时被高置信度预测）。

Method: ProPRL包含三个核心组件：(1) 从概念-资源超图和有向学习行为图中学习互补的概念表示，其中方向保留的个性化传播聚合多跳行为证据；(2) 成对条件门控（Pair-conditioned Gate）为每个候选有序概念对自适应地加权融合两个视图；(3) 不可逆约束（Irreversibility Constraint）引入反对称正则化项，惩罚同一概念对在两个方向同时高置信度的预测。

Result: 在多个真实世界教育数据集上的实验表明，ProPRL在前置关系学习任务上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过自适应证据融合和反对称约束，ProPRL有效解决了现有方法在自适应证据整合和矛盾反向预测方面的不足，验证了属性感知前置关系学习的有效性。

Abstract: Prerequisite relation learning is central to adaptive instruction, yet existing methods often formulate it as conventional link prediction, limiting their ability to adaptively integrate complementary educational evidence for individual candidate pairs and to discourage contradictory reverse predictions. We propose ProPRL, a Property-aware Prerequisite Relation Learning framework. ProPRL first learns complementary concept representations from a concept-resource hypergraph and a directed learning-behavior graph, where direction-preserving personalized propagation aggregates multi-hop behavioral evidence. It then employs a Pair-conditioned Gate to adaptively weight and fuse the two views for each candidate ordered concept pair. Finally, an \textit{Irreversibility Constraint} introduces an anti-symmetry regularizer that penalizes simultaneously high confidence in both directions of the same concept pair. Experiments on multiple real-world educational datasets show that ProPRL achieves state-of-the-art performance on prerequisite relation learning.

</details>


### [231] [UrbanAgent: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Cross-System Urban Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03018)
*Jiayu Cao,Xingyuan Zeng,feiyu Li,Zhijing Huang,Xujie Yuan,Rongxiang Chen,Shimin Di,Libin Zheng,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Urban-Agent，一个面向跨系统城市任务的大语言模型智能体框架，结合工具集（代码执行、API调用、MCP协议）在一个自适应闭环中完成意图澄清、工具落地和结果对齐。同时引入Urban-Eval基准，同时评估任务结果和执行质量（工具覆盖率、依赖有效性、证据可追溯性）。实验表明Urban-Agent达到71%任务成功率，比最强基线高10个百分点，且在多种LLM后端上均保持领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代城市依赖日益增多的数字服务运作，但服务碎片化、互操作性差，用户操作负担重。现有数字平台、城市基础模型和智能助手只解决城市任务的孤立方面，难以可靠地将复杂的自然语言请求转换为可执行的跨系统工作流，亟需一个统一的智能体框架来解决这一缺口。

Method: Urban-Agent采用工具增强的智能体框架，将大语言模型的认知与推理能力与支持代码执行、API调用和模型上下文协议（MCP）的工具集耦合。通过一个自适应闭环，在行动前澄清缺失信息，基于实时观测进行工具落地，并将最终响应与观测证据及任务约束对齐。此外，构建Urban-Eval基准，专门评估跨系统城市请求的任务结果和执行质量（包括工具覆盖率、依赖有效性和证据可追溯性）。

Result: Urban-Agent在Urban-Eval基准上达到71%的任务成功率，比最强基线高出10个百分点。该优势在GPT-5-mini、Gemini-2.5-flash、DeepSeek-V4-flash和Qwen3-235B-A22B等多个大语言模型后端上均保持一致。

Conclusion: Urban-Agent为跨系统城市任务提供了一种有效的智能体方案，通过自适应闭环机制显著提升了复杂自然语言请求到可执行工作流的转化可靠性。Urban-Eval基准弥补了现有评估体系对任务结果和执行质量双重评估的缺失，为城市智能体领域提供了更全面的评测标准。

Abstract: Modern cities rely on an increasing number of digital services to operate, but residents' daily needs are still difficult to meet. Services are fragmented and have little interoperability, placing a heavy operational burden on users. Existing digital platforms, urban foundation models, and intelligent assistants each address only isolated aspects of an urban task. But they struggle to reliably convert complex natural-language requests into executable cross-system workflows. We propose Urban-Agent, a tool-augmented agent framework for cross-system urban tasks. It couples the cognitive and reasoning capabilities of a large language model with a tool-set supporting code execution, API calls, and Model Context Protocol. Through one adaptive closed loop, it clarifies missing information before acting, grounds tool use in live observations, and aligns the final response with observed evidence and task constraints. To address the evaluation gap, we introduce Urban-Eval, a benchmark specifically designed for cross-system urban request. Unlike prior benchmarks that assess either general tool use or urban knowledge and reasoning, Urban-Eval evaluates both task results and execution quality, including required tool coverage, dependency validity, and evidence traceability. Experimental results indicate that Urban-Agent reaches a 71% task success rate, 10 points above the strongest baseline. This lead holds across GPT-5-mini, Gemini-2.5-flash, DeepSeek-V4-flash, and Qwen3-235B-A22B.

</details>


### [232] [LoCA: Forward-Only LLM Tuning after One-Shot Calibration with Local Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03020)
*Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Zhaofeng Zhang,Yihao Wang,Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Local Credit Assignment (LoCA)，一种两阶段的参数高效后训练方法，通过一次校准的前向传播替代每次适配都需要经过冻结主干网络的端到端反向传播。LoCA在每个Transformer块拟合从最终预测误差到局部隐藏状态修正的低秩映射，再利用这些映射从前向激活构造逐块回归目标，用闭式岭回归求解低秩适配器，无需额外的骨干反向传播。在Qwen2.5（0.5B到14B）的五个判别性基准上，25个任务-规模组合中16个取得低于LoRA的评估交叉熵，峰值GPU显存降低26-29%，校准后CPU稳态内存降低36-52%，单次前向时间降低43-48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效后训练虽减少了可训练参数数量，但仍需反复进行贯穿冻结主干的端到端反向传播，每次适配都必须依赖支持反向传播的硬件，并且需要存储或重新计算激活值。作者质疑是否能用一次性校准替代这一反复的反向传播链，以实现前向-only的后训练。

Method: LoCA采用两阶段方法：(1) 校准阶段——用一次探测性反向传播在每个Transformer块拟合从最终预测误差到局部隐藏状态修正的低秩映射；(2) 适配阶段——复用这些映射从前向激活构造逐块的回归目标，然后用闭式岭回归求解低秩适配器。整个过程不再需要经过骨干的反向传播。

Result: 在Qwen2.5系列（0.5B至14B）的五个判别性基准上，25个任务-规模组合中16个LoCA取得比LoRA更低的评估交叉熵。完整运行的GPU峰值（含校准）比LoRA低26-29%；校准后CPU稳态内存低36-52%，逐次前向时间低43-48%。候选集可跨Qwen2.5各尺寸及SmolLM2-1.7B复用。

Conclusion: LoCA将全局信用分配摊销为一次性校准，实现了仅前向的后期调优，在重复反向传播不可行时（如无反向硬件或内存受限场景）具有实用价值，代码已开源。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient post-training reduces the number of trainable parameters, but still requires repeated end-to-end backpropagation through the frozen backbone. Every adaptation step therefore needs backward-capable hardware and must store or recompute activations. We ask whether this repeated backward chain can be replaced by a one-time calibration. We introduce Local Credit Assignment (LoCA), a two-stage method for small-shift adaptation. One probe backward pass fits a low-rank map at each transformer block from the final prediction error to a local hidden-state correction. LoCA then reuses these maps to form blockwise regression targets from forward activations and fits low-rank adapters with closed-form ridge solves. No further backbone backward pass is required. We evaluate LoCA on five discriminative benchmarks with Qwen2.5 models from 0.5B to 14B. In 16 of 25 reported task--scale comparisons, LoCA yields lower evaluation cross-entropy than the corresponding LoRA run. Its measured full-run GPU peak, including calibration, is 26--29\% lower than LoRA's. After calibration, its CPU steady-state memory is 36--52\% lower and its per-pass time is 43--48\% lower. A shared scale-normalized candidate set is reused across all tested Qwen2.5 sizes and on SmolLM2-1.7B. LoCA thus amortizes global credit assignment into one calibration and enables later forward-only tuning when repeated backpropagation is impractical. The code associated with this paper is available \href{https://github.com/Xia12121/LoCA}{here}.

</details>


### [233] [DiffImaginE: Imagine to Verify Entity Types with Diffusio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03025)
*Feng Zhang,Feiyu Han,Rongxin Yang,Yang Liu,Yancheng Chen,Rui Wang,Yingguang Yang,Tian Xueyun,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Xu Kefu,Congjing Ran,Fuhai Chen,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffImaginE，将多模态命名实体识别（MNER）的类型验证任务建模为条件潜变量扩散推断问题，通过类型条件去噪器预测加入标准化潜变量的噪声，利用去噪误差作为类型条件负对数似然的ELBO一致代理，实现对竞争类型假设的排序，在Twitter-2015和Twitter-2017数据集上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的imagine-and-compare验证器将每个（span, type）对映射到单一预测视觉特征，将多样的视觉实现压缩为单一原型，且提供的兼容性分数缺乏明确的概率语义，无法很好地处理视觉证据的多模态多样性。

Method: DiffImaginE将MNER类型验证公式化为条件潜变量扩散推断：给定span局部视觉证据，类型条件去噪器预测注入到标准化潜变量中的噪声，去噪误差作为类型条件负对数似然的ELBO一致代理。采用分类器无关引导的扩散评分器（classifier-free-guided diffusion scorer），使用Min-SNR加权训练，直接监督每个类型的扩散分数作为分类logits，学习跨噪声水平的聚合，并使用对偶采样（antithetic sampling）减少蒙特卡洛比较方差。

Result: 在Twitter-2015和Twitter-2017数据集上的实验表明，与匹配的确定性ImaginE基线（相同编码器、辅助目标和评估协议）相比，DiffImaginE取得一致的性能提升，并通过消融实验和配对显著性检验得到支持。分析还表明分类器无关引导锐化了诱导的类型后验，并刻画了对偶配对在等去噪器成本下何时能降低方差。

Conclusion: DiffImaginE通过引入条件潜变量扩散推断来替代确定性验证器，提供了具有概率语义的类型验证分数，且在实验验证中优于匹配的确定性基线，为MNER任务中的视觉证据建模提供了新的扩散模型视角。

Abstract: Multimodal named entity recognition (MNER) determines whether each candidate span and entity-type hypothesis is supported by joint textual and visual evidence. Existing imagine-and-compare verifiers map each (span, type) pair to one predicted visual feature, compressing diverse visual realisations into a single prototype and providing a compatibility score without explicit probabilistic semantics. We introduce DiffImaginE, which formulates MNER type verification as conditional latent diffusion inference. Given span-localised visual evidence, a type-conditioned denoiser predicts noise injected into its standardised latent. The resulting denoising error provides an ELBO-consistent surrogate for type-conditional negative log-likelihood, allowing competing type hypotheses to be ranked by how well they explain the observation. DiffImaginE retains a standard multimodal encoder stack and replaces the deterministic verifier with a classifier-free-guided diffusion scorer trained using Min-SNR weighting. We directly supervise per-type diffusion scores as classification logits, learn aggregation across noise levels, and use antithetic sampling to reduce Monte Carlo comparison variance. Our analysis shows that classifier-free guidance sharpens the induced type posterior and characterises when antithetic pairing reduces variance at equal denoiser cost. Experiments on Twitter-2015 and Twitter-2017 show consistent gains over a matched deterministic ImaginE control under the same encoder, auxiliary objectives, and evaluation protocol, supported by ablations and paired significance tests.

</details>


### [234] [Evaluating Counterfactual Sensitivity to Patient Information in Medication-Safety Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03028)
*Zhitian Hou,Yuhang Liu,Pengkai Wang,Zeyu Liu,Guanghao Zhu,Zheng Liu,Shuo Cai,Congkai Xie,Zhijie Sang,Kun Zeng,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedPIC-Bench基准，通过对照反事实问题测试LLM在患者特定条件下应用药物安全规则的能力，发现所有模型在反事实问题上表现显著下降（63.6%降至45.1%），即使识别到患者信息变化仍保留原有安全判断，揭示了静态药物安全评估的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的药物安全评估多使用孤立、固定的场景，模型可能仅通过回忆药物-风险关联就能答对，而无需证明其真正利用患者信息来判断规则是否适用。当患者特定条件不满足时应用安全规则可能导致错误决策，因此需要一种能区分"记忆关联"与"条件推理"的评估方法。

Method: 构建MedPIC-Bench基准，包含基于指南的问题与配对的对照反事实问题（通过受控改变患者信息改变规则适用性），共467个问题，沿六个临床和推理维度进行专家标注。对28个医学专用、通用和专有LLM进行系统性评估。

Result: 所有模型在反事实问题上表现更差，平均准确率从63.6%降至45.1%。模型在显式患者属性直接信号熟悉的禁忌证时表现良好，但在患者信息需收窄或撤销安全警告时表现挣扎。模型推理常承认患者信息变化，但最终答案仍保留原有安全判断。医学专用LLM的反事实表现反而落后于通用LLM。

Conclusion: MedPIC-Bench使条件规则应用变得可测量，并强调了用静态药物安全准确率评估患者特定可靠性的局限性。模型存在系统性的条件推理缺陷，医学专用模型的脆弱性尤为突出。

Abstract: Applying a valid medication-safety rule when its patient-specific conditions are not met can produce an incorrect decision. Existing medical evaluations largely use isolated and fixed scenarios. A model may therefore answer correctly by recalling a drug-risk association without showing that it used patient information to decide whether the rule applies. To address this gap, we introduce MedPIC-Bench, a benchmark of source-verifiable recommendations and expert-validated questions for patient-specific medication-safety reasoning. It combines guideline-following questions with paired counterfactual questions in which a controlled change in patient information changes whether a rule applies. The benchmark contains 467 questions annotated along six clinical and reasoning dimensions. Across 28 medical-specific, general, and proprietary LLMs, every model performs worse on counterfactual questions, with mean accuracy falling from 63.6\% to 45.1\%. Models perform well when an explicit patient attribute directly signals a familiar contraindication, but struggle when patient information must narrow or withdraw a safety warning. Model rationales often acknowledge the changed patient information, yet the final answers retain the previous safety judgment. This vulnerability persists among medical-specific LLMs, whose average CF performance trails that of general LLMs. MedPIC-Bench therefore makes conditional rule application measurable and highlights the limitations of static medication-safety accuracy for assessing patient-specific reliability.

</details>


### [235] [CastFSR: A Fast--Slow--Reflect Agentic Reasoning Framework for Context-Aware Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03031)
*Xiaoyu Tao,Mingyue Cheng,Bokai Pan,Chuang Jiang,Huanjian Zhang,Tian Gao,Yaguo Liu,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CastFSR，一个将上下文感知时间序列预测建模为"快速-慢速-反思"工作流的智能体框架，通过快速思考构建数据驱动预测先验、慢速推理检索上下文证据并推理影响、反思阶段迭代优化预测以确保一致性，同时支持免训练推理和两阶段SFT+强化学习的紧凑模型部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时间序列预测方法缺乏显式机制来识别相关上下文、推理其影响，并验证预测是否符合时间和领域约束，导致预测的上下文感知能力不足。

Method: 提出CastFSR智能体框架，将上下文感知预测组织为三阶段工作流：1) 快速思考阶段，刻画观测数据并选择轻量预测器构建数据驱动预测先验；2) 慢速推理阶段，检索上下文证据、自适应确定信息量充分的历史回看窗口，并推理上下文如何重塑未来动态；3) 反思阶段，迭代细化预测以确保时间、上下文和领域一致性。支持无训练直接使用现成LLM推理，也支持通过两阶段SFT和强化学习将编排能力迁移到紧凑LLM上实现高效部署。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验表明，CastFSR在性能上持续优于代表性基线方法，代码已开源。

Conclusion: CastFSR通过显式的快速-慢速-反思工作流，增强了LLM在时间序列预测中的上下文感知与推理能力，兼顾了推理效果与部署效率，为智能体化预测提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is fundamental to decision-making in complex systems, where future dynamics are influenced not only by historical observations but also by evolving contextual features. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have extended forecasting beyond numerical extrapolation toward context-aware reasoning. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to identify relevant contexts, reason about their impacts, and validate forecasts against temporal and domain constraints. In this work, we propose CastFSR, an agentic framework that formulates context-aware forecasting as a Fast--Slow--Reflect workflow. In fast thinking, CastFSR profiles observations and selects lightweight forecasters to construct a data-driven forecast prior. In slow deliberation, it retrieves contextual evidence, adaptively determines informative look-back windows, and reasons about how contexts reshape future dynamics. In reflection, it iteratively refines forecasts to ensure temporal, contextual, and domain consistency. CastFSR supports both training-free inference with off-the-shelf LLMs and efficient deployment through a two-stage SFT and reinforcement learning strategy that transfers its orchestration capability to compact LLMs. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate that CastFSR consistently outperforms representative baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xiaoyu-Tao/CastFSR.

</details>


### [236] [Getting the Parameters Right: A Difficulty-Graded Benchmark and Probe-Guided Training for LLM Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03071)
*Guoyao Yu,Xiaoqing Sun,Ziqi Huang,Shaojing Fan,Zhongyi Zhang,Xiaomeng Hu,Xiaobo Xue,Yangyang Shi,Xiong Xiao,Yang Song,Biao Lyu,Rong Wen,Xing Li,Qinming He,Shunming Zhu,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在生成工具调用参数值时，其隐藏状态包含强正确性信号，可通过线性探针准确预测参数值是否正确。基于此提出探针引导的统一框架，包含PBT（探针过滤的自举训练）和PGR（探针引导的重排序）两种互补方法，并发布基于真实云网络API的基准ParamBench。在5个开源模型和6个外部基准上的实验显示，该方法将平均精确匹配率从19.7%提升至59.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用研究主要聚焦于工具选择和调用顺序编排，但正确填写工具调用参数同样关键却研究不足。在云网络等领域，即使前沿模型的工具调用正确率也不到一半，亟需提升参数生成质量的方法。
